enum AK_MODE
  {
   AK_DEMO_ONLY = 0,        // Demo only (real account blocked)
   AK_DEMO_AND_LIVE = 1     // Demo and real account
  };
enum AK_LOTMODE
  {
   AK_LOT_FIXED = 0,        // Fixed lot (per symbol)
   AK_LOT_RISK = 1          // Lot from risk % of the balance
  };
enum AK_MEMMODE
  {
   MEM_AUTO = 0,            // AUTO: real memory trades from MemoryMinBalance, shadow (virtual) below it
   MEM_ON = 1,              // ON: real memory trades at any balance
   MEM_SHADOW = 2,          // SHADOW: virtual trades only (learning, no orders)
   MEM_OFF = 3              // OFF: memory method switched off
  };
enum AK_PTSMODE
  {
   PTS_OFF = 0,             // OFF: points shown only
   PTS_TIGHTEN = 1,         // TIGHTEN: stop moves behind the last H1 candle
   PTS_EXIT = 2             // EXIT: close the trade right away
  };
enum AK_GOLDMODE
  {
   GM_NORMAL = 0,           // NORMAL: full lot
   GM_SMALL = 1,            // SMALL: half the lot (never below the minimum lot)
   GM_OFF = 2               // OFF: no new trades of this method on gold
  };
enum AK_HISTMODE
  {
   HIST_OFF = 0,            // OFF
   HIST_SHOW = 1,           // SHOW: panel + cards show the past (no effect on trades)
   HIST_FOLLOW = 2          // FOLLOW: skip a setup whose situation lost in the past
  };

#define AK_NAME   "AK Version 10.4"
#define AK_VER    "10.40"
#define AK_OBJ    "AK10_"
#define SC_GOLD    0
#define SC_OIL     1
#define SC_BTC     2
#define SC_ETH     3
#define SC_FX      4
#define CR_NONE    0
#define CR_SL      1
#define CR_TRAIL   2
#define CR_NOLOSS  3
#define CR_TIME    4
#define CR_WEEKEND 5
#define CR_NEWS    6
#define CR_MANUAL  7
#define CR_TP      8
#define CR_TREND   9
#define CR_POINTS  10
#define CR_H4FLIP  11
#define CR_TSTOP   13
#define NK_OTHER   0
#define NK_FED     1
#define NK_ECB     2
#define NK_OIL     3
#define NK_INFL    4
#define NK_JOBS    5
#define NK_CLAIMS  6
#define NK_GROWTH  7
#define NK_SPEECH  8
#define M_PB       0                      // methods (each has its own magic number)
#define M_BO       1
#define M_MEM      2
#define M_TR       3                      // method 4: Trend H4 / H1
#define LY_H1      60                     // layers: Pullback H1 / M15 / M5, Breakout H1 / M15, Memory
#define LY_M15     15
#define LY_M5      5
#define LY_H1X     61
#define LY_M15X    16
#define LY_MEM     90
#define LY_T1      62                     // Trend method on H1 / H4
#define LY_T4      64
#define EX_TREND   1                      // exit plans
#define EX_QUICK   2
#define EX_MEM     3                      // memory trade (its learnt exit is stored per trade)
#define EX_TX      4                      // Trend method: half at 1R, stop to entry, trailing, 96-candle limit (as tested)
#define MNB       700                    // candles loaded for the memory indicators (EMA200 / ADX settle long before)
#define MPB       400                    // candles scanned for W / M patterns
#define MSHN      40                     // shadow (virtual) trades tracked at once
#define MEX_TP1   0                      // memory exits: target 1 / 2 / 3 ATR (stop 1 ATR) or TREND RIDE
#define MEX_TP2   1
#define MEX_TP3   2
#define MEX_TREND 3
#define FM_TPB     1                      // setup families
#define FM_TPB50   2
#define FM_MOM     3
#define FM_BO20    4
#define HS_N       26                     // history setups (Pullback H1, M15 EMA20, M15 EMA50, M5; Breakout H1 x3, M15 x2; Trend x13)
#define TX_N       20                     // Trend method entries (18-20 = RANGE entries, only in a sideways market)
#define MAXPOS     60
#define NEWS_ALERT_MIN 15                 // Telegram news alert this many minutes before the release
//--- fixed values (kept out of the input list so the inputs stay short and clear)
#define TradeComment       "AK10"
#define TouchBars          3              // the pull-back must touch the EMA within the last 3 candles
#define StructBars         5              // stop behind the lowest low / highest high of the last 5 candles
#define StopBufferATR      0.2            // ... plus 0.2 ATR
#define MinStopATR         1.0            // ... and at least 1 ATR of the setup timeframe
#define FridayCloseHour    22
#define AbnormalSpreadMult 2.5
#define TierCardFrom       2              // admin progress cards from +2R upwards
#define NewsFeedURL        "https://nfs.faireconomy.media/ff_calendar_thisweek.json"
#define NewsFeedHours      4
#define UseEIAFilter       UseNewsFilter
#define EIABeforeMin       NewsBeforeMin
#define EIAAfterMin        NewsAfterMin
#define RolloverBeforeMin  10
#define RolloverAfterMin   20

//==================================================================
// INPUTS - every filter can be switched ON / OFF
//==================================================================
input string      S1                 = "======== 1. ACCOUNT ========";
input AK_MODE     AccountMode        = AK_DEMO_ONLY;   // Account mode (a real account also needs LiveConfirm)
input bool        LiveConfirm        = false;          // Real account: set true to allow real-money trading
input int         MagicBase          = 80000;          // Magic base: Pullback +0, Breakout +1000, Memory +2000 (+ symbol code)

input string      S2                 = "======== 2. SYMBOLS & LOTS ========";
input bool        TradeGold          = true;   // Trade XAUUSD (gold)
input bool        TradeBTC           = true;   // Trade BTCUSD
input bool        TradeETH           = true;   // Trade ETHUSD
input bool        TradeEUR           = false;  // Trade EURUSD (full test: only the Trend entries made money - OFF by default)
input bool        TradeOil           = true;   // Trade USOIL / WTI (Trend method entries tested on oil)
input bool        V10MethodsOnOil    = false;  // Pullback / Breakout of v10 on oil too (never positive in tests)
input bool        V10MethodsOnEUR    = true;   // Pullback / Breakout of v10 on EURUSD (tests: negative; Trend method entries are separate)
input bool        AllowOtherSymbols  = false;  // Trade other symbols too (never tested)
input AK_LOTMODE  LotMode            = AK_LOT_FIXED;   // Lot mode
input double      LotGold            = 0.02;   // Gold lot per trade (0.02 = 0.01 booked at the first target + 0.01 runs; v10.4 test lot)
input double      LotBTC             = 0.02;   // BTC lot per trade
input double      LotETH             = 0.20;   // ETH lot per trade (ETH moves fewer dollars, so a bigger lot)
input double      LotEUR             = 0.02;   // EURUSD (and other forex) lot per trade
input double      RiskPercent        = 0.5;    // Risk mode: risk per trade in % of the balance (never below the minimum lot)
input double      BTCRiskPct         = 0.5;    // BTC: lot by risk % of the balance even in fixed-lot mode (0 = use LotBTC). Full 6-year test: fixed lot nearly wiped the account in 2021
input AK_GOLDMODE GoldPullbackMode   = GM_NORMAL;   // Gold: Pullback method (2006-2020 test: weak) - NORMAL / SMALL lot / OFF
input AK_GOLDMODE GoldMemoryMode     = GM_NORMAL;   // Gold: Memory method - NORMAL / SMALL lot / OFF (Breakout always runs with the full lot)

input string      S3                 = "======== 3. METHOD 1 - PULLBACK ========";
input bool        UsePullback        = true;   // Pullback method ON (own magic, own trades)
input bool        PullbackH1         = true;   // ... H1: pull-back to EMA20 + strong close
input bool        PullbackM15        = true;   // ... M15: pull-back to EMA20 / EMA50 + strong close
input bool        PullbackM5         = true;   // ... M5: pull-back to EMA20 + strong close (smallest stop - spread matters most)
input bool        PullbackNeedH1     = true;   // M15 / M5 pull-backs also need the H1 trend to agree (M5 also M15)
input int         MaxOpenPullback    = 2;      // Max open Pullback trades on this symbol

input string      S4                 = "======== 4. METHOD 2 - BREAKOUT ========";
input bool        UseBreakout        = true;   // Breakout method ON (own magic, own trades)
input bool        BreakoutH1         = true;   // ... H1: 20-candle breakout, momentum candle, deep pull-back to EMA50
input bool        BreakoutM15        = true;   // ... M15: 20-candle breakout, momentum candle (H1 + H4 must agree)
input bool        BreakoutStrongClose = true;  // The breakout candle must close strong (in the top / bottom third of its range)
input int         MaxOpenBreakout    = 2;      // Max open Breakout trades on this symbol

input string      S5                 = "======== 5. METHOD 3 - MEMORY (learnt M15 / H1 setups, 5 symbols, all data) ========";
input AK_MEMMODE  MemoryMode         = MEM_ON;     // Memory mode: ON = real trades (~30 learnt setups), SHADOW = virtual only (safer: on unseen years memory lost)
input double      MemoryMinBalance   = 5000;   // AUTO: real memory trades from this balance (shadow below)
input double      MemoryLot          = 0.02;   // Memory trade lot (fixed lot mode)
input double      MemoryLotETH       = 0.20;   // Memory trade lot on ETH (fixed lot mode; ETH moves fewer dollars)
input double      MemoryRiskPct      = 0.10;   // Risk lot mode: memory risk in % of balance x setup strength (0.5x-1.5x)
input int         MemoryMaxOpen      = 2;      // Max open memory trades on this symbol (one per setup)
input int         MemoryPauseAfter   = 10;     // Live learning: judge a setup after this many live + shadow trades ...
input double      MemoryPauseR       = 0.0;    // ... and pause it when its average result is this R or worse (walk-forward best: 0)
input bool        MemoryCards        = true;   // Telegram cards for memory trades (H1 to VIP, M15 only if VipLowTFCards)
input bool        MemoryOnGold       = true;   // Memory setups learnt for gold (all data 2006-2026)
input bool        MemoryOnBTC        = true;   // ... BTC (2015-2026)
input bool        MemoryOnETH        = true;   // ... ETH (2020-2026)
input bool        MemoryOnEUR        = true;   // ... EURUSD (2005-2026)
input bool        MemoryOnOil        = true;   // ... USOIL (2005-2022, 2026)

input string      S5B                = "======== 5B. METHOD 4 - TREND H4 / H1 (new in 10.1) ========";
input bool        UseTrendMethod     = true;   // Trend method ON (own magic, own trades, own risk budget)
input bool        TrendOnGold        = true;   // Trend method on gold (test: +3,053 USD vs v10 alone from 1,000)
input bool        TrendOnBTC         = true;   // Trend method on BTC (v10.3 test with the step-lock exit: +5,626 vs +2,648 USD without it)
input bool        TrendOnETH         = true;   // Trend method on ETH (test: +112 USD)
input bool        TrendOnEUR         = true;   // Trend method on EURUSD (Ichimoku H4, Inside-day breakout H4)
input bool        TrendOnOil         = true;   // Trend method on USOIL (EMA20 pullback H4, Pin bar H4, EMA20 pullback + ADX H4)  // Trend method on EURUSD (no entry tested positive)
input bool        TrendTestedSets    = true;   // Each symbol uses only the entries that tested positive on it (false = all 13 on every symbol)
input string      TrendAddGold       = "";     // Extra Trend entries for gold (codes, e.g. "13,9"): 1 HA H4, 2 EMA50 pb H4, 3 EMA20 pb H4, 4 MACD H4, 5 momentum H4, 6 Supertrend H1, 7 BB squeeze H1, 8 Supertrend H4, 9 EMA cross H4, 10 MACD H1, 11 55-bar H1, 12 MACD zero H4, 13 RSI50 H4, 14 pin bar H4
input string      TrendDropGold      = "3";     // Trend entries to switch off on gold (codes)
input string      TrendAddBTC        = "";     // ... BTC extra
input string      TrendDropBTC       = "10";     // ... BTC off
input string      TrendAddETH        = "14";     // ... ETH extra
input string      TrendDropETH       = "";     // ... ETH off
input string      TrendAddOil        = "";     // ... USOIL extra
input string      TrendDropOil       = "";     // ... USOIL off
input string      SkipSetups         = "ETH:Breakout H1 20-candle";     // Pullback / Breakout setups to switch off per symbol, e.g. "ETH:Pullback M5;ETH:Breakout H1 20-candle"
input bool        TX_HeikinAshiH4    = true;   // Heikin-Ashi colour change H4 (gold)
input bool        TX_EMA50PullbackH4 = true;   // EMA50 deep pullback H4 (gold, BTC)
input bool        TX_EMA20PullbackH4 = true;   // EMA20 pullback H4 (gold)
input bool        TX_MACDCrossH4     = true;   // MACD signal cross H4 (gold)
input bool        TX_MomentumH4      = true;   // Momentum candle H4 (gold, BTC)
input bool        TX_SupertrendH1    = true;   // Supertrend flip H1 (gold)
input bool        TX_BollingerSqueezeH1 = true; // Bollinger squeeze breakout H1 (gold)
input bool        TX_SupertrendH4    = true;   // Supertrend flip H4 (BTC)
input bool        TX_EMACrossH4      = true;   // EMA20/50 cross H4 (BTC, ETH)
input bool        TX_MACDCrossH1     = true;   // MACD signal cross H1 (BTC)
input bool        TX_Breakout55H1    = true;   // 55-candle breakout H1 (BTC)
input bool        TX_MACDZeroH4      = true;   // MACD zero cross H4 (ETH)
input bool        TX_RSI50H4         = true;   // RSI cross 50 H4 (ETH)
input bool        TX_PinBarH4        = true;   // Pin bar in trend H4 (oil)
input bool        TX_EMA20ADXH4      = true;   // EMA20 pullback + ADX>25 H4 (oil)
input bool        TX_IchimokuH4      = true;   // Ichimoku TK cross above / below the cloud H4 (EURUSD)
input bool        TX_InsideDayH4     = true;   // Inside-day breakout H4 (EURUSD)
input bool        UseRangeEntries    = true;   // RANGE entries: trade only when the H4 trend is weak (sideways / choppy market)
input bool        TX_RangeFadeH4     = true;   // Failed-breakout fade H4 (ETH)
input bool        TX_RangeBandH4     = true;   // Bollinger band re-entry H4 (ETH)
input bool        TX_RangeBandH1     = false;  // Bollinger band re-entry H1 (gold) - OFF: lost in the account test
input double      RangeMaxADX_ETH    = 20;     // ETH: range entries only while the H4 ADX is below this
input double      RangeMaxADX_Gold   = 15;     // Gold: range entries only while the H4 ADX is below this
input int         MaxOpenTrend       = 2;      // Max open Trend trades on this symbol
input double      TrendRiskPct       = 4;      // Trend method's own risk budget: open Trend risk not yet risk-free, % of balance (0 = off)
input double      TrendLotH4Gold     = 0.02;   // Lot for H4 Trend entries: gold (H4 stops are wide; H1 entries use the symbol lot)
input double      TrendLotH4BTC      = 0.01;   // ... BTC
input double      TrendLotH4ETH      = 0.20;   // ... ETH
input double      TrendLotH4EUR      = 0.01;   // ... EURUSD / forex
input double      TrendLotH4Oil      = 0.02;   // ... USOIL

input string      S6                 = "======== 6. TRADE LIMITS & RISK (ON / OFF) ========";
input int         MaxTradesPerSymbol = 7;      // Max open trades on this symbol (all 4 methods together)
input int         MaxOpenAccount     = 16;     // Max AK v10 trades open on the whole account
input bool        StackOnlyAfterBE   = true;   // A method adds a 2nd trade on the same timeframe only when the 1st is risk-free
input double      MaxRiskPerTradePct = 6;      // Skip a trade whose stop would lose more than this % of the balance (0 = off)
input double      MaxOpenRiskPct     = 10;     // Risk limit of Pullback + Breakout + Memory: open risk not yet risk-free, % of balance (0 = off)
input double      MinMarginLevel     = 200;    // No new trade if the margin level would fall below this % (0 = off)
input bool        UseDailyLossLimit  = false;  // Daily loss limit ON: no new trades today after a closed loss of ...
input double      DailyLossLimitUSD  = 50;     // ... this many USD (all AK v10 charts)
input bool        UseMaxDDStop       = false;  // Max drawdown stop ON: no new trades while equity is ...
input double      MaxDDStopPct       = 20;     // ... this % below its peak

input string      S6B                = "======== 6B. CHOP GUARD & LOSS BRAKE (smaller lot, never a bigger one) ========";
input bool        ChopGuardGold      = true;  // Choppy / sideways market: smaller lot on gold (no trade is skipped)
input bool        ChopGuardBTC       = true;  // ... BTC
input bool        ChopGuardETH       = true;  // ... ETH
input bool        ChopGuardOil       = false;  // ... USOIL
input bool        ChopGuardEUR       = false;  // ... EURUSD / forex
input double      ChopLotFactor      = 0.5;    // Lot x this while the market is choppy (0.5 = half)
input double      ChopMaxEff         = 0.19;   // Choppy = the last 24 H1 candles moved less straight than this (0-1: net move / total path) ...
input int         ChopMinCross       = 4;      // ... and price crossed the H1 EMA20 at least this many times in them
input bool        ChopFullLotAligned = false;   // Full lot anyway when D1, H4 and H1 all agree with the trade (strong trade)
input int         ChopReleaseBars    = 6;      // Back to the full lot at once when an H1 candle broke out of the 24-candle range in the last N candles
input bool        LossBrakeGold      = false;  // Losing streak brake on gold: smaller lot after N losses in a row, until a win
input bool        LossBrakeBTC       = false;  // ... BTC
input bool        LossBrakeETH       = false;  // ... ETH
input bool        LossBrakeOil       = true;  // ... USOIL
input bool        LossBrakeEUR       = false;  // ... EURUSD / forex
input int         LossBrakeAfter     = 3;      // ... after this many losses in a row on the symbol
input double      LossBrakeFactor    = 0.5;    // ... lot x this
input bool        LossBrakeSkipChop  = false;  // Skip new trades while BOTH the market is choppy and the losing streak is on (strict)

input string      S7                 = "======== 7. EXIT ========";
input int         ExitStyle          = -1;     // Exit for Pullback / Breakout / Trend: -1 = BEST PER SYMBOL (gold v10, BTC / ETH step lock), 0 = v10 (half at 1R, trail), 1 = STEP LOCK, 2 = full target 3R
input bool        ExitBestPerSymbol  = true;   // ExitStyle -1: use the exit numbers that tested best per symbol in v10.4 (gold trail from 4R, 2.5R behind; BTC / ETH full 3R target; ETH / oil stop to entry at 1.5R)
input int         TimeStopCandles    = 0;      // Time stop: close if the trade has not reached TimeStopR within this many candles (0 = off)
input double      TimeStopR          = 0.3;    // ... best profit needed (R)
input double      GoldMaxSpreadPct   = 6;      // Gold filter: skip when the spread is more than this % of the stop (0 = off)
input bool        GoldNeedH4         = true;   // Gold filter: the H4 trend (EMA20 / 50 and close) must agree
input bool        CryptoNoWild       = true;   // BTC / ETH filter: no new trades in a wild (top 40% volatility) market
input bool        UsePartial         = true;   // Book part of the lot at the first target (needs 2x the minimum lot, e.g. 0.02)
input double      PartialAtR         = 1.0;    // First target in risk multiples (1.0 = 1:1)
input double      PartialPct         = 50;     // ... % of the lot booked there (0.02 -> 0.01)
input double      NoLossAtR          = 1.0;    // Stop to entry at this profit in risk multiples (with a partial: right after it)

double Op(int tf, int i) { return iOpen(_Symbol, tf, i); }
double Hi(int tf, int i) { return iHigh(_Symbol, tf, i); }

double ATR(int tf, int i) { return iATR(_Symbol, tf, 14, i); }

string Px(double p) { return DoubleToString(p, Digits); }
string Usd(double v) { return ((v >= 0) ? "+" : "") + DoubleToString(v, 2); }
string Rtx(double r) { return ((r >= 0) ? "+" : "") + DoubleToString(r, 2) + "R"; }
double GVGet(string n, double def) { if(GlobalVariableCheck(n)) return GlobalVariableGet(n); return def; }
void   GVSet(string n, double v) { GlobalVariableSet(n, v); }
string TrimStr(string s) { return StringTrimLeft(StringTrimRight(s)); }
string SideName(int dir) { return (dir > 0) ? "BUY" : "SELL"; }

//--- position keys: open time x 4 + method (the open time survives a partial close, the method comes from the magic)
long     MakeKey(datetime ot, int m) { return (long)ot * 4 + m; }
datetime KeyTime(long key) { return (datetime)(key / 4); }
int      KeyMethod(long key) { return (int)(key % 4); }

//--- per-position memory, keyed by the position key
string PName(long key, string f) { return g_G + "P" + IntegerToString(key) + "_" + f; }
double PGet(long key, string f, double def) { return GVGet(PName(key, f), def); }
void   PSet(long key, string f, double v) { GlobalVariableSet(PName(key, f), v); }
void   PClear(long key) { GlobalVariablesDeleteAll(g_G + "P" + IntegerToString(key) + "_"); }

string Emo(int cp)
  {
   if(cp < 0x10000) return ShortToString((ushort)cp);
   int v = cp - 0x10000;
   ushort hi = (ushort)(0xD800 + (v >> 10));
   ushort lo = (ushort)(0xDC00 + (v & 0x3FF));
   return ShortToString(hi) + ShortToString(lo);
  }

string ShortHash(string s)
  {
   uint h1 = 5381;
   uint h2 = 2166136261;
   int n = StringLen(s);
   for(int i = 0; i < n; i++)
     {
      uint c = (uint)StringGetCharacter(s, i);
      h1 = ((h1 << 5) + h1) + c;
      h2 = (h2 ^ c) * (uint)16777619;
     }
   return StringFormat("%08X%08X", h1, h2);
  }

int LiveOffsetNow()
  {
   double d = (double)(TimeCurrent() - TimeGMT());
   return (int)(MathRound(d / 1800.0) * 1800.0);
  }

//--- broker time - GMT in seconds (tester: winter offset + 1 hour in US summer time; live: measured on fresh ticks)
int GmtOffsetSec()
  {
   if(IsTesting())
     {
      datetime hr = (datetime)(((long)TimeCurrent() / 3600) * 3600);
      if(hr != g_offHour)
        {
         g_offHour = hr;
         int w = TesterGMTWinter * 3600;
         g_offCache = w + ((TesterUSDST && IsUSDST(TimeCurrent() - w)) ? 3600 : 0);
        }
      return g_offCache;
     }
   if(g_gmtOk) return g_gmtOff;
   return LiveOffsetNow();
  }

//--- every live tick: the quotes are fresh, so the broker offset can be measured (kept over the weekend)
void LearnGmtOffset()
  {
   if(IsTesting()) return;
   int o = LiveOffsetNow();
   if(o >= -14 * 3600 && o <= 14 * 3600)
     {
      if(!g_gmtOk || o != g_gmtOff) GlobalVariableSet("AK10_GMTOFF", (double)o);
      g_gmtOff = o;
      g_gmtOk = true;
     }
  }
datetime ToIST(datetime server) { return server - GmtOffsetSec() + 19800; }
datetime NowIST() { return ToIST(TimeCurrent()); }
string   IstClock(datetime server) { return TimeToString(ToIST(server), TIME_MINUTES); }
string   IstStamp(datetime server) { return TimeToString(ToIST(server), TIME_DATE | TIME_MINUTES); }
string   DateKey() { datetime t = NowIST(); return StringFormat("%04d%02d%02d", TimeYear(t), TimeMonth(t), TimeDay(t)); }

int ClassifySymbol(string s)
  {
   string u = s;
   StringToUpper(u);
   if(StringFind(u, "XAU") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "GOLD") >= 0) return SC_GOLD;
   if(StringFind(u, "BTC") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "XBT") >= 0) return SC_BTC;
   if(StringFind(u, "ETH") >= 0) return SC_ETH;
   if(StringFind(u, "OIL") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "WTI") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "XTI") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "BRENT") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "CL-") == 0) return SC_OIL;
   return SC_FX;
  }

string ClassName(int c)
  {
   if(c == SC_GOLD) return "GOLD";
   if(c == SC_OIL) return "OIL";
   if(c == SC_BTC) return "BTC";
   if(c == SC_ETH) return "ETH";
   return "FX";
  }

bool IsCrypto() { return (g_cls == SC_BTC || g_cls == SC_ETH); }
bool IsEURUSD() { string u = _Symbol; StringToUpper(u); return (StringFind(u, "EURUSD") >= 0); }

//--- is this symbol switched on? (gold / BTC / ETH / EURUSD by their own switch, the rest only with AllowOtherSymbols)
bool SymbolSupported()
  {
   if(g_cls == SC_GOLD) return TradeGold;
   if(g_cls == SC_BTC) return TradeBTC;
   if(g_cls == SC_ETH) return TradeETH;
   if(g_cls == SC_FX && IsEURUSD()) return TradeEUR;
   if(g_cls == SC_OIL) return TradeOil;
   return AllowOtherSymbols;
  }
//--- may the v10 Pullback / Breakout methods trade this symbol?
bool V10Here()
  {
   if(g_cls == SC_OIL) return V10MethodsOnOil;
   if(g_cls == SC_FX && IsEURUSD()) return V10MethodsOnEUR;
   return true;
  }
string SymbolOffText()
  {
   if(g_cls == SC_GOLD) return "gold is switched off (TradeGold = false)";
   if(g_cls == SC_BTC) return "BTC is switched off (TradeBTC = false)";
   if(g_cls == SC_ETH) return "ETH is switched off (TradeETH = false)";
   if(g_cls == SC_FX && IsEURUSD()) return "EURUSD is switched off (TradeEUR = false)";
   return "only XAUUSD / BTCUSD / ETHUSD / EURUSD are traded (AllowOtherSymbols = true trades it anyway, never tested)";
  }

//--- methods
string MethodName(int m) { return (m == M_TR) ? "Trend" : ((m == M_MEM) ? "Memory" : ((m == M_BO) ? "Breakout" : "Pullback")); }
string MethodTag(int m) { return (m == M_TR) ? "TR" : ((m == M_MEM) ? "MEM" : ((m == M_BO) ? "BO" : "PB")); }
int    MethodOfLayer(int ly)
  {
   if(ly == LY_MEM) return M_MEM;
   if(ly == LY_T1 || ly == LY_T4) return M_TR;
   if(ly == LY_H1X || ly == LY_M15X) return M_BO;
   return M_PB;
  }
bool MethodOn(int m)
  {
   if(m == M_PB) return UsePullback;
   if(m == M_BO) return UseBreakout;
   if(m == M_TR) return UseTrendMethod;
   return (MemoryMode != MEM_OFF);
  }
int MethodCap(int m)
  {
   int c = (m == M_PB) ? MaxOpenPullback : ((m == M_BO) ? MaxOpenBreakout : ((m == M_TR) ? MaxOpenTrend : MemoryMaxOpen));
   return (c < 0) ? 0 : c;
  }

//--- magic numbers: MagicBase + method x 1000 + symbol code (0-996)
int SymbolCode()
  {
   int h = 0;
   int n = StringLen(_Symbol);
   for(int i = 0; i < n; i++) h = (h * 31 + StringGetCharacter(_Symbol, i)) % 997;
   return h;
  }
int  MethodMagic(int m) { return MagicBase + m * 1000 + g_h; }
bool IsAKMagic(int mg) { return (mg >= MagicBase && mg < MagicBase + 4000 && (mg - MagicBase) % 1000 < 997); }
int  MethodOfMagic(int mg) { return (mg - MagicBase) / 1000; }
bool IsMyMagic(int mg) { return (IsAKMagic(mg) && (mg - MagicBase) % 1000 == g_h); }
//--- the selected order is an AK v10 market order of this chart
bool MyOrder() { return (OrderSymbol() == _Symbol && OrderType() <= OP_SELL && IsMyMagic(OrderMagicNumber())); }
long OrderKey() { return MakeKey(OrderOpenTime(), MethodOfMagic(OrderMagicNumber())); }

int LotStepDigits(double step)
  {
   if(step >= 1.0) return 0;
   if(step >= 0.1) return 1;
   if(step >= 0.01) return 2;
   return 3;
  }

double MinLot()
  {
   double step = MarketInfo(_Symbol, MODE_LOTSTEP), minL = MarketInfo(_Symbol, MODE_MINLOT);
   if(step <= 0) step = 0.01;
   if(minL <= 0) minL = step;
   return minL;
  }

double NormLot(double lots)
  {
   double minL = MarketInfo(_Symbol, MODE_MINLOT), maxL = MarketInfo(_Symbol, MODE_MAXLOT), step = MarketInfo(_Symbol, MODE_LOTSTEP);
   if(step <= 0) step = 0.01;
   if(minL <= 0) minL = step;
   if(maxL <= 0) maxL = 100;
   lots = MathFloor(lots / step + 1e-9) * step;
   if(lots < minL) lots = minL;
   if(lots > maxL) lots = maxL;
   return NormalizeDouble(lots, LotStepDigits(step));
  }

//--- money per 1.0 price move for a lot size
double ValuePerPrice(double lots)
  {
   double tv = MarketInfo(_Symbol, MODE_TICKVALUE), ts = MarketInfo(_Symbol, MODE_TICKSIZE);
   if(tv <= 0 || ts <= 0) return 0;
   return tv / ts * lots;
  }

//--- the fixed lot of this symbol
double SymbolLot()
  {
   if(g_cls == SC_GOLD) return LotGold;
   if(g_cls == SC_BTC) return LotBTC;
   if(g_cls == SC_ETH) return LotETH;
   return LotEUR;
  }

//--- lot for a new Pullback / Breakout trade with stop distance S (price)
bool RiskLotsHere() { return (LotMode == AK_LOT_RISK || (g_cls == SC_BTC && BTCRiskPct > 0)); }
double RiskPctHere() { return (LotMode == AK_LOT_RISK) ? RiskPercent : BTCRiskPct; }

double TradeLots(double S)
  {
   if(RiskLotsHere() && RiskPctHere() > 0 && S > 0)
     {
      double v1 = ValuePerPrice(1.0);
      double bal = AccountBalance();
      if(v1 > 0 && bal > 0) return NormLot(bal * RiskPctHere() / 100.0 / (S * v1));
     }
   return NormLot(SymbolLot());
  }

//--- the part booked at the first target (0 = no partial possible: the lot is below 2x the minimum lot)
double PartialLots(double lots)
  {
   if(!UsePartial || PartialPct <= 0) return 0;
   double minL = MinLot(), step = MarketInfo(_Symbol, MODE_LOTSTEP);
   if(step <= 0) step = 0.01;
   if(lots < 2 * minL - 1e-9) return 0;
   double p = MathFloor(lots * PartialPct / 100.0 / step + 1e-9) * step;
   if(p < minL) p = minL;
   if(lots - p < minL - 1e-9) p = lots - minL;
   if(p < minL - 1e-9) return 0;
   return NormalizeDouble(p, LotStepDigits(step));
  }

double StopLevelPx() { return MarketInfo(_Symbol, MODE_STOPLEVEL) * Point; }

string TfName(int tf)
  {
   if(tf == PERIOD_M1) return "M1";
   if(tf == PERIOD_M5) return "M5";
   if(tf == PERIOD_M15) return "M15";
   if(tf == PERIOD_H1) return "H1";
   if(tf == PERIOD_H4) return "H4";
   if(tf == PERIOD_D1) return "D1";
   return "";
  }

//--- progress ladder by the best profit reached (R): T1 +1R, T2 +2R, T3 +4R, T4 +6R, T5 +8R, T6 +12R
int TierOfR(double r)
  {
   if(r >= 12.0) return 6;
   if(r >= 8.0) return 5;
   if(r >= 6.0) return 4;
   if(r >= 4.0) return 3;
   if(r >= 2.0) return 2;
   if(r >= 1.0) return 1;
   return 0;
  }

double TierR(int t)
  {
   if(t <= 0) return 0.0;
   if(t == 1) return 1.0;
   if(t == 2) return 2.0;
   if(t == 3) return 4.0;
   if(t == 4) return 6.0;
   if(t == 5) return 8.0;
   return 12.0;
  }

string LayerName(int ly)
  {
   if(ly == LY_MEM) return "Memory";
   if(ly == LY_T1) return "Trend H1";
   if(ly == LY_T4) return "Trend H4";
   if(ly == LY_M5) return "Pullback M5";
   if(ly == LY_M15) return "Pullback M15";
   if(ly == LY_H1X) return "Breakout H1";
   if(ly == LY_M15X) return "Breakout M15";
   return "Pullback H1";
  }
string LayerTag(int ly)
  {
   if(ly == LY_MEM) return "MEM";
   if(ly == LY_T1) return "TX1";
   if(ly == LY_T4) return "TX4";
   if(ly == LY_M5) return "PB5";
   if(ly == LY_M15) return "PB15";
   if(ly == LY_H1X) return "BO60";
   if(ly == LY_M15X) return "BO15";
   return "PB60";
  }
int LayerTf(int ly)
  {
   if(ly == LY_M5) return PERIOD_M5;
   if(ly == LY_T4) return PERIOD_H4;
   if(ly == LY_M15 || ly == LY_M15X) return PERIOD_M15;
   return PERIOD_H1;
  }
bool LowTfLayer(int ly) { return (ly == LY_M5 || ly == LY_M15 || ly == LY_M15X); }
string PlanName(int ex) { return (ex == EX_TX) ? "TREND H4/H1 EXIT" : ((ex == EX_MEM) ? "MEMORY EXIT" : ((ex == EX_QUICK) ? "QUICK 2R" : "TREND RIDE")); }

string FamName(int fm)
  {
   if(fm > 100) return TxName(fm - 100);
   if(fm == FM_TPB50) return "pull-back to EMA50";
   if(fm == FM_MOM) return "momentum candle";
   if(fm == FM_BO20) return "20-candle breakout";
   return "pull-back to EMA20";
  }

string FamCode(int fm)
  {
   if(fm > 100) return "X" + IntegerToString(fm - 100);
   if(fm == FM_TPB50) return "TPB50";
   if(fm == FM_MOM) return "MOM";
   if(fm == FM_BO20) return "BO20";
   return "TPB";
  }

string SetupName(int ly, int fm)
  {
   if(ly == LY_MEM) return "Memory: " + ((fm >= 0 && fm < MEM_STRATS) ? g_msName[fm] : "setup");
   if(fm > 100) return TxName(fm - 100);
   return TfName(LayerTf(ly)) + " " + FamName(fm);
  }

//--- does the timeframe agree with the trade? (EMA20 on the right side of EMA50 and the close beyond EMA20)
bool TfAgrees(int tf, int dir)
  {
   if(iBars(_Symbol, tf) < 60) return false;
   double e20 = EMAv(tf, 20, 1), e50 = EMAv(tf, 50, 1), c = Cl(tf, 1);
   if(e20 <= 0 || e50 <= 0) return false;
   if(dir > 0) return (e20 > e50 && c > e20);
   return (e20 < e50 && c < e20);
  }

//--- trend side of D1 / H4 (close above / below EMA50): +1 up, -1 down, 0 unknown
int TrendOf(int tf)
  {
   if(iBars(_Symbol, tf) < 60) return 0;
   double c = Cl(tf, 1), e = EMAv(tf, 50, 1);
   if(e <= 0 || c <= 0) return 0;
   return (c > e) ? 1 : -1;
  }

//--- the side the trend filter allows: +1 buys, -1 sells, 0 none (D1 and H4 disagree)
int TrendSide()
  {
   int d1 = TrendOf(PERIOD_D1), h4 = TrendOf(PERIOD_H4);
   return (d1 == h4) ? d1 : 0;
  }

string TrendWord(int t) { return (t > 0) ? "UP" : ((t < 0) ? "DOWN" : "-"); }

//==================================================================
// MARKET MODE (regime engine) - all from completed D1 candles, so it never repaints
//==================================================================
//--- D1 volatility rank: share of the last 250 completed D1 candles whose ATR(14) / close was at or below the latest one
//    (0 = calmest of the year, 1 = wildest); -1 = not enough history (then treated as normal)
double VolRank()
  {
   static datetime bar = 0;
   static double cache = -1;
   datetime b = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_D1, 0);
   if(b == bar && b != 0) return cache;
   bar = b;
   cache = -1;
   int n = iBars(_Symbol, PERIOD_D1);
   int avail = n - 16;
   if(avail > 250) avail = 250;
   if(avail < 60) return cache;
   double c1 = Cl(PERIOD_D1, 1), a1 = ATR(PERIOD_D1, 1);
   if(c1 <= 0 || a1 <= 0) return cache;
   double cur = a1 / c1;
   int le = 0, tot = 0;
   for(int s = 1; s <= avail; s++)
     {
      double c = Cl(PERIOD_D1, s), a = ATR(PERIOD_D1, s);
      if(c <= 0 || a <= 0) continue;
      tot++;
      if(a / c <= cur) le++;
     }
   if(tot >= 60) cache = (double)le / tot;
   return cache;
  }

//--- volatility band: 0 calm, 1 normal, 2 wild (not enough history = normal)
int VolBand()
  {
   double r = VolRank();
   if(r < 0) return 1;
   if(r <= CalmVolRank) return 0;
   if(r > WildVolRank) return 2;
   return 1;
  }
string BandName(int b)
  {
   if(b == 0) return "CALM";
   if(b == 2) return "WILD";
   return "NORMAL";
  }

//--- is a trade in direction dir on the side of the big trend? (D1 close vs D1 EMA200)
bool WithBigTrend(int dir)
  {
   double e = EMAv(PERIOD_D1, 200, 1), c = Cl(PERIOD_D1, 1);
   if(e <= 0 || c <= 0) return true;
   return (dir > 0) ? (c > e) : (c < e);
  }

//--- weekend for crypto (Saturday / Sunday in UTC, as tested)
bool CryptoWeekend()
  {
   if(!IsCrypto()) return false;
   int dow = TimeDayOfWeek(NowUTC());
   return (dow == 0 || dow == 6);
  }

//--- THE MAP: may layer ly trade in direction dir now, and with which exit? why = reason when not
bool MarketPlan(int dir, int ly, int &ex, string &why)
  {
   ex = EX_TREND;
   why = "";
   if(!UseMarketMode) return true;
   int band = VolBand();
   bool withT = WithBigTrend(dir);
   if(CryptoWeekend() && !(band == 1 && withT)) { why = "weekend: crypto trades only in a normal market with the big trend"; return false; }
   if(band == 2)
     {
      if(!withT) { why = "wild market against the D1 EMA200 - no method worked there"; return false; }
      if(LowTfLayer(ly)) { why = "wild market - M5 / M15 entries paused (H1 trades use QUICK 2R)"; return false; }
      ex = EX_QUICK;
      return true;
     }
   if(!withT && !(band == 0 && IsCrypto())) { why = "against the D1 EMA200 (wrong side of the big trend) - no method worked there"; return false; }
   return true;
  }

//--- one line for the panel and the admin cards
string MarketModeText()
  {
   double r = VolRank();
   int band = VolBand();
   int ts = TrendSide();
   string v = "volatility " + BandName(band) + ((r >= 0) ? " (rank " + DoubleToString(r, 2) + ")" : " (rank n/a)");
   if(!UseMarketMode) return v + " | market mode off - every setup uses TREND RIDE";
   if(ts == 0) return v + " | D1 and H4 disagree - waiting";
   bool withT = WithBigTrend(ts);
   string side = SideName(ts) + ((withT) ? " with the D1 EMA200" : " against the D1 EMA200");
   string plan = "";
   if(CryptoWeekend() && !(band == 1 && withT)) plan = "weekend pause (crypto)";
   else if(band == 2) plan = withT ? "QUICK 2R on H1, M5 / M15 paused" : "no trade (wild + against the big trend)";
   else if(!withT && !(band == 0 && IsCrypto())) plan = "no trade (against the big trend)";
   else plan = "TREND RIDE on M5 / M15 / H1";
   return v + " | " + side + " -> " + plan;
  }


//==================================================================
// TIME ZONES, NEWS CALENDAR, EIA, ROLLOVER (safety filters)
//==================================================================
#define MAXNW    260
#define NW_USD   1
#define NW_EUR   2
#define NW_OIL   4
#define NW_CACHE "AK10_news_cache.json"
#define MAXEIA   20

datetime g_nwT[MAXNW];              // event time (UTC)
int      g_nwMask[MAXNW];           // which markets it moves (USD / EUR / OIL)
int      g_nwBef[MAXNW];            // minutes blocked before
int      g_nwAft[MAXNW];            // minutes blocked after
string   g_nwName[MAXNW];
string   g_nwNote[MAXNW];           // forecast / previous from the live calendar
int      g_nwN = 0;
int      g_nwBase = 0;              // events below this index are built-in / manual; the rest come from the live calendar
datetime g_feedT = 0;               // local time of the last calendar load
string   g_feedState = "off";
bool     g_feedUrlErr = false;
datetime g_eiaSkip[MAXEIA];         // normal Wednesday (New York date) that was moved
datetime g_eiaMove[MAXEIA];         // the moved release (UTC)
int      g_eiaN = 0;

datetime MakeDT(int y, int mo, int d, int hh, int mi)
  {
   return StringToTime(StringFormat("%04d.%02d.%02d %02d:%02d", y, mo, d, hh, mi));
  }

int DaysInMonth(int y, int mo)
  {
   if(mo == 2) return (((y % 4 == 0) && (y % 100 != 0)) || (y % 400 == 0)) ? 29 : 28;
   if(mo == 4 || mo == 6 || mo == 9 || mo == 11) return 30;
   return 31;
  }

//--- day of the month of the nth Sunday (nth = 1, 2 ...) or of the last Sunday (nth = -1)
int SundayOf(int y, int mo, int nth)
  {
   if(nth > 0)
     {
      int dow1 = TimeDayOfWeek(MakeDT(y, mo, 1, 12, 0));
      int first = 1 + ((7 - dow1) % 7);
      return first + 7 * (nth - 1);
     }
   int dim = DaysInMonth(y, mo);
   int dowL = TimeDayOfWeek(MakeDT(y, mo, dim, 12, 0));
   return dim - dowL;
  }

//--- New York summer time: 2nd Sunday of March 07:00 UTC .. 1st Sunday of November 06:00 UTC
bool IsUSDST(datetime utc)
  {
   int y = TimeYear(utc);
   datetime a = MakeDT(y, 3, SundayOf(y, 3, 2), 7, 0);
   datetime b = MakeDT(y, 11, SundayOf(y, 11, 1), 6, 0);
   return (utc >= a && utc < b);
  }

//--- European summer time: last Sunday of March 01:00 UTC .. last Sunday of October 01:00 UTC
bool IsEUDST(datetime utc)
  {
   int y = TimeYear(utc);
   datetime a = MakeDT(y, 3, SundayOf(y, 3, -1), 1, 0);
   datetime b = MakeDT(y, 10, SundayOf(y, 10, -1), 1, 0);
   return (utc >= a && utc < b);
  }

datetime ETtoUTC(datetime etLocal) { return etLocal + (IsUSDST(etLocal + 18000) ? 14400 : 18000); }
datetime UTCtoET(datetime utc) { return utc - (IsUSDST(utc) ? 14400 : 18000); }
datetime CETtoUTC(datetime cetLocal) { return cetLocal - (IsEUDST(cetLocal - 3600) ? 7200 : 3600); }
datetime NowUTC() { return TimeCurrent() - GmtOffsetSec(); }
datetime DayStart(datetime t) { return (datetime)(((long)t / 86400) * 86400); }
string   UtcIst(datetime utc) { return TimeToString(utc + 19800, TIME_MINUTES); }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// news calendar - built-in official dates + manual list + live high-impact calendar
//------------------------------------------------------------------
int NewsAdd(datetime utc, int mask, int before, int after, string name)
  {
   if(utc <= 0 || mask == 0) return -1;
   for(int i = 0; i < g_nwN; i++)
     {
      if((g_nwMask[i] & mask) == 0) continue;
      if(MathAbs((double)(g_nwT[i] - utc)) > 300) continue;
      if(after > g_nwAft[i]) g_nwAft[i] = after;                       // same event from two sources: keep the longer window
      if(before > g_nwBef[i]) g_nwBef[i] = before;
      g_nwMask[i] = g_nwMask[i] | mask;
      return i;
     }
   if(g_nwN >= MAXNW) return -1;
   g_nwT[g_nwN] = utc;
   g_nwMask[g_nwN] = mask;
   g_nwBef[g_nwN] = before;
   g_nwAft[g_nwN] = after;
   g_nwName[g_nwN] = name;
   g_nwNote[g_nwN] = "";
   g_nwN++;
   return g_nwN - 1;
  }

//--- "YYYY.MM.DD,..." at a fixed local time of New York (cet = false) or Frankfurt (cet = true)
void NewsAddList(string dates, int hh, int mi, bool cet, int mask, int before, int after, string name)
  {
   string parts[];
   int n = StringSplit(dates, ',', parts);
   for(int i = 0; i < n; i++)
     {
      string d = TrimStr(parts[i]);
      if(StringLen(d) < 10) continue;
      int y = (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(d, 0, 4));
      int mo = (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(d, 5, 2));
      int dd = (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(d, 8, 2));
      if(y < 2000 || mo < 1 || mo > 12 || dd < 1 || dd > 31) continue;
      datetime loc = MakeDT(y, mo, dd, hh, mi);
      datetime utc = cet ? CETtoUTC(loc) : ETtoUTC(loc);
      NewsAdd(utc, mask, before, after, name);
     }
  }

//--- manual list in IST: "2026.10.21 18:00, 2026.10.29 17:45 EUR, 2026.10.28 20:00 OIL"
void NewsAddManual()
  {
   if(StringLen(NewsManualIST) < 16) return;
   string parts[];
   int n = StringSplit(NewsManualIST, ',', parts);
   for(int i = 0; i < n; i++)
     {
      string w = TrimStr(parts[i]);
      if(StringLen(w) < 16) continue;
      datetime ist = StringToTime(StringSubstr(w, 0, 16));
      if(ist <= 0) continue;
      string tag = TrimStr(StringSubstr(w, 16));
      StringToUpper(tag);
      int mask = NW_USD;
      if(tag == "EUR") mask = NW_EUR;
      else if(tag == "OIL") mask = NW_OIL;
      NewsAdd(ist - 19800, mask, NewsBeforeMin, NewsAfterMin, "news (manual list)");
     }
  }

//--- official dates 2024-2027 (2024-2025 make Strategy Tester runs realistic): FOMC, CPI, jobs report, ECB
void NewsBuild()
  {
   g_nwN = 0;
   if(UseNewsFilter)
     {
      NewsAddList("2024.01.31,2024.03.20,2024.05.01,2024.06.12,2024.07.31,2024.09.18,2024.11.07,2024.12.18,2025.01.29,2025.03.19,2025.05.07,2025.06.18,2025.07.30,2025.09.17,2025.10.29,2025.12.10,2026.01.28,2026.03.18,2026.04.29,2026.06.17,2026.07.29,2026.09.16,2026.10.28,2026.12.09,2027.01.27,2027.03.17,2027.04.28,2027.06.09,2027.07.28,2027.09.15,2027.10.27,2027.12.08",
                  14, 0, false, NW_USD, NewsBeforeMin, CBAfterMin, "FOMC rate decision");
      NewsAddList("2024.01.11,2024.02.13,2024.03.12,2024.04.10,2024.05.15,2024.06.12,2024.07.11,2024.08.14,2024.09.11,2024.10.10,2024.11.13,2024.12.11,2025.01.15,2025.02.12,2025.03.12,2025.04.10,2025.05.13,2025.06.11,2025.07.15,2025.08.12,2025.09.11,2025.10.24,2025.12.18,2026.01.13,2026.02.13,2026.03.11,2026.04.10,2026.05.12,2026.06.10,2026.07.14,2026.08.12,2026.09.11,2026.10.14,2026.11.10,2026.12.10,2027.01.13,2027.02.11,2027.03.10,2027.04.13,2027.05.12,2027.06.10,2027.07.14,2027.08.11,2027.09.14,2027.10.14,2027.11.10,2027.12.10",
                  8, 30, false, NW_USD, NewsBeforeMin, NewsAfterMin, "US CPI inflation");
      NewsAddList("2024.01.05,2024.02.02,2024.03.08,2024.04.05,2024.05.03,2024.06.07,2024.07.05,2024.08.02,2024.09.06,2024.10.04,2024.11.01,2024.12.06,2025.01.10,2025.02.07,2025.03.07,2025.04.04,2025.05.02,2025.06.06,2025.07.03,2025.08.01,2025.09.05,2025.11.20,2025.12.16,2026.01.09,2026.02.11,2026.03.06,2026.04.03,2026.05.08,2026.06.05,2026.07.02,2026.08.07,2026.09.04,2026.10.02,2026.11.06,2026.12.04,2027.01.08,2027.02.05,2027.03.05,2027.04.02,2027.05.07,2027.06.04,2027.07.02,2027.08.06,2027.09.03,2027.10.08,2027.11.05,2027.12.03",
                  8, 30, false, NW_USD, NewsBeforeMin, NewsAfterMin, "US jobs report (NFP)");
      NewsAddList("2024.01.25,2024.03.07,2024.04.11,2024.06.06,2024.07.18,2024.09.12,2024.10.17,2024.12.12,2025.01.30,2025.03.06,2025.04.17,2025.06.05,2025.07.24,2025.09.11,2025.10.30,2025.12.18,2026.02.05,2026.03.19,2026.04.30,2026.06.11,2026.07.23,2026.09.10,2026.10.29,2026.12.17",
                  14, 15, true, NW_EUR, NewsBeforeMin, CBAfterMin, "ECB rate decision");
      NewsAddManual();
     }
   g_nwBase = g_nwN;
  }

//--- jobs report by rule for years without dates in the list: 1st Friday (2nd in early January), 08:30 New York
datetime NFPRuleUTC(int y, int mo)
  {
   int dow1 = TimeDayOfWeek(MakeDT(y, mo, 1, 12, 0));
   int ff = 1 + ((5 - dow1 + 7) % 7);
   if(mo == 1 && ff <= 3) ff += 7;
   return ETtoUTC(MakeDT(y, mo, ff, 8, 30));
  }

int NewsMask()
  {
   int m = NW_USD;                                                      // all five markets react to big US news
   string u = _Symbol;
   StringToUpper(u);
   if(StringFind(u, "EUR") >= 0) m = m | NW_EUR;
   if(g_cls == SC_OIL) m = m | NW_OIL;
   return m;
  }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// EIA weekly crude report: Wednesday 10:30 New York; holiday weeks move (official EIA schedule 2025-2026)
//------------------------------------------------------------------
void EIABuild()
  {
   g_eiaN = 0;
   string a[];
   string b[];
   int na = StringSplit("2025.01.01,2025.01.22,2025.02.19,2025.05.28,2025.09.03,2025.10.15,2025.11.12,2025.12.24,2026.01.21,2026.02.18,2026.05.27,2026.09.09,2026.10.14,2026.11.11", ',', a);
   int nb = StringSplit("2025.01.02 11:00,2025.01.23 12:00,2025.02.20 12:00,2025.05.29 12:00,2025.09.04 12:00,2025.10.16 12:00,2025.11.13 12:00,2025.12.29 17:00,2026.01.22 12:00,2026.02.19 12:00,2026.05.28 12:00,2026.09.10 12:00,2026.10.15 12:00,2026.11.12 12:00", ',', b);
   int n = (na < nb) ? na : nb;
   for(int i = 0; i < n && g_eiaN < MAXEIA; i++)
     {
      datetime sk = StringToTime(TrimStr(a[i]));
      datetime mv = StringToTime(TrimStr(b[i]));
      if(sk <= 0 || mv <= 0) continue;
      g_eiaSkip[g_eiaN] = sk;
      g_eiaMove[g_eiaN] = ETtoUTC(mv);
      g_eiaN++;
     }
  }

//--- release time (UTC) of the EIA report in the New York week that contains 'utc' (+ weekShift weeks)
datetime EIAReleaseOfWeek(datetime utc, int weekShift)
  {
   datetime et = UTCtoET(utc) + weekShift * 7 * 86400;
   int dow = TimeDayOfWeek(et);
   datetime wed = DayStart(et) + (3 - dow) * 86400;
   for(int k = 0; k < g_eiaN; k++) if(g_eiaSkip[k] == wed) return g_eiaMove[k];
   return ETtoUTC(wed + 10 * 3600 + 30 * 60);
  }

bool EIAWindow(datetime utc, datetime &rel)
  {
   rel = 0;
   int bef = EIABeforeMin * 60, aft = EIAAfterMin * 60;
   for(int w = -1; w <= 0; w++)                                         // a moved release can fall in the next week (Christmas)
     {
      datetime r = EIAReleaseOfWeek(utc, w);
      if(utc >= r - bef && utc <= r + aft) { rel = r; return true; }
     }
   return false;
  }

datetime EIANext(datetime utc)
  {
   for(int w = 0; w <= 1; w++)
     {
      datetime r = EIAReleaseOfWeek(utc, w);
      if(r + EIAAfterMin * 60 >= utc) return r;
     }
   return 0;
  }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// news checks for this symbol
//------------------------------------------------------------------
bool NewsBlock(string &why)
  {
   why = "";
   datetime utc = NowUTC();
   int mask = NewsMask();
   if(UseNewsFilter)
     {
      for(int i = 0; i < g_nwN; i++)
        {
         if((g_nwMask[i] & mask) == 0) continue;
         if(utc < g_nwT[i] - g_nwBef[i] * 60 || utc > g_nwT[i] + g_nwAft[i] * 60) continue;
         why = "[NEWS] " + g_nwName[i] + " at " + UtcIst(g_nwT[i]) + " IST - no new trades until " + UtcIst(g_nwT[i] + g_nwAft[i] * 60) + " IST";
         return true;
        }
      datetime et = UTCtoET(utc);
      int y = TimeYear(et);
      if(y < 2024 || y > 2027)
        {
         datetime nf = NFPRuleUTC(y, TimeMonth(et));
         if(utc >= nf - NewsBeforeMin * 60 && utc <= nf + NewsAfterMin * 60)
           {
            why = "[NEWS] US jobs report (NFP, first-Friday rule) at " + UtcIst(nf) + " IST - no new trades until " + UtcIst(nf + NewsAfterMin * 60) + " IST";
            return true;
           }
        }
     }
   if(UseEIAFilter && g_cls == SC_OIL)
     {
      datetime rel = 0;
      if(EIAWindow(utc, rel))
        {
         why = "[EIA] weekly crude oil report at " + UtcIst(rel) + " IST - no new oil trades until " + UtcIst(rel + EIAAfterMin * 60) + " IST";
         return true;
        }
     }
   return false;
  }

//--- does big news start within the next 'minutes'?
bool NewsSoon(int minutes, string &nm, datetime &ev)
  {
   nm = "";
   ev = 0;
   datetime utc = NowUTC();
   int mask = NewsMask();
   if(UseNewsFilter)
     {
      for(int i = 0; i < g_nwN; i++)
        {
         if((g_nwMask[i] & mask) == 0) continue;
         if(g_nwT[i] > utc && g_nwT[i] - utc <= minutes * 60) { nm = g_nwName[i]; ev = g_nwT[i]; return true; }
        }
     }
   if(UseEIAFilter && g_cls == SC_OIL)
     {
      datetime r = EIANext(utc);
      if(r > utc && r - utc <= minutes * 60) { nm = "EIA crude oil report"; ev = r; return true; }
     }
   return false;
  }

string NextNewsText()
  {
   datetime utc = NowUTC();
   int mask = NewsMask();
   datetime best = 0;
   string nm = "";
   if(UseNewsFilter)
     {
      for(int i = 0; i < g_nwN; i++)
        {
         if((g_nwMask[i] & mask) == 0) continue;
         if(g_nwT[i] + g_nwAft[i] * 60 < utc) continue;
         if(best == 0 || g_nwT[i] < best) { best = g_nwT[i]; nm = g_nwName[i]; }
        }
     }
   if(UseEIAFilter && g_cls == SC_OIL)
     {
      datetime r = EIANext(utc);
      if(r > 0 && (best == 0 || r < best)) { best = r; nm = "EIA oil report"; }
     }
   if(best == 0) return "none in the list";
   int mins = (int)((best - utc) / 60);
   string when = (mins > 0) ? "in " + IntegerToString(mins / 60) + "h" + StringFormat("%02d", mins % 60) + "m" : "NOW";
   return nm + " " + TimeToString(best + 19800, TIME_DATE | TIME_MINUTES) + " IST (" + when + ")";
  }

//--- upcoming events for the /news command (next 7 days, all markets)
string NewsListText()
  {
   datetime utc = NowUTC();
   string s = Emo(0x1F4C5) + " <b>NEWS - next 7 days (IST)</b>\n" + Divider();
   bool used[];
   ArrayResize(used, MAXNW);
   for(int z = 0; z < MAXNW; z++) used[z] = false;
   int shown = 0;
   for(int k = 0; k < 14; k++)
     {
      int bi = -1;
      for(int i = 0; i < g_nwN; i++)
        {
         if(used[i] || g_nwT[i] + g_nwAft[i] * 60 < utc || g_nwT[i] > utc + 7 * 86400) continue;
         if(bi < 0 || g_nwT[i] < g_nwT[bi]) bi = i;
        }
      if(bi < 0) break;
      used[bi] = true;
      string mk = ((g_nwMask[bi] & NW_EUR) != 0 && (g_nwMask[bi] & NW_USD) == 0) ? " (EURUSD)" : (((g_nwMask[bi] & NW_OIL) != 0 && (g_nwMask[bi] & NW_USD) == 0) ? " (oil)" : "");
      s += "\n" + TimeToString(g_nwT[bi] + 19800, TIME_DATE | TIME_MINUTES) + " - " + HtmlEsc(g_nwName[bi]) + mk;
      shown++;
     }
   if(UseEIAFilter)
     {
      datetime r = EIANext(utc);
      if(r > 0) s += "\n" + TimeToString(r + 19800, TIME_DATE | TIME_MINUTES) + " - EIA crude oil report (oil)";
     }
   if(shown == 0) s += "\nNo big news in the list for the next 7 days.";
   else
     {
      int ni = -1;
      for(int q = 0; q < g_nwN; q++)
        {
         if(g_nwT[q] + g_nwAft[q] * 60 < utc) continue;
         if(ni < 0 || g_nwT[q] < g_nwT[ni]) ni = q;
        }
      if(ni >= 0) s += "\n\n<b>Next: " + HtmlEsc(g_nwName[ni]) + "</b>\n" + HtmlEsc(NewsImpactAll(g_nwName[ni], g_nwMask[ni]));
     }
   return s + "\n\nNo new trades from " + IntegerToString(NewsBeforeMin) + " min before to " + IntegerToString(NewsAfterMin) + " min after (rate decisions: " + IntegerToString(CBAfterMin) + " min).";
  }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// live high-impact calendar (JSON feed, live only; cached for all charts in the common folder)
//------------------------------------------------------------------
datetime FeedTimeUTC(string s)
  {
   if(StringLen(s) < 16) return 0;
   int y = (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(s, 0, 4));
   int mo = (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(s, 5, 2));
   int d = (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(s, 8, 2));
   int hh = (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(s, 11, 2));
   int mi = (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(s, 14, 2));
   if(y < 2000 || mo < 1 || mo > 12 || d < 1 || d > 31) return 0;
   datetime loc = MakeDT(y, mo, d, hh, mi);
   int off = 0;
   if(StringLen(s) >= 25)
     {
      ushort sg = StringGetCharacter(s, 19);
      int oh = (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(s, 20, 2));
      int om = (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(s, 23, 2));
      off = oh * 3600 + om * 60;
      if(sg == '-') off = -off;
      else if(sg != '+') off = 0;
     }
   return loc - off;                                                    // local = UTC + offset
  }

void NewsParseFeed(string js)
  {
   g_nwN = g_nwBase;
   int cnt = 0;
   int pos = 0;
   int n = StringLen(js);
   while(pos < n)
     {
      int a = StringFind(js, "{", pos);
      if(a < 0) break;
      int b = StringFind(js, "}", a);
      if(b < 0) break;
      string ob = StringSubstr(js, a, b - a + 1);
      pos = b + 1;
      if(JsonValue(ob, "impact", 0) != "High") continue;
      string cur = JsonValue(ob, "country", 0);
      int mask = 0;
      if(cur == "USD") mask = NW_USD;
      else if(cur == "EUR") mask = NW_EUR;
      if(mask == 0) continue;
      datetime utc = FeedTimeUTC(JsonValue(ob, "date", 0));
      if(utc <= 0) continue;
      string title = JsonValue(ob, "title", 0);
      bool cb = (StringFind(title, "FOMC") >= 0 || StringFind(title, "Federal Funds") >= 0 || StringFind(title, "Main Refinancing") >= 0 ||
                 StringFind(title, "Monetary Policy") >= 0 || StringFind(title, "Press Conference") >= 0);
      int ix = NewsAdd(utc, mask, NewsBeforeMin, cb ? CBAfterMin : NewsAfterMin, cur + " " + title);
      string fc = JsonValue(ob, "forecast", 0), pv = JsonValue(ob, "previous", 0);
      if(ix >= 0 && (fc != "" || pv != "")) g_nwNote[ix] = "forecast " + ((fc != "") ? fc : "-") + ", previous " + ((pv != "") ? pv : "-");
      cnt++;
     }
   g_feedState = "live calendar: " + IntegerToString(cnt) + " high-impact USD / EUR events this week";
  }

void NewsCacheWrite(string s)
  {
   int h = FileOpen(NW_CACHE, FILE_WRITE | FILE_TXT | FILE_ANSI | FILE_COMMON);
   if(h == INVALID_HANDLE) return;
   FileWriteString(h, s);
   FileClose(h);
  }

bool NewsCacheRead(string &s)
  {
   s = "";
   int h = FileOpen(NW_CACHE, FILE_READ | FILE_TXT | FILE_ANSI | FILE_COMMON | FILE_SHARE_READ);
   if(h == INVALID_HANDLE) return false;
   while(!FileIsEnding(h)) s += FileReadString(h);
   FileClose(h);
   return (StringLen(s) > 20);
  }

int NewsHttpGet(string url, string &body)
  {
   char data[];
   char res[];
   string rh = "";
   body = "";
   ArrayResize(data, 0);
   ResetLastError();
   int code = WebRequest("GET", url, "User-Agent: Mozilla/5.0\r\n", 5000, data, res, rh);
   if(code == -1)
     {
      int e = GetLastError();
      if(e == 4060 || e == 4014)
        {
         g_feedUrlErr = true;
         g_feedState = "calendar URL not allowed - add it in Tools > Options > Expert Advisors (built-in dates still work)";
         Print("[NEWS] add this address to the WebRequest list: ", url);
        }
      return -1;
     }
   body = CharArrayToString(res, 0, WHOLE_ARRAY, CP_UTF8);
   return code;
  }

//--- called from the timer (live only)
void NewsFeedTick()
  {
   if(IsTesting() || !UseNewsFilter || !UseNewsFeed || StringLen(NewsFeedURL) < 10) { g_feedState = "off - built-in dates only"; return; }
   if(g_feedUrlErr) return;
   datetime nowL = TimeLocal();
   datetime ft = (datetime)GVGet("AK10N_FT", 0);
   int hours = (NewsFeedHours < 1) ? 1 : NewsFeedHours;
   if(ft > 0 && nowL >= ft && nowL - ft < hours * 3600)
     {
      if(g_feedT < ft)
        {
         string js = "";
         if(NewsCacheRead(js)) NewsParseFeed(js);
         g_feedT = ft;
        }
      return;
     }
   datetime lk = (datetime)GVGet("AK10N_LOCK", 0);                     // one chart downloads, the others read the file
   if(lk > 0 && nowL >= lk && nowL - lk < 60) return;
   GVSet("AK10N_LOCK", (double)nowL);
   string body = "";
   int code = NewsHttpGet(NewsFeedURL, body);
   if(code == 200 && StringFind(body, "\"title\"") >= 0)
     {
      NewsCacheWrite(body);
      NewsParseFeed(body);
      g_feedT = nowL;
      GVSet("AK10N_FT", (double)nowL);
      return;
     }
   if(!g_feedUrlErr) g_feedState = "calendar download failed (HTTP " + IntegerToString(code) + ") - built-in dates used, retry in 30 min";
   GVSet("AK10N_FT", (double)(nowL - hours * 3600 + 1800));
  }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// session rules
//------------------------------------------------------------------
bool RolloverTime(string &why)
  {
   if(!UseRolloverBlock) return false;
   datetime utc = NowUTC();
   datetime r = ETtoUTC(DayStart(UTCtoET(utc)) + 17 * 3600);
   if(utc >= r - RolloverBeforeMin * 60 && utc <= r + RolloverAfterMin * 60)
     {
      why = "[ROLLOVER] daily rollover (17:00 New York = " + UtcIst(r) + " IST) - spreads are wide, no new trades until " + UtcIst(r + RolloverAfterMin * 60) + " IST";
      return true;
     }
   return false;
  }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// SESSION FILTER (IST windows "HH:MM-HH:MM", several separated by commas; a window may cross midnight)
//------------------------------------------------------------------
string SessionSpec()
  {
   if(g_cls == SC_GOLD) return SessionGoldIST;
   if(IsCrypto()) return SessionCryptoIST;
   return SessionEURIST;
  }

int HmToMin(string s)
  {
   s = TrimStr(s);
   int c = StringFind(s, ":");
   if(c < 1) return -1;
   int hh = (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(s, 0, c)), mi = (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(s, c + 1));
   if(hh < 0 || hh > 24 || mi < 0 || mi > 59) return -1;
   return hh * 60 + mi;
  }

//--- is the IST time inside the session of this symbol? (bad text = always open, so a typo never stops the EA silently)
bool InSession()
  {
   if(!UseSessionFilter) return true;
   string spec = SessionSpec();
   if(StringLen(TrimStr(spec)) < 3) return true;
   datetime ist = NowIST();
   int m = TimeHour(ist) * 60 + TimeMinute(ist);
   string w[];
   int n = StringSplit(spec, ',', w);
   bool anyOk = false;
   for(int i = 0; i < n; i++)
     {
      int dash = StringFind(w[i], "-");
      if(dash < 1) continue;
      int a = HmToMin(StringSubstr(w[i], 0, dash)), b = HmToMin(StringSubstr(w[i], dash + 1));
      if(a < 0 || b < 0) continue;
      anyOk = true;
      if(a == b || (a == 0 && b >= 1440)) return true;
      if(a < b && m >= a && m < b) return true;
      if(a > b && (m >= a || m < b)) return true;
     }
   return !anyOk;
  }

string SessionText()
  {
   if(!UseSessionFilter) return "session filter off";
   return "session " + TrimStr(SessionSpec()) + " IST: " + (InSession() ? "OPEN" : "closed - no new Pullback / Breakout trades");
  }

//--- Friday filter: no new gold / forex trades from one hour before FridayCloseHour (server time) on Friday
bool FridayBlock()
  {
   if(!UseFridayFilter || IsCrypto()) return false;
   return (TimeDayOfWeek(TimeCurrent()) == 5 && TimeHour(TimeCurrent()) >= FridayCloseHour - 1);
  }

//--- safety filters by time: news and rollover
bool TimeBlock(string &why)
  {
   why = "";
   if(NewsBlock(why)) return true;
   if(RolloverTime(why)) return true;
   return false;
  }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// NEWS IMPACT - what the news is and how it usually moves this symbol
//------------------------------------------------------------------
int NewsKind(string nm)
  {
   string u = nm;
   StringToUpper(u);
   if(StringFind(u, "FOMC") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "FEDERAL FUNDS") >= 0) return NK_FED;
   if(StringFind(u, "ECB") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "MAIN REFINANCING") >= 0) return NK_ECB;
   if(StringFind(u, "CRUDE") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "EIA") >= 0) return NK_OIL;
   if(StringFind(u, "CLAIMS") >= 0) return NK_CLAIMS;
   if(StringFind(u, "CPI") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "PCE") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "PPI") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "INFLATION") >= 0) return NK_INFL;
   if(StringFind(u, "NON-FARM") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "NFP") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "EMPLOYMENT") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "JOBS") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "PAYROLL") >= 0) return NK_JOBS;
   if(StringFind(u, "GDP") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "PMI") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "RETAIL") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "ISM") >= 0) return NK_GROWTH;
   if(StringFind(u, "SPEAK") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "TESTIF") >= 0 || StringFind(u, "PRESS CONFERENCE") >= 0) return NK_SPEECH;
   return NK_OTHER;
  }

string NewsStrongText(int k)
  {
   if(k == NK_FED) return "a hawkish Fed (fewer rate cuts)";
   if(k == NK_INFL) return "hotter inflation than forecast";
   if(k == NK_JOBS) return "stronger jobs than forecast";
   if(k == NK_CLAIMS) return "fewer jobless claims than forecast";
   if(k == NK_GROWTH) return "stronger data than forecast";
   return "a stronger number than forecast";
  }

string NewsWeakText(int k)
  {
   if(k == NK_FED) return "dovish";
   if(k == NK_INFL) return "cooler";
   if(k == NK_JOBS) return "weaker jobs";
   if(k == NK_CLAIMS) return "more claims";
   if(k == NK_GROWTH) return "weaker data";
   return "a weaker number";
  }

//--- the usual effect on THIS chart's symbol
string NewsImpact(string nm, int mask)
  {
   int k = NewsKind(nm);
   bool eur = ((mask & NW_EUR) != 0 && (mask & NW_USD) == 0);
   if(k == NK_OIL) return (g_cls == SC_OIL) ? "a bigger stock build than forecast = oil DOWN, a stock draw = oil UP" : "oil report - little effect on " + _Symbol;
   if(k == NK_SPEECH) return "speech - the tone can move USD both ways";
   if(eur)
     {
      if(g_cls != SC_FX) return "euro news - small effect on " + _Symbol;
      if(k == NK_ECB) return "a hawkish ECB = EURUSD UP, dovish = EURUSD DOWN";
      return "stronger than forecast = EURUSD UP, weaker = EURUSD DOWN";
     }
   string me = "";
   if(g_cls == SC_GOLD) me = "gold DOWN";
   else if(g_cls == SC_OIL) me = "oil often DOWN (strong dollar)";
   else if(IsCrypto()) me = _Symbol + " usually DOWN";
   else me = (StringFind(_Symbol, "USD") > 0) ? _Symbol + " DOWN" : _Symbol + " UP";
   string s = NewsStrongText(k) + " = USD UP = " + me + "; " + NewsWeakText(k) + " = the opposite";
   if(k == NK_FED) s += " (the first move can turn in the press conference)";
   return s;
  }

//--- the usual effect on all five markets (Telegram alert)
string NewsImpactAll(string nm, int mask)
  {
   int k = NewsKind(nm);
   bool eur = ((mask & NW_EUR) != 0 && (mask & NW_USD) == 0);
   if(k == NK_OIL) return "Oil: a bigger stock build than forecast = oil DOWN, a stock draw = oil UP. The first move often fades within 30 minutes.";
   if(k == NK_SPEECH) return "Speech: the tone can move USD both ways.";
   if(eur)
     {
      if(k == NK_ECB) return "EURUSD: a hawkish ECB = UP, dovish = DOWN. Gold, oil and crypto: small effect.";
      return "EURUSD: stronger than forecast = UP, weaker = DOWN. Gold, oil and crypto: small effect.";
     }
   string s = "If " + NewsStrongText(k) + ": USD UP - gold DOWN, EURUSD DOWN, BTC / ETH usually DOWN, oil often DOWN. If " + NewsWeakText(k) + ": the opposite.";
   if(k == NK_FED) s += " The first move can turn in the press conference 30 minutes later.";
   return s;
  }

//--- effect of the next news for this symbol (panel)
string NextNewsImpact()
  {
   datetime utc = NowUTC();
   int mask = NewsMask();
   int bi = -1;
   if(UseNewsFilter)
     {
      for(int i = 0; i < g_nwN; i++)
        {
         if((g_nwMask[i] & mask) == 0) continue;
         if(g_nwT[i] + g_nwAft[i] * 60 < utc) continue;
         if(bi < 0 || g_nwT[i] < g_nwT[bi]) bi = i;
        }
     }
   datetime eia = (UseEIAFilter && g_cls == SC_OIL) ? EIANext(utc) : 0;
   if(eia > 0 && (bi < 0 || eia < g_nwT[bi])) return NewsImpact("EIA crude oil report", NW_OIL);
   if(bi < 0) return "";
   string s = NewsImpact(g_nwName[bi], g_nwMask[bi]);
   if(g_nwNote[bi] != "") s += " (" + g_nwNote[bi] + ")";
   return s;
  }

//--- Telegram: one alert per big news, 15 minutes before (only the chart that reads the commands sends it)
void NewsAlertTick()
  {
   if(!g_tg || !SendNewsAlerts || !UseNewsFilter) return;
   datetime utc = NowUTC();
   for(int i = 0; i < g_nwN; i++)
     {
      if(g_nwT[i] <= utc || g_nwT[i] - utc > NEWS_ALERT_MIN * 60) continue;
      string key = "NEWS_" + IntegerToString((long)g_nwT[i]) + "_" + IntegerToString(g_nwMask[i]);
      string txt = Emo(0x1F4F0) + " <b>NEWS IN " + IntegerToString(NEWS_ALERT_MIN) + " MIN - " + HtmlEsc(g_nwName[i]) + "</b>\n" + Divider() +
                   "\nTime: " + TimeToString(g_nwT[i] + 19800, TIME_MINUTES) + " IST" +
                   ((g_nwNote[i] != "") ? "\n" + HtmlEsc(g_nwNote[i]) : "") +
                   "\n\n" + HtmlEsc(NewsImpactAll(g_nwName[i], g_nwMask[i])) +
                   "\n\nNo new trades from " + IntegerToString(g_nwBef[i]) + " min before to " + IntegerToString(g_nwAft[i]) +
                   " min after the release. Open trades keep their stops.";
      SendOnce(key, AdminChatId, txt);
      if(SendToVip) SendOnce(key, VipChannelId, txt);
     }
   datetime r = EIANext(utc);
   if(UseEIAFilter && r > utc && r - utc <= NEWS_ALERT_MIN * 60)
     {
      string k2 = "NEWS_EIA_" + IntegerToString((long)r);
      string t2 = Emo(0x1F6E2) + " <b>NEWS IN " + IntegerToString(NEWS_ALERT_MIN) + " MIN - EIA crude oil report</b>\n" + Divider() +
                  "\nTime: " + TimeToString(r + 19800, TIME_MINUTES) + " IST\n\n" + HtmlEsc(NewsImpactAll("EIA crude oil report", NW_OIL)) +
                  "\n\nNo new oil trades from " + IntegerToString(EIABeforeMin) + " min before to " + IntegerToString(EIAAfterMin) + " min after the report.";
      SendOnce(k2, AdminChatId, t2);
      if(SendToVip) SendOnce(k2, VipChannelId, t2);
     }
  }


//==================================================================
// SIGNALS (checked once when an H1 / M15 / M5 candle closes; the candle at shift 1)
//==================================================================
//--- trend pullback: EMA20 beyond EMA50, low / high touched the EMA (20 or 50) in the last 3 candles,
//    the candle closed in the trend direction beyond the previous candle and beyond EMA20. +1 buy, -1 sell, 0 none
int SigPullback(int tf, int touch)
  {
   if(iBars(_Symbol, tf) < 120) return 0;
   double c = Cl(tf, 1), o = Op(tf, 1), ph = Hi(tf, 2), pl = Lo(tf, 2);
   double e20 = EMAv(tf, 20, 1), e50 = EMAv(tf, 50, 1);
   if(e20 <= 0 || e50 <= 0) return 0;
   bool tL = false, tS = false;
   for(int s = 1; s <= TouchBars; s++)
     {
      double e = EMAv(tf, touch, s);
      if(Lo(tf, s) <= e) tL = true;
      if(Hi(tf, s) >= e) tS = true;
     }
   if(e20 > e50 && tL && c > o && c > ph && c > e20) return 1;
   if(e20 < e50 && tS && c < o && c < pl && c < e20) return -1;
   return 0;
  }

//--- momentum candle: body at least 60% of the range, range at least 1.2 ATR, close beyond the last 10 candles
int SigMomentum(int tf)
  {
   if(iBars(_Symbol, tf) < 120) return 0;
   double c = Cl(tf, 1), o = Op(tf, 1), a = ATR(tf, 1);
   double rng = Hi(tf, 1) - Lo(tf, 1), body = MathAbs(c - o);
   if(a <= 0 || rng <= 0 || body < 0.6 * rng || rng < 1.2 * a) return 0;
   int kh = iHighest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_HIGH, 10, 2), kl = iLowest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_LOW, 10, 2);
   if(kh < 0 || kl < 0) return 0;
   if(c > o && c > Hi(tf, kh)) return 1;
   if(c < o && c < Lo(tf, kl)) return -1;
   return 0;
  }

//--- fresh 20-candle breakout: the close breaks the last 20 candles and the candle before did not;
//    with BreakoutStrongClose the candle must close in the direction of the break, in the outer third of its range
int SigBreakout(int tf)
  {
   if(iBars(_Symbol, tf) < 120) return 0;
   int kh = iHighest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_HIGH, 20, 2), kl = iLowest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_LOW, 20, 2);
   int kh2 = iHighest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_HIGH, 20, 3), kl2 = iLowest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_LOW, 20, 3);
   if(kh < 0 || kl < 0 || kh2 < 0 || kl2 < 0) return 0;
   double c1 = Cl(tf, 1), c2 = Cl(tf, 2), o1 = Op(tf, 1), h1 = Hi(tf, 1), l1 = Lo(tf, 1);
   double rng = h1 - l1;
   double pos = (rng > 0) ? (c1 - l1) / rng : 0.5;
   if(c1 > Hi(tf, kh) && c2 <= Hi(tf, kh2))
     {
      if(BreakoutStrongClose && !(c1 > o1 && pos >= 2.0 / 3.0)) return 0;
      return 1;
     }
   if(c1 < Lo(tf, kl) && c2 >= Lo(tf, kl2))
     {
      if(BreakoutStrongClose && !(c1 < o1 && pos <= 1.0 / 3.0)) return 0;
      return -1;
     }
   return 0;
  }

//--- PULLBACK M15: pull-back to EMA20, else to EMA50 (H1 must agree when PullbackNeedH1)
int M15PbSignal(int ts, int &fm)
  {
   fm = FM_TPB;
   if(ts == 0) return 0;
   int d = 0;
   if(SigPullback(PERIOD_M15, 20) == ts) { fm = FM_TPB; d = ts; }
   else if(SigPullback(PERIOD_M15, 50) == ts) { fm = FM_TPB50; d = ts; }
   if(d == 0) return 0;
   if(PullbackNeedH1 && !TfAgrees(PERIOD_H1, ts)) return 0;
   return d;
  }

//--- PULLBACK M5: pull-back to EMA20 (H1 and M15 must agree when PullbackNeedH1)
int M5PbSignal(int ts)
  {
   if(ts == 0) return 0;
   if(SigPullback(PERIOD_M5, 20) != ts) return 0;
   if(PullbackNeedH1 && !(TfAgrees(PERIOD_H1, ts) && TfAgrees(PERIOD_M15, ts))) return 0;
   return ts;
  }

//--- BREAKOUT H1: deep pull-back to EMA50 (unless the Pullback H1 setup fired on the same candle), momentum candle, 20-candle breakout
int H1XSignal(int ts, int &fm)
  {
   fm = FM_BO20;
   if(ts == 0) return 0;
   bool pbSame = (UsePullback && PullbackH1 && SigPullback(PERIOD_H1, 20) == ts);
   if(!pbSame && SigPullback(PERIOD_H1, 50) == ts) { fm = FM_TPB50; return ts; }
   if(SigMomentum(PERIOD_H1) == ts) { fm = FM_MOM; return ts; }
   if(SigBreakout(PERIOD_H1) == ts) { fm = FM_BO20; return ts; }
   return 0;
  }

//--- BREAKOUT M15: momentum candle, 20-candle breakout - only while H1 and H4 agree
int M15XSignal(int ts, int &fm)
  {
   fm = FM_BO20;
   if(ts == 0) return 0;
   if(!(TfAgrees(PERIOD_H1, ts) && TfAgrees(PERIOD_H4, ts))) return 0;
   if(SigMomentum(PERIOD_M15) == ts) { fm = FM_MOM; return ts; }
   if(SigBreakout(PERIOD_M15) == ts) { fm = FM_BO20; return ts; }
   return 0;
  }

//--- stop distance (price) for a buy / sell from the 5-candle extreme of tf
double StopDistance(int tf, int d, double &atr)
  {
   atr = ATR(tf, 1);
   if(atr <= 0) return 0;
   int k = (d > 0) ? iLowest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_LOW, StructBars, 1) : iHighest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_HIGH, StructBars, 1);
   if(k < 0) return 0;
   double ext = (d > 0) ? Lo(tf, k) : Hi(tf, k);
   RefreshRates();
   double S = (d > 0) ? Bid - (ext - StopBufferATR * atr) : (ext + StopBufferATR * atr) - Bid;
   if(S < MinStopATR * atr) S = MinStopATR * atr;
   double minS = StopLevelPx() + (Ask - Bid) + 3 * Point;
   if(S < minS) S = minS;
   return S;
  }

//--- what the Pullback method waits for on a timeframe (panel)
string PbStatus(int tf)
  {
   if(iBars(_Symbol, tf) < 120) return "loading " + TfName(tf) + " history";
   int ts = TrendSide();
   double e20 = EMAv(tf, 20, 1), e50 = EMAv(tf, 50, 1);
   if(ts == 0) return "waiting - D1 / H4 disagree";
   if(ts > 0 && e20 <= e50) return "BUY side - wait for " + TfName(tf) + " to turn up";
   if(ts < 0 && e20 >= e50) return "SELL side - wait for " + TfName(tf) + " to turn down";
   if(tf != PERIOD_H1 && PullbackNeedH1 && !TfAgrees(PERIOD_H1, ts)) return "H1 does not agree yet";
   bool touched = false;
   for(int s = 1; s <= TouchBars; s++)
     {
      double e = EMAv(tf, 20, s);
      if(ts > 0 && Lo(tf, s) <= e) touched = true;
      if(ts < 0 && Hi(tf, s) >= e) touched = true;
     }
   if(!touched) return SideName(ts) + " - wait pull-back to EMA20 " + Px(e20);
   return SideName(ts) + " - pulled back, wait strong close";
  }

//--- what the Breakout method waits for (panel)
string BoStatus()
  {
   int ts = TrendSide();
   if(ts == 0) return "waiting - D1 / H4 disagree";
   int kh = iHighest(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, MODE_HIGH, 20, 1), kl = iLowest(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, MODE_LOW, 20, 1);
   string lv = "";
   if(kh >= 0 && kl >= 0) lv = " " + Px((ts > 0) ? Hi(PERIOD_H1, kh) : Lo(PERIOD_H1, kl));
   bool agree = (TfAgrees(PERIOD_H1, ts) && TfAgrees(PERIOD_H4, ts));
   return SideName(ts) + " - watch break of H1 " + ((ts > 0) ? "high" : "low") + lv + (agree ? " (M15 on)" : "");
  }

//==================================================================
// POSITIONS OF THIS CHART (several per method; each one is remembered by its key = open time x 4 + method)
//==================================================================
bool IsTracked(long key)
  {
   for(int i = 0; i < g_pkN; i++) if(g_pk[i] == key) return true;
   return false;
  }

void TrackAdd(long key)
  {
   if(IsTracked(key) || g_pkN >= MAXPOS) return;
   g_pk[g_pkN] = key;
   g_pkN++;
  }

void TrackRemove(long key)
  {
   for(int i = 0; i < g_pkN; i++)
     {
      if(g_pk[i] != key) continue;
      for(int j = i; j < g_pkN - 1; j++) g_pk[j] = g_pk[j + 1];
      g_pkN--;
      return;
     }
  }

//--- open trades of this chart: all methods (m = -1) or one method
int CountMyOpen(int m)
  {
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = OrdersTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
     {
      if(!OrderSelect(i, SELECT_BY_POS, MODE_TRADES)) continue;
      if(!MyOrder()) continue;
      if(m >= 0 && MethodOfMagic(OrderMagicNumber()) != m) continue;
      n++;
     }
   return n;
  }

int CountAKOpen()
  {
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = OrdersTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
     {
      if(!OrderSelect(i, SELECT_BY_POS, MODE_TRADES)) continue;
      if(!IsAKMagic(OrderMagicNumber()) || OrderType() > OP_SELL) continue;
      n++;
     }
   return n;
  }

//--- open market order of this chart with this key (0 = none)
int TicketOfKey(long key)
  {
   for(int i = OrdersTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
     {
      if(!OrderSelect(i, SELECT_BY_POS, MODE_TRADES)) continue;
      if(!MyOrder()) continue;
      if(OrderKey() == key) return OrderTicket();
     }
   return 0;
  }

//--- risk (USD) of all AK v10 trades on the account whose stop is still on the losing side of the entry
//    which: -1 all, 0 Pullback + Breakout + Memory, 1 Trend method only
double OpenRiskUSD() { return OpenRiskUSDw(-1); }
double OpenRiskUSDw(int which)
  {
   double sum = 0;
   for(int i = OrdersTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
     {
      if(!OrderSelect(i, SELECT_BY_POS, MODE_TRADES)) continue;
      if(!IsAKMagic(OrderMagicNumber()) || OrderType() > OP_SELL) continue;
      if(which == 0 && MethodOfMagic(OrderMagicNumber()) == M_TR) continue;
      if(which == 1 && MethodOfMagic(OrderMagicNumber()) != M_TR) continue;
      double sl = OrderStopLoss();
      if(sl <= 0) continue;
      int dir = (OrderType() == OP_BUY) ? 1 : -1;
      double dist = dir * (OrderOpenPrice() - sl);
      if(dist <= 0) continue;
      string sym = OrderSymbol();
      double tv = MarketInfo(sym, MODE_TICKVALUE), ts = MarketInfo(sym, MODE_TICKSIZE);
      if(tv <= 0 || ts <= 0) continue;
      sum += dist * tv / ts * OrderLots();
     }
   return sum;
  }

//--- does method m have an open trade on timeframe tf that is not risk-free yet? (no partial booked, stop behind the entry)
bool MethodTfAtRisk(int m, int tf)
  {
   for(int i = OrdersTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
     {
      if(!OrderSelect(i, SELECT_BY_POS, MODE_TRADES)) continue;
      if(!MyOrder() || MethodOfMagic(OrderMagicNumber()) != m) continue;
      long key = OrderKey();
      if(LayerTf((int)PGet(key, "LAY", LY_H1)) != tf) continue;
      int dir = (OrderType() == OP_BUY) ? 1 : -1;
      double sl = OrderStopLoss();
      if(sl > 0 && dir * (sl - OrderOpenPrice()) >= 0) continue;
      return true;
     }
   return false;
  }

//--- minutes the market was open since the trade opened (M1 candles, as in the tests; clock time as a fallback)
int MarketMinutes(long key)
  {
   int sh = iBarShift(_Symbol, PERIOD_M1, KeyTime(key), false);
   if(sh >= 0) return sh;
   return (int)((TimeCurrent() - KeyTime(key)) / 60);
  }

//==================================================================
// ENTRY GATES + SKIP LOG
//==================================================================
//--- closed result of today on the whole account (all charts): one running total per day, so no scan of all the global
//--- variables every few seconds (there can be thousands: Telegram message ids, statistics)
string DayPLName() { return g_SP + DateKey() + "_ALLPL"; }
double DayPLAll()
  {
   string tot = DayPLName();
   if(GlobalVariableCheck(tot)) return GlobalVariableGet(tot);
   //--- first call of the day (or after an update from an older version): add up the per-symbol results once
   string pre = g_SP + DateKey() + "_";
   double pl = 0;
   int n = GlobalVariablesTotal();
   for(int i = 0; i < n; i++)
     {
      string nm = GlobalVariableName(i);
      if(StringFind(nm, pre) != 0) continue;
      if(StringSubstr(nm, StringLen(nm) - 3) != "_PL") continue;
      pl += GlobalVariableGet(nm);
     }
   GlobalVariableSet(tot, pl);
   return pl;
  }

//--- add to a global variable shared by several charts (compare-and-set, so two charts closing at once both count)
void GVAdd(string name, double v)
  {
   for(int k = 0; k < 20; k++)
     {
      if(!GlobalVariableCheck(name)) { GlobalVariableSet(name, v); return; }
      double old = GlobalVariableGet(name);
      if(GlobalVariableSetOnCondition(name, old + v, old)) return;
     }
   GlobalVariableSet(name, GlobalVariableGet(name) + v);
  }

//--- highest equity seen (account-wide, used by the drawdown stop)
double EquityPeak() { return GVGet(g_SP + "EQPK", 0); }

void UpdateEquityPeak()
  {
   double eq = AccountEquity();
   if(eq > EquityPeak()) GVSet(g_SP + "EQPK", eq);
  }

bool MarginOK(double lots, string &why)
  {
   ResetLastError();
   double fm = AccountFreeMarginCheck(_Symbol, OP_BUY, lots);
   if(fm <= 0 || GetLastError() == 134) { why = "not enough free margin for " + DoubleToString(lots, 2) + " lot"; return false; }
   if(MinMarginLevel > 0)
     {
      double need = MarketInfo(_Symbol, MODE_MARGINREQUIRED) * lots;
      double used = AccountMargin() + need;
      if(used > 0)
        {
         double lvl = 100.0 * AccountEquity() / used;
         if(lvl < MinMarginLevel) { why = "margin level would fall to " + DoubleToString(lvl, 0) + "% (minimum " + DoubleToString(MinMarginLevel, 0) + "%)"; return false; }
        }
     }
   return true;
  }

//--- quiet = a normal limit (cap / risk limit): shown on the panel, not written to the skip log
bool EntryGates(double S, double lots, string &why, bool &quiet, int m)
  {
   why = "";
   quiet = false;
   if(!g_canTrade) { why = g_block; quiet = true; return false; }
   if(!IsTesting() && (!IsTradeAllowed() || !IsConnected())) { why = "AutoTrading is off or the terminal is not connected"; quiet = true; return false; }
   if(!IsTesting() && MarketInfo(_Symbol, MODE_TRADEALLOWED) == 0) { why = "trading is closed for this symbol right now"; quiet = true; return false; }
