   if(iBars(_Symbol, PERIOD_D1) < 60 || iBars(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4) < 60) { why = "loading price history (needs 60 D1 candles)"; quiet = true; return false; }
   int capS = (MaxTradesPerSymbol < 1) ? 1 : MaxTradesPerSymbol;
   if(CountMyOpen(-1) >= capS) { why = "max " + IntegerToString(capS) + " open trade(s) on " + _Symbol; quiet = true; return false; }
   int capM = MethodCap(m);
   if(CountMyOpen(m) >= capM) { why = "max " + IntegerToString(capM) + " open " + MethodName(m) + " trade(s) on " + _Symbol; quiet = true; return false; }
   if(MaxOpenAccount > 0 && CountAKOpen() >= MaxOpenAccount) { why = "max " + IntegerToString(MaxOpenAccount) + " trades open on the account"; quiet = true; return false; }
   if(UseDailyLossLimit && DailyLossLimitUSD > 0)
     {
      double dpl = DayPLAll();
      if(dpl <= -DailyLossLimitUSD) { why = "daily loss limit reached (" + Usd(dpl) + " USD) - new trades tomorrow"; quiet = true; return false; }
     }
   if(UseMaxDDStop && MaxDDStopPct > 0)
     {
      double pk = EquityPeak(), eq = AccountEquity();
      if(pk > 0 && 100.0 * (pk - eq) / pk >= MaxDDStopPct)
        {
         why = "equity is " + DoubleToString(100.0 * (pk - eq) / pk, 1) + "% below its peak (stop at " + DoubleToString(MaxDDStopPct, 1) + "%) - no new trades";
         quiet = true;
         return false;
        }
     }
   if(FridayBlock()) { why = "Friday evening - no new trades before the weekend"; quiet = true; return false; }
   double riskUSD = S * ValuePerPrice(lots);
   double bal = AccountBalance();
   if(MaxRiskPerTradePct > 0 && bal > 0 && riskUSD > MaxRiskPerTradePct / 100.0 * bal)
     {
      why = "the stop would risk " + DoubleToString(riskUSD, 2) + " USD = " + DoubleToString(100.0 * riskUSD / bal, 1) + "% of the balance (limit " + DoubleToString(MaxRiskPerTradePct, 1) + "%)";
      return false;
     }
   double lim = (m == M_TR) ? TrendRiskPct : MaxOpenRiskPct;
   if(lim > 0 && bal > 0)
     {
      double orisk = OpenRiskUSDw((m == M_TR) ? 1 : 0);
      if(orisk + riskUSD > lim / 100.0 * bal)
        {
         why = ((m == M_TR) ? "Trend risk budget: open Trend risk " : "risk limit: open risk ") + DoubleToString(orisk, 2) + " + this trade " + DoubleToString(riskUSD, 2) + " USD is more than " + DoubleToString(lim, 1) +
               "% of the balance (" + DoubleToString(lim / 100.0 * bal, 2) + " USD)";
         quiet = true;
         return false;
        }
     }
   if(!MarginOK(lots, why)) return false;
   RefreshRates();
   double spr = Ask - Bid;
   if(UseSpreadFilter && g_spreadAvg > 0 && spr > AbnormalSpreadMult * g_spreadAvg) { why = "spread suddenly " + DoubleToString(spr / g_spreadAvg, 1) + "x its normal size"; return false; }
   return true;
  }

void CsvLine(string file, string header, string line)
  {
   if(!WriteCsv) return;
   int h = FileOpen(file, FILE_READ | FILE_WRITE | FILE_TXT | FILE_ANSI | FILE_SHARE_READ);
   if(h == INVALID_HANDLE) { Print("[CSV] cannot open ", file, " error ", GetLastError()); return; }
   bool fresh = (FileSize(h) == 0);
   FileSeek(h, 0, SEEK_END);
   if(fresh) FileWriteString(h, header + "\r\n");
   FileWriteString(h, line + "\r\n");
   FileClose(h);
  }

string CsvSafe(string s) { StringReplace(s, ",", ";"); StringReplace(s, "\n", " "); StringReplace(s, "\r", " "); return s; }

void LogSkip(int ly, int fm, int dir, double entry, double sl, string why)
  {
   g_lastSkip = SideName(dir) + " " + MethodName(MethodOfLayer(ly)) + " " + SetupName(ly, fm) + " skipped: " + why;
   g_lastSkipT = TimeCurrent();
   CsvLine("AK10_skipped_" + DateKey() + "_" + _Symbol + ".csv", "Date,Symbol,Side,TimeIST,Method,Layer,Setup,Reason,Entry,SL",
           DateKey() + "," + _Symbol + "," + SideName(dir) + "," + IstStamp(TimeCurrent()) + "," + MethodName(MethodOfLayer(ly)) + "," + LayerName(ly) + "," + FamName(fm) + "," +
           CsvSafe(why) + "," + Px(entry) + "," + Px(sl));
   Print("[AK10] ", g_lastSkip);
  }

//--- last signal of a method (panel) + its history key
//--- a skipped signal: panel note + grey chart mark with the reason
void NoteMark(int m, int d, int tf, int fm, string txt, int hkey)
  {
   Note(m, txt, hkey);
   int p = StringFind(txt, " - ");
   MarkSignal(m, d, tf, FamCode(fm) + " " + TfName(tf), false, (p >= 0) ? StringSubstr(txt, p + 3) : txt);
  }

void Note(int m, string txt, int hkey)
  {
   if(m < 0 || m > 3) return;
   g_note[m] = txt;
   g_noteT[m] = TimeCurrent();
   if(hkey >= 0) g_noteKey[m] = hkey;
  }

//==================================================================
// SCAN - a new H1 / M15 / M5 candle: check the setups of each method (each method on its own)
//==================================================================
int g_scanDir[4];                   // direction of a trade opened on this tick on M5 / M15 / H1 / H4 (one trade per candle and direction)
int TfSlot(int tf) { return (tf == PERIOD_M5) ? 0 : ((tf == PERIOD_M15) ? 1 : ((tf == PERIOD_H4) ? 3 : 2)); }

string TradeStory(int d, int ly, int fm)
  {
   string tf = TfName(LayerTf(ly));
   string side = (d > 0) ? "UP" : "DOWN";
   string s = _Symbol + " is trending " + side + " on D1 and H4. ";
   if(fm == FM_TPB) s += "Price pulled back to the " + tf + " EMA20 and the last " + tf + " candle closed " + ((d > 0) ? "above the previous high" : "below the previous low") +
                       ", so " + ((d > 0) ? "buyers" : "sellers") + " are back - we join the trend.";
   else if(fm == FM_TPB50) s += "Price pulled back deeper, to the " + tf + " EMA50, and the last candle closed " + ((d > 0) ? "above the previous high" : "below the previous low") + " - the trend is starting again.";
   else if(fm == FM_MOM) s += "A strong " + tf + " candle with a big body closed " + ((d > 0) ? "above" : "below") + " the last 10 candles" + ((ly == LY_M15X) ? ", and H1 and H4 trend " + side + " too" : "") + " - momentum is with the trend.";
   else s += "The " + tf + " close broke " + ((d > 0) ? "above" : "below") + " the last 20 candles with a strong candle (a fresh breakout)" + ((ly == LY_M15X) ? ", and H1 and H4 trend " + side + " too." : ".");
   return s;
  }

//--- the study filters (ON / OFF): why = reason, true = skip
bool StudyFilter(int d, double S, string &why)
  {
   why = "";
   RefreshRates();
   if(g_cls == SC_GOLD && GoldMaxSpreadPct > 0 && S > 0 && (Ask - Bid) > GoldMaxSpreadPct / 100.0 * S) { why = "gold filter: spread above " + DoubleToString(GoldMaxSpreadPct, 0) + "% of the stop"; return true; }
   if(g_cls == SC_GOLD && GoldNeedH4 && !TfAgrees(PERIOD_H4, d)) { why = "gold filter: H4 trend does not agree"; return true; }
   if(IsCrypto() && CryptoNoWild && VolBand() == 2) { why = "crypto filter: wild market"; return true; }
   return false;
  }

//--- returns true when a trade was opened
bool TryEntry(int ly)
  {
   int m = MethodOfLayer(ly);
   int ts = TrendSide();
   int fm = FM_TPB;
   int tf = LayerTf(ly);
   int d = 0;
   if(ly == LY_H1) d = SigPullback(PERIOD_H1, 20);
   else if(ly == LY_M15) d = M15PbSignal(ts, fm);
   else if(ly == LY_M5) d = M5PbSignal(ts);
   else if(ly == LY_H1X) d = H1XSignal(ts, fm);
   else d = M15XSignal(ts, fm);
   if(d == 0) return false;
   string head = SideName(d) + " " + SetupName(ly, fm) + " at " + IstClock(TimeCurrent()) + " IST";
   if(SetupSkipped(ly, fm)) { Note(m, head + " - no trade: this setup is switched off for " + ClassName(g_cls) + " (SkipSetups)", -1); return false; }
   if(ts != d)
     {
      Note(m, head + " - no trade: the D1 / H4 trend does not agree", -1);
      return false;
     }
   int hkey = HistKeyNow(HistSetup(ly, fm), d);
   //--- market mode (v8 map): which plan fits this market? filtered signals never take a trade slot
   int ex = EX_TREND;
   string mwhy = "";
   if(!MarketPlan(d, ly, ex, mwhy)) { NoteMark(m, d, tf, fm, head + " - skipped: " + mwhy, hkey); return false; }
   if(!InSession()) { NoteMark(m, d, tf, fm, head + " - skipped: outside the session (" + TrimStr(SessionSpec()) + " IST)", hkey); return false; }
   if(g_scanDir[TfSlot(tf)] == d) { NoteMark(m, d, tf, fm, head + " - skipped: this " + TfName(tf) + " candle was already traded " + SideName(d) + " by the Pullback method", hkey); return false; }
   if(StackOnlyAfterBE && MethodTfAtRisk(m, tf)) { NoteMark(m, d, tf, fm, head + " - skipped: the open " + TfName(tf) + " " + MethodName(m) + " trade is not risk-free yet", hkey); return false; }
   string hv = "";
   if(HistoryMode == HIST_FOLLOW && HistSaysSkip(hkey, hv)) { NoteMark(m, d, tf, fm, head + " - skipped by history: " + hv, hkey); return false; }
   double atr = 0;
   double S = StopDistance(tf, d, atr);
   if(S <= 0) return false;
   RefreshRates();
   double spread = Ask - Bid;
   double entry = (d > 0) ? Ask : Bid;
   double sl = NormalizeDouble(entry - d * S, Digits);
   double lots = TradeLots(S);
   if(m == M_PB && g_cls == SC_GOLD && GoldPullbackMode == GM_SMALL) lots = NormLot(lots * 0.5);    // gold Pullback: small lot (option)
   string gnote = "";
   lots = GuardLots(lots, d, gnote);
   if(lots <= 0) { NoteMark(m, d, tf, fm, head + " - skipped: " + gnote, hkey); return false; }
   string why = "";
   bool quiet = false;
   string fw = "";
   if(StudyFilter(d, S, fw)) { NoteMark(m, d, tf, fm, head + " - skipped: " + fw, hkey); return false; }
   if(UseSpreadFilter && MaxSpreadPctSL > 0 && spread > MaxSpreadPctSL / 100.0 * S) why = "spread is " + DoubleToString(100.0 * spread / S, 0) + "% of the stop (limit " + DoubleToString(MaxSpreadPctSL, 0) + "%)";
   else if(EntryGates(S, lots, why, quiet, m))
     {
      string tb = "";
      if(TimeBlock(tb)) why = tb;
     }
   if(why != "")
     {
      NoteMark(m, d, tf, fm, head + (quiet ? " - not taken: " : " - skipped: ") + why, hkey);
      if(quiet) g_status = SideName(d) + " " + MethodName(m) + " " + SetupName(ly, fm) + " not taken: " + why;
      else LogSkip(ly, fm, d, entry, sl, why);
      return false;
     }
   return PlaceTrade(ly, fm, d, sl, S, atr, lots, ex, hkey);
  }

void ScanSetups()
  {
   datetime h1 = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, 0), m15 = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_M15, 0), m5 = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_M5, 0), h4 = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, 0);
   bool newH4 = (h4 > 0 && h4 != g_lastH4);
   if(newH4) g_lastH4 = h4;
   bool newH1 = (h1 > 0 && h1 != g_lastH1);
   bool newM15 = (m15 > 0 && m15 != g_lastM15);
   bool newM5 = (m5 > 0 && m5 != g_lastM5);
   if(newH1) g_lastH1 = h1;
   if(newM15) g_lastM15 = m15;
   if(newM5) g_lastM5 = m5;
   if(!SymbolSupported()) return;
   for(int q = 0; q < 4; q++) g_scanDir[q] = 0;
   //--- METHOD 1 PULLBACK (at most one new trade per tick: H1 first, then M15, then M5)
   if(UsePullback && V10Here() && !(g_cls == SC_GOLD && GoldPullbackMode == GM_OFF))
     {
      bool done = false;
      if(newH1 && PullbackH1) done = TryEntry(LY_H1);
      if(!done && newM15 && PullbackM15) done = TryEntry(LY_M15);
      if(!done && newM5 && PullbackM5) TryEntry(LY_M5);
     }
   //--- METHOD 2 BREAKOUT (H1 first, then M15)
   if(UseBreakout && V10Here())
     {
      bool done2 = false;
      if(newH1 && BreakoutH1) done2 = TryEntry(LY_H1X);
      if(!done2 && newM15 && BreakoutM15) TryEntry(LY_M15X);
     }
   //--- METHOD 4 TREND H4 / H1 (the entries that tested positive on this symbol)
   ScanTrend(newH1, newH4);
   //--- METHOD 3 MEMORY (gold: learnt setups on M15 / H1)
   MemScan(newM15, newH1);
  }

//==================================================================
// ORDERS
//==================================================================
bool RetryErr(int e) { return (e == 4 || e == 6 || e == 8 || e == 128 || e == 129 || e == 135 || e == 136 || e == 137 || e == 138 || e == 146); }

//--- exit style for Pullback / Breakout / Trend on this symbol (-1 = the one that tested best here)
//--- SkipSetups: "ETH:Pullback M5;ETH:Breakout H1 20-candle" (symbol class : start of layer name + setup name)
bool SetupSkipped(int ly, int fm)
  {
   if(SkipSetups == "") return false;
   string me = LayerName(ly) + " " + FamName(fm);
   string parts[];
   int n = StringSplit(SkipSetups, ';', parts);
   for(int i = 0; i < n; i++)
     {
      string p = TrimStr(parts[i]);
      int c = StringFind(p, ":");
      if(c <= 0) continue;
      if(StringSubstr(p, 0, c) != ClassName(g_cls)) continue;
      string w = TrimStr(StringSubstr(p, c + 1));
      if(w != "" && StringFind(me, w) == 0) return true;
     }
   return false;
  }

int ExitStyleNow()
  {
   if(ExitStyle >= 0) return ExitStyle;
   if(ExitBestPerSymbol && (g_cls == SC_BTC || g_cls == SC_ETH)) return 2;
   if(g_cls == SC_BTC || g_cls == SC_ETH) return 1;
   return 0;
  }

//--- exit numbers: the inputs, or (ExitStyle -1 + ExitBestPerSymbol) the ones that tested best on this symbol
bool ExitAuto() { return (ExitStyle < 0 && ExitBestPerSymbol); }
double NoLossNow() { return (ExitAuto() && (g_cls == SC_ETH || g_cls == SC_OIL)) ? 1.5 : NoLossAtR; }
double TrailStartNow() { return (ExitAuto() && g_cls == SC_GOLD) ? 4.0 : TrailStartR; }
double TrailBehindNow() { return (ExitAuto() && g_cls == SC_GOLD) ? 2.5 : TrailBehindR; }

bool PlaceTrade(int ly, int fm, int d, double sl, double S, double atr, double lots, int ex, int hkey)
  {
   int m = MethodOfLayer(ly);
   int cmd = (d > 0) ? OP_BUY : OP_SELL;
   string cmt = TradeComment + " " + LayerTag(ly) + " " + FamCode(fm) + ((ex == EX_QUICK) ? " Q" : " T");
   int tk = -1;
   int err = 0;
   double price = 0;
   for(int a = 0; a < 3 && tk < 0; a++)
     {
      RefreshRates();
      price = (d > 0) ? Ask : Bid;
      tk = OrderSend(_Symbol, cmd, lots, NormalizeDouble(price, Digits), 10, sl, 0, cmt, MethodMagic(m), 0, (d > 0) ? clrDodgerBlue : clrTomato);
      if(tk < 0)
        {
         err = GetLastError();
         if(err == 130)
           {
            //--- some brokers refuse stops on a market order: open first, set the stop right after
            tk = OrderSend(_Symbol, cmd, lots, NormalizeDouble(price, Digits), 10, 0, 0, cmt, MethodMagic(m), 0, (d > 0) ? clrDodgerBlue : clrTomato);
            if(tk > 0 && OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET)) { if(!OrderModify(tk, OrderOpenPrice(), sl, 0, 0, clrNONE)) Print("[AK10] stop could not be set right away, error ", GetLastError(), " - retrying from the trade manager"); }
            break;
           }
         if(!RetryErr(err)) break;
         Sleep(300);
        }
     }
   if(tk < 0) { LogSkip(ly, fm, d, price, sl, "order rejected by the broker (error " + IntegerToString(err) + ")"); return false; }
   g_scanDir[TfSlot(LayerTf(ly))] = d;
   string pre = g_SP + DateKey() + "_" + _Symbol;
   GVSet(pre + "_OPN", GVGet(pre + "_OPN", 0) + 1);
   GVSet(pre + "_OPN" + MethodTag(m), GVGet(pre + "_OPN" + MethodTag(m), 0) + 1);
   if(!OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET)) { Print("[AK10] order #", tk, " opened but could not be read yet"); return true; }
   long key = OrderKey();
   double open = OrderOpenPrice();
   double R = d * (open - sl);
   if(R <= 0) R = S;
   PClear(key);
   PSet(key, "DIR", d);
   PSet(key, "LAY", ly);
   PSet(key, "FAM", fm);
   PSet(key, "EN", open);
   PSet(key, "SL0", sl);
   PSet(key, "CSL", sl);
   PSet(key, "R", R);
   PSet(key, "LOT0", lots);
   PSet(key, "TIER", 0);
   PSet(key, "ATR", atr);
   PSet(key, "SPP", (R > 0) ? 100.0 * (Ask - Bid) / R : 0);
   PSet(key, "TD1", TrendOf(PERIOD_D1));
   PSet(key, "TH4", TrendOf(PERIOD_H4));
   PSet(key, "PUB", (SendToPublic && LayerTf(ly) >= PERIOD_H1) ? 1 : 0);
   PSet(key, "VIP", (SendToVip && (!LowTfLayer(ly) || VipLowTFCards)) ? 1 : 0);
   PSet(key, "VRK", VolRank());
   PSet(key, "EXP", ex);
   PSet(key, "HK", hkey);
   PSet(key, "PB", g_ptsB);
   PSet(key, "PS", g_ptsS);
   PSet(key, "PTB", (double)g_ptsBar);
   PSet(key, "PRT", 0);
   if(ex == EX_TX) PSet(key, "MAXM", 96.0 * LayerTf(ly));
   PSet(key, "XS", (g_nextXS >= 0) ? g_nextXS : ExitStyleNow());
   PSet(key, "XT", (g_nextXT > 0) ? g_nextXT : 3.0);
   g_nextXS = -1;
   g_nextXT = 0;                 // Trend method: closed after 96 candles at the latest (as tested)
   //--- QUICK plan: the 2R target sits on the order itself
   if(ex == EX_QUICK && QuickTargetR > 0)
     {
      double tp = NormalizeDouble(open + d * QuickTargetR * R, Digits);
      PSet(key, "TP", tp);
      if(OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET) && OrderTakeProfit() <= 0)
        {
         if(!OrderModify(tk, OrderOpenPrice(), OrderStopLoss(), tp, 0, clrNONE)) Print("[AK10] target could not be set right away, error ", GetLastError(), " - retrying from the trade manager");
        }
     }
   TrackAdd(key);
   MarkSignal(m, d, LayerTf(ly), FamCode(fm) + " " + TfName(LayerTf(ly)), true, "");
   NotifyOpen(key, TradeStory(d, ly, fm));
   InstaEvent("open", key, open, 0, "");
   string head = SideName(d) + " " + SetupName(ly, fm) + " at " + IstClock(TimeCurrent()) + " IST";
   Note(m, head + " -> OPENED " + DoubleToString(lots, 2) + " lot @ " + Px(open) + " (" + PlanName(ex) + ")", hkey);
   g_status = "opened " + MethodName(m) + " " + SideName(d) + " " + SetupName(ly, fm) + " (" + PlanName(ex) + ") @ " + Px(open);
   SetLastTrade(IstStamp(TimeCurrent()) + " IST | " + MethodName(m) + " | " + SideName(d) + " | " + SetupName(ly, fm) + " | " + DoubleToString(lots, 2) + " lot @ " + Px(open) + " SL " + Px(sl));
   Print("[AK10] ", MethodName(m), " ", SetupName(ly, fm), " | ", SideName(d), " ", _Symbol, " ", DoubleToString(lots, 2), " lot @", Px(open), " SL ", Px(sl), " | risk ",
         DoubleToString(R * ValuePerPrice(lots), 2), " USD | D1 ", TrendWord(TrendOf(PERIOD_D1)), " H4 ", TrendWord(TrendOf(PERIOD_H4)), " | points B", g_ptsB, "/S", g_ptsS);
   return true;
  }

bool ModifySL(int tk, int dir, long key, double nsl)
  {
   if(!OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET) || OrderCloseTime() > 0) return false;
   nsl = NormalizeDouble(nsl, Digits);
   double cur = OrderStopLoss();
   if(cur > 0 && dir * (nsl - cur) <= Point / 2.0) return true;          // never widen; already there
   RefreshRates();
   double px = (dir > 0) ? Bid : Ask;
   double minD = StopLevelPx() + MarketInfo(_Symbol, MODE_FREEZELEVEL) * Point + Point;
   if(dir * (px - nsl) <= minD) return false;                            // too close to the price right now
   for(int a = 0; a < 3; a++)
     {
      if(OrderModify(tk, OrderOpenPrice(), nsl, OrderTakeProfit(), 0, clrNONE)) { PSet(key, "CSL", nsl); return true; }
      int e = GetLastError();
      if(!RetryErr(e)) { Print("[AK10] stop move failed, error ", e); break; }
      Sleep(200);
      RefreshRates();
      if(!OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET)) return false;
     }
   return false;
  }

//--- put the QUICK target on the order (only when the price is far enough from it)
bool SetTP(int tk, int dir, double tp)
  {
   if(!OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET) || OrderCloseTime() > 0) return false;
   tp = NormalizeDouble(tp, Digits);
   RefreshRates();
   double px = (dir > 0) ? Bid : Ask;
   double minD = StopLevelPx() + MarketInfo(_Symbol, MODE_FREEZELEVEL) * Point + Point;
   if(dir * (tp - px) <= minD) return false;
   if(OrderModify(tk, OrderOpenPrice(), OrderStopLoss(), tp, 0, clrNONE)) return true;
   Print("[AK10] target could not be set, error ", GetLastError());
   return false;
  }

bool CloseLots(int tk, int dir, double lots)
  {
   for(int a = 0; a < 3; a++)
     {
      RefreshRates();
      double px = (dir > 0) ? Bid : Ask;
      if(OrderClose(tk, lots, NormalizeDouble(px, Digits), 10, clrGold)) return true;
      int e = GetLastError();
      if(!RetryErr(e)) { Print("[AK10] close failed, error ", e); return false; }
      Sleep(300);
     }
   return false;
  }

void CloseTrade(int tk, int dir, long key, int reason)
  {
   if(!OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET) || OrderCloseTime() > 0) return;
   PSet(key, "CR", reason);
   if(!CloseLots(tk, dir, OrderLots())) { PSet(key, "CR", CR_NONE); return; }
   if(TicketOfKey(key) == 0 && OnPositionClosed(key)) TrackRemove(key);
  }

//--- book part of the trade at the first target, then the stop of the rest goes to entry (returns true when booked)
bool BookPartial(int tk, int dir, long key, double plots, double beLv)
  {
   if(!OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET) || OrderCloseTime() > 0) return false;
   double open = OrderOpenPrice();
   RefreshRates();
   double px = (dir > 0) ? Bid : Ask;
   if(!CloseLots(tk, dir, plots)) return false;
   double pl = dir * (px - open) * ValuePerPrice(plots);
   PSet(key, "PRT", 1);
   PSet(key, "PRTLOT", plots);
   PSet(key, "PRTPX", px);
   PSet(key, "PRTPL", pl);
   PSet(key, "PRTT", (double)TimeCurrent());
   int nt = TicketOfKey(key);
   if(nt > 0 && ModifySL(nt, dir, key, beLv)) PSet(key, "NLN", 1);
   NotifyPartial(key, dir, plots, px, pl);
   InstaEvent("partial", key, px, PartialAtR, "");
   Print("[AK10] ", MethodName(KeyMethod(key)), " ", SideName(dir), " ", _Symbol, ": booked ", DoubleToString(plots, 2), " lot at the first target @", Px(px), " (", Usd(pl), " USD), stop of the rest to entry");
   return true;
  }

//==================================================================
// TRADE MANAGEMENT (every tick, each open trade of this chart)
//==================================================================
//--- H4 agreement, worked out once per H4 candle (it only changes when an H4 candle closes)
bool H4Agrees(int dir)
  {
   static datetime bar = 0;
   static bool up = false;
   static bool dn = false;
   datetime b = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, 0);
   if(b != bar || b == 0)
     {
      bar = b;
      up = TfAgrees(PERIOD_H4, 1);
      dn = TfAgrees(PERIOD_H4, -1);
     }
   return (dir > 0) ? up : dn;
  }

void ManageOne(int tk, long key)
  {
   if(PGet(key, "IMGO", 0) > 0) TryOpenPhoto(key);                     // entry picture not sent yet: try again
   if(!OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET) || OrderCloseTime() > 0) return;
   int dir = (OrderType() == OP_BUY) ? 1 : -1;
   double open = OrderOpenPrice(), sl = OrderStopLoss();
   RefreshRates();
   double px = (dir > 0) ? Bid : Ask;
   double R = PGet(key, "R", 0);
   if(R <= 0)
     {
      double s0 = PGet(key, "SL0", 0);
      R = (s0 > 0) ? MathAbs(open - s0) : MathAbs(open - sl);
      if(R <= 0) R = ATR(PERIOD_H1, 1);
      if(R <= 0) R = 100 * Point;
      PSet(key, "R", R);
     }
   //--- stop missing (restart / ECN) -> set the planned one; price already beyond it -> close now, never run unprotected
   double sl0 = PGet(key, "SL0", 0);
   if(sl <= 0 && sl0 > 0)
     {
      if(dir * (px - sl0) <= 0) { CloseTrade(tk, dir, key, CR_SL); return; }
      if(ModifySL(tk, dir, key, sl0) && OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET)) sl = OrderStopLoss();
     }
   double fav = dir * (px - open);
   double peak = PGet(key, "PK", 0);
   if(fav > peak) { peak = fav; PSet(key, "PK", peak); }
   if(fav < PGet(key, "WR", 0)) PSet(key, "WR", fav);
   double peakR = peak / R;
   double beLv = open + dir * 0.05 * R;
   int mm = MarketMinutes(key);
   //--- weekend (gold / forex, only when switched on)
   if(CloseBeforeWeekend && !IsCrypto() && TimeDayOfWeek(TimeCurrent()) == 5 && TimeHour(TimeCurrent()) >= FridayCloseHour) { CloseTrade(tk, dir, key, CR_WEEKEND); return; }
   //--- memory trade: its learnt exit (target / trend ride), time limit
   if((int)PGet(key, "LAY", LY_H1) == LY_MEM) { MemManageOne(tk, key, dir, open, sl, px, R, fav, peak, mm); return; }
   int exm = (int)PGet(key, "EXP", EX_TREND);
   //--- 0. time stop (option)
   if(TimeStopCandles > 0 && exm != EX_QUICK && peakR < TimeStopR && mm >= TimeStopCandles * LayerTf((int)PGet(key, "LAY", LY_H1))) { CloseTrade(tk, dir, key, CR_TSTOP); return; }
   //--- 0b. exit style of this trade (0 v10, 1 step lock, 2 full target XT R)
   int xs = (int)PGet(key, "XS", ExitStyleNow());
   double xt = PGet(key, "XT", 3.0);
   if(xs == 2 && exm != EX_QUICK && fav >= xt * R) { CloseTrade(tk, dir, key, CR_TP); return; }
   if(xs != 0 && exm != EX_QUICK && (int)PGet(key, "PRT", 0) == 0) PSet(key, "PRT", 2);   // no partial with the step-lock / target styles
   //--- 1. time limits
   if(exm == EX_TX)
     {
      int mx = (int)PGet(key, "MAXM", 0);
      if(mx > 0 && mm >= mx) { CloseTrade(tk, dir, key, CR_TIME); return; }
     }
   else if(exm == EX_QUICK)
     {
      if(QuickMaxHours > 0 && mm >= QuickMaxHours * 60) { CloseTrade(tk, dir, key, CR_TIME); return; }
     }
   else
     {
      if(MaxHoldDays > 0 && mm >= MaxHoldDays * 1440) { CloseTrade(tk, dir, key, CR_TIME); return; }
      if(MinHoldHours > 0 && mm >= MinHoldHours * 60 && !H4Agrees(dir)) { CloseTrade(tk, dir, key, CR_TREND); return; }
     }
   //--- 2. H4 trend flipped (an H4 candle closed on the other side of the H4 EMA50)
   if(H4FlipExit && exm != EX_TX && TrendOf(PERIOD_H4) == -dir) { CloseTrade(tk, dir, key, CR_H4FLIP); return; }
   //--- 3. trend points rule, once per completed H1 candle: the other side leads by PointsFlipMargin or more
   if(PointsExitMode != PTS_OFF && exm != EX_TX && g_ptsBar > 0 && (datetime)PGet(key, "PTB", 0) != g_ptsBar)
     {
      PSet(key, "PTB", (double)g_ptsBar);
      int lead = (dir > 0) ? g_ptsS - g_ptsB : g_ptsB - g_ptsS;
      if(lead >= PointsFlipMargin)
        {
         if(PointsExitMode == PTS_EXIT) { CloseTrade(tk, dir, key, CR_POINTS); return; }
         double a1 = ATR(PERIOD_H1, 1);
         double ns = (dir > 0) ? Lo(PERIOD_H1, 1) - 0.2 * a1 : Hi(PERIOD_H1, 1) + 0.2 * a1;
         double minD = StopLevelPx() + MarketInfo(_Symbol, MODE_FREEZELEVEL) * Point + Point;
         if(dir * (px - ns) <= minD) { CloseTrade(tk, dir, key, CR_POINTS); return; }   // price is already beyond the last H1 candle
         if(sl <= 0 || dir * (ns - sl) > Point)
           {
            if(ModifySL(tk, dir, key, ns))
              {
               PSet(key, "PTT", PGet(key, "PTT", 0) + 1);
               NotifyUpdate(key, dir, 2, ns);
               if(OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET)) sl = OrderStopLoss();
              }
           }
        }
     }
   //--- 4. first target: book part of the lot (once), then the stop of the rest goes to entry
   if(!OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET) || OrderCloseTime() > 0) return;
   if((int)PGet(key, "PRT", 0) == 0 && PartialAtR > 0 && fav >= PartialAtR * R)       // the price is at the first target now
     {
      double plots = PartialLots(OrderLots());
      if(plots > 0)
        {
         if(BookPartial(tk, dir, key, plots, beLv)) return;
         PSet(key, "PRTF", PGet(key, "PRTF", 0) + 1);                   // the broker refused the partial close: try 3 times, then only the stop
         if(PGet(key, "PRTF", 0) >= 3) { PSet(key, "PRT", 2); Print("[AK10] partial close refused 3 times - only the stop moves to entry on this trade"); }
         if(!OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET) || OrderCloseTime() > 0) return;
        }
      else PSet(key, "PRT", 2);                                          // lot too small to split: only the stop moves to entry
     }
   //--- 5. stop to entry (no-loss): right after the partial, or at NoLossAtR (QUICK: QuickNoLossR)
   double nlR = (exm == EX_QUICK) ? QuickNoLossR : NoLossNow();
   bool wantBE = ((int)PGet(key, "PRT", 0) == 1) || (nlR > 0 && peakR >= nlR);
   if(xs == 2 && exm != EX_QUICK) wantBE = false;                 // 3R style: only stop or target
   if(xs == 1 && exm != EX_QUICK && peakR >= 2.0 && OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET))
     {
      double lk = open + dir * (MathFloor(peakR) - 1.0) * R;            // step lock: 2R -> +1R, 3R -> +2R ...
      sl = OrderStopLoss();
      if(sl <= 0 || dir * (lk - sl) > Point) { if(ModifySL(tk, dir, key, lk)) { PSet(key, "TRL", 1); NotifyUpdate(key, dir, 4, lk); } }
      if(!OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET)) return;
      sl = OrderStopLoss();
     }
   if(wantBE && (sl <= 0 || dir * (sl - open) < 0))
     {
      if(ModifySL(tk, dir, key, beLv))
        {
         PSet(key, "NLN", 1);
         if((int)PGet(key, "PRT", 0) != 1) NotifyUpdate(key, dir, 1, beLv);
        }
      else if(fav <= 0.05 * R) { CloseTrade(tk, dir, key, CR_NOLOSS); return; }   // the stop could not be moved and price is back at entry
      if(!OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET)) return;
      sl = OrderStopLoss();
     }
   //--- 6. QUICK plan: the target sits on the order
   if(exm == EX_QUICK)
     {
      double tpx = PGet(key, "TP", 0);
      if(tpx > 0 && OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET) && OrderTakeProfit() <= 0)
        {
         if(dir * (px - tpx) >= 0) { CloseTrade(tk, dir, key, CR_TP); return; }   // target passed while it was not on the order
         SetTP(tk, dir, tpx);
        }
     }
   //--- 7. trailing stop for the rest: best price minus TrailBehindR x R, from TrailStartR (never behind the entry)
   else if(xs == 0 && TrailStartNow() > 0 && peakR >= TrailStartNow())
     {
      double ns2 = open + dir * (peak - TrailBehindNow() * R);
      if(dir * (ns2 - beLv) < 0) ns2 = beLv;
      if(sl <= 0 || dir * (ns2 - sl) > MathMax(Point, 0.05 * R))           // move in small steps, not on every tick
        {
         if(ModifySL(tk, dir, key, ns2))
           {
            if(PGet(key, "TRL", 0) <= 0) NotifyUpdate(key, dir, 3, ns2);
            PSet(key, "TRL", 1);
           }
        }
     }
   //--- 7b. option: at +2R the stop goes to +1R
   if(LockAt2R && peakR >= 2.0 && OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET))
     {
      double ls = open + dir * 1.0 * R;
      sl = OrderStopLoss();
      if(sl <= 0 || dir * (ls - sl) > Point) ModifySL(tk, dir, key, ls);
     }
   //--- 8. progress ladder (admin cards from +2R)
   int tier = TierOfR(peakR);
   int told = (int)PGet(key, "TIER", 0);
   if(tier > told)
     {
      PSet(key, "TIER", tier);
      if(tier >= TierCardFrom) NotifyTier(key, dir, tier);
     }
  }

//--- best move in the trade's favour since it opened, from the M1 candles (after a restart)
double PeakFromHistory(long key, int dir, double open)
  {
   int sh = iBarShift(_Symbol, PERIOD_M1, KeyTime(key), false);
   if(sh < 0) return 0;
   double best = 0;
   if(dir > 0)
     {
      int k = iHighest(_Symbol, PERIOD_M1, MODE_HIGH, sh + 1, 0);
      if(k >= 0) best = iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_M1, k) - open;
     }
   else
     {
      int k2 = iLowest(_Symbol, PERIOD_M1, MODE_LOW, sh + 1, 0);
      if(k2 >= 0) best = open - iLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_M1, k2) - (Ask - Bid);
     }
   return (best > 0) ? best : 0;
  }

//--- a position without memory (restart on a new terminal / manual) -> manage it by its method and comment
void AdoptPosition(int tk)
  {
   if(!OrderSelect(tk, SELECT_BY_TICKET)) return;
   long key = OrderKey();
   int m = MethodOfMagic(OrderMagicNumber());
   int dir = (OrderType() == OP_BUY) ? 1 : -1;
   double open = OrderOpenPrice();
   if(PGet(key, "DIR", 0) == 0)
     {
      double sl = OrderStopLoss();
      double R = (sl > 0 && dir * (open - sl) > 0) ? MathAbs(open - sl) : ATR(PERIOD_H1, 1);
      if(R <= 0) R = 100 * Point;
      string cm = OrderComment();
      if(m == M_MEM)
        {
         int mtf = (StringFind(cm, " MEM H1") >= 0) ? 60 : 15;
         int pm = StringFind(cm, " MEM ");
         int ps = (pm >= 0) ? StringFind(cm, " S", pm + 4) : -1;
         int pe = (ps >= 0) ? StringFind(cm, " E", ps + 1) : -1;
         int sid = (ps >= 0) ? (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(cm, ps + 2, (pe > ps) ? pe - ps - 2 : 2)) : 0;
         int mex = (pe >= 0) ? (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(cm, pe + 2, 1)) : MEX_TREND;
         if(sid < 0 || sid >= MEM_STRATS) sid = 0;
         if(mex < MEX_TP1 || mex > MEX_TREND) mex = MEX_TREND;
         PSet(key, "DIR", dir);
         PSet(key, "LAY", LY_MEM);
         PSet(key, "FAM", sid);
         PSet(key, "EXP", EX_MEM);
         PSet(key, "MEX", mex);
         PSet(key, "MTF", mtf);
         PSet(key, "CELL", -1);
         PSet(key, "MAXM", MemMaxBars(mex) * mtf);
         PSet(key, "ATR", (mex == MEX_TREND) ? R / 1.5 : R);
         PSet(key, "EN", open);
         PSet(key, "SL0", (sl > 0 && dir * (open - sl) > 0) ? sl : open - dir * R);
         PSet(key, "CSL", (sl > 0) ? sl : open - dir * R);
         PSet(key, "R", R);
         PSet(key, "LOT0", OrderLots());
         PSet(key, "TIER", 0);
         PSet(key, "HK", -1);
         if(OrderTakeProfit() > 0) PSet(key, "TP", OrderTakeProfit());
         Print("[AK10] took over open memory trade #", tk, " (", g_msName[sid], ", ", MemExitName(mex), ")");
        }
      else
        {
         int ly = (m == M_BO) ? LY_H1X : ((m == M_TR) ? ((StringFind(cm, "TX4") >= 0) ? LY_T4 : LY_T1) : LY_H1);
         if(StringFind(cm, "PB15") >= 0) ly = LY_M15;
         else if(StringFind(cm, "PB5") >= 0) ly = LY_M5;
         else if(StringFind(cm, "BO15") >= 0) ly = LY_M15X;
         int exm = (m == M_TR) ? EX_TX : ((StringFind(cm, " Q") >= 0) ? EX_QUICK : EX_TREND);
         int fm = (m == M_BO) ? FM_BO20 : FM_TPB;
         int px = StringFind(cm, " X");
         if(m == M_TR && px >= 0) fm = 100 + (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(cm, px + 2, 2));
         if(m == M_TR) PSet(key, "MAXM", 96.0 * LayerTf(ly));
         if(m == M_TR) { }
         else if(StringFind(cm, "TPB50") >= 0) fm = FM_TPB50;
         else if(StringFind(cm, "MOM") >= 0) fm = FM_MOM;
         else if(StringFind(cm, "BO20") >= 0) fm = FM_BO20;
         else if(StringFind(cm, "TPB") >= 0) fm = FM_TPB;
         PSet(key, "DIR", dir);
         PSet(key, "LAY", ly);
         PSet(key, "FAM", fm);
         PSet(key, "EN", open);
         PSet(key, "SL0", (sl > 0 && dir * (open - sl) > 0) ? sl : open - dir * R);
         PSet(key, "CSL", (sl > 0) ? sl : open - dir * R);
         PSet(key, "R", R);
         PSet(key, "LOT0", OrderLots());
         PSet(key, "TIER", 0);
         PSet(key, "EXP", exm);
         PSet(key, "HK", -1);
         PSet(key, "PTB", (double)g_ptsBar);
         //--- a stop already at / beyond the entry means the first target was probably booked before
         PSet(key, "PRT", (sl > 0 && dir * (sl - open) >= 0) ? 2 : 0);
         if(exm == EX_QUICK) PSet(key, "TP", (OrderTakeProfit() > 0) ? OrderTakeProfit() : NormalizeDouble(open + dir * QuickTargetR * R, Digits));
         Print("[AK10] took over open trade #", tk, " (no memory found - managed as a ", MethodName(m), " ", LayerName(ly), " ", PlanName(exm), " trade)");
        }
     }
   //--- moves while the EA was off count for the stop-to-entry and the trailing stop
   double pk = PeakFromHistory(key, dir, open);
   if(pk > PGet(key, "PK", 0)) PSet(key, "PK", pk);
   TrackAdd(key);
  }

void ManagePositions()
  {
   int tks[];
   long keys[];
   int n = 0;
   ArrayResize(tks, OrdersTotal() + 1);
   ArrayResize(keys, OrdersTotal() + 1);
   for(int i = OrdersTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
     {
      if(!OrderSelect(i, SELECT_BY_POS, MODE_TRADES)) continue;
      if(!MyOrder()) continue;
      tks[n] = OrderTicket();
      keys[n] = OrderKey();
      n++;
     }
   for(int k = 0; k < n; k++)
     {
      if(!IsTracked(keys[k])) AdoptPosition(tks[k]);
      ManageOne(tks[k], keys[k]);
     }
   //--- tracked trades that are gone: closed by the stop / trailing stop / target / manually
   for(int q = g_pkN - 1; q >= 0; q--)
     {
      long key = g_pk[q];
      if(TicketOfKey(key) > 0) continue;
      if(OnPositionClosed(key)) TrackRemove(key);
     }
  }

//==================================================================
// CLOSE DETECTION, STATS, CSV
//==================================================================
string CloseText(int cr)
  {
   if(cr == CR_SL) return "Stop loss hit";
   if(cr == CR_TRAIL) return "Trailing stop hit in profit";
   if(cr == CR_NOLOSS) return "Closed at entry - no-loss stop";
   if(cr == CR_TIME) return "Time limit reached";
   if(cr == CR_TREND) return "H4 trend no longer agrees (after the minimum hold) - trade done";
   if(cr == CR_WEEKEND) return "Closed before the weekend";
   if(cr == CR_NEWS) return "Closed before big news";
   if(cr == CR_TP) return "Target +2R hit (QUICK plan)";
   if(cr == CR_POINTS) return "Trend points turned against the trade - closed";
   if(cr == CR_H4FLIP) return "H4 trend flipped - closed";
   if(cr == CR_TSTOP) return "Time stop - no follow-through";
   return "Closed manually / other";
  }

string CloseTextL(int cr, int ly)
  {
   if(ly == LY_MEM)
     {
      if(cr == CR_TP) return "Target hit (learnt memory exit)";
      if(cr == CR_TIME) return "Time limit of the memory exit reached";
      if(cr == CR_NOLOSS) return "Closed at entry - no-loss stop (memory trend ride)";
     }
   return CloseText(cr);
  }

//--- returns true when the closed trade was logged (false = history not loaded yet, try again)
bool OnPositionClosed(long key)
  {
   int dir = (int)PGet(key, "DIR", 0);
   int m = KeyMethod(key);
   datetime ot = KeyTime(key);
   double profit = 0, exitPx = 0;
   datetime ct = 0;
   int n = OrdersHistoryTotal();
   for(int i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--)
     {
      if(!OrderSelect(i, SELECT_BY_POS, MODE_HISTORY)) continue;
      if(!MyOrder() || MethodOfMagic(OrderMagicNumber()) != m) continue;
      if(MathAbs((double)(OrderOpenTime() - ot)) > 2) continue;
      profit += OrderProfit() + OrderSwap() + OrderCommission();
      if(OrderCloseTime() >= ct) { ct = OrderCloseTime(); exitPx = OrderClosePrice(); }
      if(dir == 0) dir = (OrderType() == OP_BUY) ? 1 : -1;
     }
   if(ct == 0)
     {
      datetime first = (datetime)PGet(key, "CHK", 0);
      if(first == 0) { PSet(key, "CHK", (double)TimeCurrent()); return false; }
      if(TimeCurrent() - first < 60) return false;
      ct = TimeCurrent();
     }
   if(dir == 0) dir = 1;
   double en = PGet(key, "EN", exitPx), R = PGet(key, "R", 0), lot0 = PGet(key, "LOT0", SymbolLot());
   int ly = (int)PGet(key, "LAY", LY_H1);
   int fm = (int)PGet(key, "FAM", FM_TPB);
   int cr = (int)PGet(key, "CR", CR_NONE);
   int exm = (int)PGet(key, "EXP", EX_TREND);
   int hk = (int)PGet(key, "HK", -1);
   int mcell = (int)PGet(key, "CELL", -1), mmex = (int)PGet(key, "MEX", MEX_TREND), mtfm = (int)PGet(key, "MTF", 15);
   if(cr == CR_NONE)
     {
      double csl = PGet(key, "CSL", PGet(key, "SL0", 0));
      double tpx = PGet(key, "TP", 0);
      double tol = MathMax(5 * Point, 0.1 * R);
      bool trl = (PGet(key, "TRL", 0) > 0 || PGet(key, "PTT", 0) > 0);
      if(tpx > 0 && (MathAbs(exitPx - tpx) <= tol || dir * (exitPx - tpx) > 0)) cr = CR_TP;                       // at the target (or a gap beyond it)
      else if(csl > 0 && (MathAbs(exitPx - csl) <= tol || dir * (exitPx - csl) < 0))                          // at the stop (or a gap through it)
         cr = (dir * (csl - en) > 0) ? (trl ? CR_TRAIL : CR_NOLOSS) : CR_SL;
      else cr = CR_MANUAL;
     }
   double riskUSD = R * ValuePerPrice(lot0);
   double resR = (riskUSD > 0) ? profit / riskUSD : 0;
   double peakR = (R > 0) ? PGet(key, "PK", 0) / R : 0, worstR = (R > 0) ? PGet(key, "WR", 0) / R : 0;
   int tmax = (int)PGet(key, "TIER", 0);
   double prtLot = ((int)PGet(key, "PRT", 0) == 1) ? PGet(key, "PRTLOT", 0) : 0;
   //--- daily stats (per symbol and method) + scoreboard per method (all time)
   string tag = MethodTag(m);
   string pre = g_SP + DateKey() + "_" + _Symbol;
   GVSet(pre + "_N", GVGet(pre + "_N", 0) + 1);
   if(profit > 0) GVSet(pre + "_W", GVGet(pre + "_W", 0) + 1);
   else GVSet(pre + "_L", GVGet(pre + "_L", 0) + 1);
   DayPLAll();                                                         // makes sure today's account total exists (before this trade)
   GVSet(pre + "_PL", GVGet(pre + "_PL", 0) + profit);
   GVAdd(DayPLName(), profit);
   GVSet(pre + "_PL" + tag, GVGet(pre + "_PL" + tag, 0) + profit);
   string sb = g_SP + "SB_" + _Symbol + "_" + tag;
   GVSet(sb + "_N", GVGet(sb + "_N", 0) + 1);
   if(profit > 0) GVSet(sb + "_W", GVGet(sb + "_W", 0) + 1);
   GVSet(sb + "_PL", GVGet(sb + "_PL", 0) + profit);
   GVSet(sb + "_R", GVGet(sb + "_R", 0) + resR);
   LossStreakAdd(resR);
   string plan = (ly == LY_MEM) ? "MEMORY " + ((mtfm == 15) ? "M15 " : "H1 ") + MemExitName(mmex) : PlanName(exm);
   string hdr = "Date,Symbol,Side,Method,Layer,Setup,Lots,OpenTimeIST,Entry,StopLoss,RiskUSD,CloseTimeIST,Exit,ProfitUSD,ResultR,PeakR,WorstR,MaxTier,PartialLots,CloseReason," +
                "TrendD1,TrendH4,SpreadPctSL,HoursHeld,VolRank,Plan,HistKey,PtsBuy,PtsSell,MemCell";
   string setupTxt = (ly == LY_MEM) ? ((fm >= 0 && fm < MEM_STRATS) ? g_msName[fm] : "memory setup") + ((mtfm == 15) ? " M15" : " H1") : FamName(fm);
   string line = DateKey() + "," + _Symbol + "," + SideName(dir) + "," + MethodName(m) + "," + LayerName(ly) + "," + CsvSafe(setupTxt) + "," + DoubleToString(lot0, 2) + "," +
                 IstStamp(ot) + "," + Px(en) + "," + Px(PGet(key, "SL0", 0)) + "," + DoubleToString(riskUSD, 2) + "," + IstStamp(ct) + "," + Px(exitPx) + "," +
                 DoubleToString(profit, 2) + "," + DoubleToString(resR, 2) + "," + DoubleToString(peakR, 2) + "," + DoubleToString(worstR, 2) + "," +
                 IntegerToString(tmax) + "," + DoubleToString(prtLot, 2) + "," + CsvSafe(CloseTextL(cr, ly)) + "," + TrendWord((int)PGet(key, "TD1", 0)) + "," + TrendWord((int)PGet(key, "TH4", 0)) + "," +
                 DoubleToString(PGet(key, "SPP", 0), 1) + "," + DoubleToString((double)(ct - ot) / 3600.0, 1) + "," + DoubleToString(PGet(key, "VRK", -1), 2) + "," +
                 plan + "," + IntegerToString(hk) + "," + IntegerToString((int)PGet(key, "PB", 0)) + "," + IntegerToString((int)PGet(key, "PS", 0)) + "," + IntegerToString(mcell);
   CsvLine("AK10_trades_" + DateKey() + "_" + _Symbol + ".csv", hdr, line);
   NotifyClose(key, dir, en, exitPx, profit, resR, cr, tmax, (double)(ct - ot) / 3600.0);
   InstaEvent("close", key, exitPx, resR, ProCloseReason(cr));
   Print("[AK10] CLOSED ", MethodName(m), " ", SideName(dir), " ", _Symbol, " ", SetupName(ly, fm), " P/L ", DoubleToString(profit, 2), " (", DoubleToString(resR, 2), "R) - ", CloseTextL(cr, ly));
   if(ly == LY_MEM && mcell >= 0) MemLiveAdd(mcell, resR, true);         // live learning: the memory setup's record gets this result
   if(hk >= 0) HistLiveAdd(hk, ToIST(ot), dir, resR);                    // live history of the situation
   SetLastExit(IstStamp(ct) + " IST | " + MethodName(m) + " | " + SideName(dir) + " | " + ((ly == LY_MEM) ? setupTxt : SetupName(ly, fm)) + " | " + CloseTextL(cr, ly) + " | " + Usd(profit) + " USD (" + Rtx(resR) + ")");
   PanelAddExit(dir, m, resR, profit, ct);
   DrawClosedBox(key, dir, en, exitPx, ct, resR);
   SendClosePhoto(key, dir, exitPx, resR, PGet(key, "VIP", 0) > 0, PGet(key, "PUB", 0) > 0);
   PClear(key);
   g_status = "trade closed: " + MethodName(m) + " - " + CloseTextL(cr, ly) + " (" + Usd(profit) + " USD)";
   return true;
  }

//--- on start: trades that closed while the EA was off are logged; open ones are taken over
void ResumeAfterRestart()
  {
   g_pkN = 0;
   for(int i = OrdersTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
     {
      if(!OrderSelect(i, SELECT_BY_POS, MODE_TRADES)) continue;
      if(!MyOrder()) continue;
      AdoptPosition(OrderTicket());
     }
   long gone[];
   int ng = 0;
   string pre = g_G + "P";
   int n = GlobalVariablesTotal();
   for(int k = 0; k < n; k++)
     {
      string nm = GlobalVariableName(k);
      if(StringFind(nm, pre) != 0) continue;
      int us = StringFind(nm, "_DIR");
      if(us < 0 || us != StringLen(nm) - 4) continue;
      long key = StringToInteger(StringSubstr(nm, StringLen(pre), us - StringLen(pre)));
      if(key <= 0 || IsTracked(key)) continue;
      ArrayResize(gone, ng + 1);
      gone[ng] = key;
      ng++;
     }
   for(int q = 0; q < ng; q++) TrackAdd(gone[q]);                      // closed while off: logged by the next ManagePositions pass
  }


//==================================================================
// TREND POINTS (every completed H1 candle): 0-10 for each side
//   D1 trend 2, H4 trend 2, H1 EMA20/50 1, D1 EMA200 side 1, MACD(12,26,9) 1, RSI(14) 1, M15 EMA20/50 1, 3-candle momentum 1
//==================================================================
void PointsUpdate()
  {
   datetime b = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, 0);
   if(b == 0 || b == g_ptsBar) return;
   if(iBars(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1) < 60 || iBars(_Symbol, PERIOD_D1) < 60 || iBars(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4) < 60) return;
   g_ptsBar = b;
   int B = 0, S = 0;
   int d1 = TrendOf(PERIOD_D1), h4 = TrendOf(PERIOD_H4);
   if(d1 > 0) B += 2;
   else if(d1 < 0) S += 2;
   if(h4 > 0) B += 2;
   else if(h4 < 0) S += 2;
   double e20 = EMAv(PERIOD_H1, 20, 1), e50 = EMAv(PERIOD_H1, 50, 1);
   if(e20 > e50) B++;
   else if(e20 < e50) S++;
   double c1 = Cl(PERIOD_D1, 1), e200 = EMAv(PERIOD_D1, 200, 1);
   if(e200 > 0 && c1 > e200) B++;
   else if(e200 > 0 && c1 < e200) S++;
   double mm = iMACD(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, 12, 26, 9, PRICE_CLOSE, MODE_MAIN, 1), ms = iMACD(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, 12, 26, 9, PRICE_CLOSE, MODE_SIGNAL, 1);
   if(mm > ms) B++;
   else if(mm < ms) S++;
   double r = iRSI(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, 14, PRICE_CLOSE, 1);
   if(r > 50) B++;
   else if(r < 50) S++;
   double f20 = EMAv(PERIOD_M15, 20, 1), f50 = EMAv(PERIOD_M15, 50, 1);
   if(f20 > f50) B++;
   else if(f20 < f50) S++;
   double cm = Cl(PERIOD_H1, 1), cp = Cl(PERIOD_H1, 4);
   if(cm > cp) B++;
   else if(cm < cp) S++;
   g_ptsB = B;
   g_ptsS = S;
  }

string PointsText()
  {
   if(g_ptsBar == 0) return "waiting for the first H1 close";
   int lead = g_ptsB - g_ptsS;
   string w = (lead >= PointsFlipMargin) ? "buyers lead" : ((-lead >= PointsFlipMargin) ? "sellers lead" : "balanced");
   return "BUY " + IntegerToString(g_ptsB) + " / SELL " + IntegerToString(g_ptsS) + " (" + w + ", H1 " + IstClock(g_ptsBar) + " IST)";
  }

//==================================================================
// HISTORY - the same setup in the same situation in the past (5-year table built into the EA + the live record)
//   key = (((setup x 2 + H4 agrees) x 2 + with the D1 EMA200) x 3 + volatility band) x 5 + session; the side is NOT in the key,
//   so the list shows which side was traded each time
//==================================================================
int HistSetup(int ly, int fm)
  {
   if(ly == LY_H1) return 0;
   if(ly == LY_M15) return (fm == FM_TPB50) ? 2 : 1;
   if(ly == LY_M5) return 3;
   if(ly == LY_H1X) return (fm == FM_TPB50) ? 4 : ((fm == FM_MOM) ? 5 : 6);
   if(ly == LY_M15X) return (fm == FM_MOM) ? 7 : 8;
   if((ly == LY_T1 || ly == LY_T4) && fm > 100 && fm <= 100 + TX_N) return 8 + (fm - 100);
   return -1;
  }

string HistSetupName(int hs)
  {
   switch(hs)
     {
      case 0: return "Pullback H1 EMA20";
      case 1: return "Pullback M15 EMA20";
      case 2: return "Pullback M15 EMA50";
      case 3: return "Pullback M5 EMA20";
      case 4: return "Breakout H1 deep pull-back EMA50";
      case 5: return "Breakout H1 momentum candle";
      case 6: return "Breakout H1 20-candle breakout";
      case 7: return "Breakout M15 momentum candle";
      case 8: return "Breakout M15 20-candle breakout";
     }
   if(hs >= 9 && hs <= 8 + TX_N) return "Trend " + TxName(hs - 8);
   return "setup";
  }

int HistKeyNow(int hs, int dir)
  {
   if(hs < 0 || hs >= HS_N || dir == 0) return -1;
   int h4a = TfAgrees(PERIOD_H4, dir) ? 1 : 0;
   int big = WithBigTrend(dir) ? 1 : 0;
   int vb = VolBand(), ss = MemSession();
   return (((hs * 2 + h4a) * 2 + big) * 3 + vb) * 5 + ss;
  }

string HistSitText(int key)
  {
   if(key < 0) return "-";
   int ss = key % 5, vb = (key / 5) % 3, big = (key / 15) % 2, h4a = (key / 30) % 2, hs = key / 60;
   return HistSetupName(hs) + " | H4 " + ((h4a > 0) ? "agrees" : "not aligned") + " | " + ((big > 0) ? "with" : "against") + " the D1 EMA200 | " + BandName(vb) + " | " + MemSessionName(ss);
  }

//--- symbol class of the history table: 0 gold, 1 BTC, 2 ETH, 3 EURUSD, -1 none
int HistSymClass()
  {
   int c = MemSymClass();
   return (c >= 0 && c <= 4) ? c : -1;                                 // 0 gold, 1 BTC, 2 ETH, 3 EURUSD, 4 USOIL
  }

int HistRow(int key)
  {
   int c = HistSymClass();
   if(c < 0 || key < 0) return -1;
   for(int i = 0; i < HIST_N; i++) if(g_hCls[i] == c && g_hKey[i] == key) return i;
   return -1;
  }

string HistGV(int key, string f) { return g_SP + "H_" + _Symbol + "_" + IntegerToString(key) + "_" + f; }

//--- live record: a closed trade of this situation (open time in IST, side, result in R)
void HistLiveAdd(int key, datetime ist, int dir, double r)
  {
   if(key < 0) return;
   GVSet(HistGV(key, "N"), GVGet(HistGV(key, "N"), 0) + 1);
   if(r > 0) GVSet(HistGV(key, "W"), GVGet(HistGV(key, "W"), 0) + 1);
   GVSet(HistGV(key, "R"), GVGet(HistGV(key, "R"), 0) + r);
   for(int k = 5; k >= 2; k--)
     {
      string a = IntegerToString(k), b = IntegerToString(k - 1);
      GVSet(HistGV(key, "T" + a), GVGet(HistGV(key, "T" + b), 0));
      GVSet(HistGV(key, "S" + a), GVGet(HistGV(key, "S" + b), 0));
      GVSet(HistGV(key, "Q" + a), GVGet(HistGV(key, "Q" + b), 0));
     }
   GVSet(HistGV(key, "T1"), (double)ist);
   GVSet(HistGV(key, "S1"), dir);
   GVSet(HistGV(key, "Q1"), r);
  }

//--- count, wins and sum of R: 5-year table + live record
void HistStats(int key, int &n, int &w, double &sum)
  {
   n = 0;
   w = 0;
   sum = 0;
   if(key < 0) return;
   int row = HistRow(key);
   if(row >= 0) { n = g_hN[row]; w = g_hW[row]; sum = g_hSum[row]; }
   n += (int)GVGet(HistGV(key, "N"), 0);
   w += (int)GVGet(HistGV(key, "W"), 0);
   sum += GVGet(HistGV(key, "R"), 0);
  }

string HistVerdict(int n, double avg)
  {
   if(n <= 0) return "no history";
   if(n < HistoryMinTrades) return "INFO (only " + IntegerToString(n) + " cases)";
   return (avg >= HistoryMinAvgR) ? "TAKE" : "SKIP";
  }

string HistLine(int key)
  {
   if(key < 0) return "-";
   int n = 0, w = 0;
   double sum = 0;
   HistStats(key, n, w, sum);
   if(n <= 0) return "not seen before (5 years + live)";
   double avg = sum / n;
   return "seen " + IntegerToString(n) + " times, won " + DoubleToString(100.0 * w / n, 0) + "%, avg " + Rtx(avg) + " -> " + HistVerdict(n, avg);
  }

//--- one past occurrence as text
string HistItem(datetime ist, int side, double r)
  {
   return TimeToString(ist, TIME_DATE | TIME_MINUTES) + " " + SideName(side) + " " + Rtx(r);
  }

//--- the last k occurrences: live record first (newest), then the 5-year table
string HistLastList(int key, int k)
  {
   if(key < 0 || k <= 0) return "";
   string out = "";
   int shown = 0;
   for(int i = 1; i <= 5 && shown < k; i++)
     {
      datetime t = (datetime)GVGet(HistGV(key, "T" + IntegerToString(i)), 0);
      if(t <= 0) break;
      out += ((shown > 0) ? " | " : "") + HistItem(t, (int)GVGet(HistGV(key, "S" + IntegerToString(i)), 1), GVGet(HistGV(key, "Q" + IntegerToString(i)), 0)) + " (live)";
      shown++;
     }
   int row = HistRow(key);
   if(row >= 0)
     {
      for(int j = 1; j <= 5 && shown < k; j++)
        {
         int tt = (j == 1) ? g_hT1[row] : ((j == 2) ? g_hT2[row] : ((j == 3) ? g_hT3[row] : ((j == 4) ? g_hT4[row] : g_hT5[row])));
         int sd = (j == 1) ? g_hS1[row] : ((j == 2) ? g_hS2[row] : ((j == 3) ? g_hS3[row] : ((j == 4) ? g_hS4[row] : g_hS5[row])));
         double rr = (j == 1) ? g_hR1[row] : ((j == 2) ? g_hR2[row] : ((j == 3) ? g_hR3[row] : ((j == 4) ? g_hR4[row] : g_hR5[row])));
         if(tt <= 0) break;
         out += ((shown > 0) ? " | " : "") + HistItem((datetime)tt, sd, rr);
         shown++;
        }
     }
   return out;
  }

//--- FOLLOW mode: enough past trades and a poor average -> skip
bool HistSaysSkip(int key, string &txt)
  {
   txt = "";
   if(key < 0) return false;
   int n = 0, w = 0;
   double sum = 0;
   HistStats(key, n, w, sum);
   if(n < HistoryMinTrades || n <= 0) return false;
   if(sum / n >= HistoryMinAvgR) return false;
   txt = HistSitText(key) + ": " + HistLine(key);
   return true;
  }

//==================================================================
// LAST TRADE / LAST EXIT (panel; kept in a small file so a restart still shows them)
//==================================================================
string LastFile() { return "AK10_last_" + _Symbol + ".txt"; }

void SaveLast()
  {
   if(IsTesting()) return;
   int h = FileOpen(LastFile(), FILE_WRITE | FILE_TXT | FILE_ANSI);
   if(h == INVALID_HANDLE) return;
   FileWriteString(h, g_lastTradeTxt + "\r\n" + g_lastExitTxt + "\r\n");
   for(int i = 0; i < 3; i++) FileWriteString(h, IntegerToString(g_lxR[i]) + "|" + g_lx[i] + "\r\n");
   FileClose(h);
  }

void LoadLast()
  {
   if(IsTesting()) return;
   int h = FileOpen(LastFile(), FILE_READ | FILE_TXT | FILE_ANSI | FILE_SHARE_READ);
   if(h == INVALID_HANDLE) return;
   if(!FileIsEnding(h)) g_lastTradeTxt = FileReadString(h);
   if(!FileIsEnding(h)) g_lastExitTxt = FileReadString(h);
   for(int i = 0; i < 3 && !FileIsEnding(h); i++)
     {
      string ln = FileReadString(h);
      int p = StringFind(ln, "|");
      if(p < 0) continue;
      g_lxR[i] = (int)StringToInteger(StringSubstr(ln, 0, p));
      g_lx[i] = StringSubstr(ln, p + 1);
     }
   FileClose(h);
  }

void SetLastTrade(string s) { g_lastTradeTxt = s; SaveLast(); }
void SetLastExit(string s) { g_lastExitTxt = s; SaveLast(); }

//==================================================================
// PROFESSIONAL WORDING for the VIP / public channels (no robot words: no EA, method, layer, tier, R)
//==================================================================
string ProTf(int tf)
  {
   if(tf == PERIOD_M5) return "5-minute";
   if(tf == PERIOD_M15) return "15-minute";
   if(tf == PERIOD_H4) return "4-hour";
   if(tf == PERIOD_D1) return "daily";
   return "1-hour";
  }

string ProSetupTitle(int ly, int fm, int d)
  {
   if(ly == LY_MEM) return ProMemTitle(fm, d);
   if(fm > 100) return ProTxTitle(fm - 100, d);
   if(fm == FM_TPB50) return "Deep pullback";
   if(fm == FM_MOM) return "Momentum continuation";
   if(fm == FM_BO20) return (d > 0) ? "Breakout" : "Breakdown";
   return "Trend pullback";
  }

string ProMemTitle(int sid, int d)
  {
   bool b = (d > 0);
   switch(sid)
     {
      case 0: return "Exhaustion reversal";
      case 1: return b ? "Break and retest" : "Breakdown and retest";
      case 2: return "Overextension reversal";
      case 3: return "Trend bounce at the 20 EMA";
      case 4: return "Fair value gap retest";
      case 5: return "Big-candle reversal";
      case 6: return "Stretch reversal";
      case 7: return b ? "Pivot support bounce" : "Pivot resistance rejection";
      case 8: return b ? "Buying the dip in the trend" : "Selling the rally in the trend";
      case 9: return "Round-number rejection";
      case 10: return "Stochastic reversal";
      case 11: return b ? "Three white soldiers" : "Three black crows";
      case 12: return "Mean reversion to the 50 EMA";
      case 13: return b ? "Bullish engulfing" : "Bearish engulfing";
      case 14: return b ? "Sweep of the previous day's low" : "Sweep of the previous day's high";
      case 15: return b ? "Round-number breakout" : "Round-number breakdown";
     }
   return "Price-action setup";
  }

//--- why the trade was taken, in plain trader language
string ProIdea(int ly, int fm, int d)
  {
   if(fm > 100) return ProTxIdea(fm - 100, d);
   bool b = (d > 0);
   string tf = ProTf(LayerTf(ly));
   string t = _Symbol + " is in " + (b ? "an uptrend" : "a downtrend") + " on the daily and 4-hour charts. ";
   if(fm == FM_TPB)
      t += "Price pulled back to the 20-period moving average on the " + tf + " chart and the last candle closed strongly " + (b ? "above the previous high" : "below the previous low") +
           " - " + (b ? "buyers" : "sellers") + " are stepping back in, so we join the trend.";
   else if(fm == FM_TPB50)
      t += "Price pulled back deeper, to the 50-period moving average on the " + tf + " chart, and " + (b ? "buyers" : "sellers") + " defended it with a strong close - the trend is resuming.";
   else if(fm == FM_MOM)
      t += "A strong " + tf + " candle with a large body just closed " + (b ? "above" : "below") + " the last 10 candles - momentum is clearly with the trend.";
   else
      t += "Price closed " + (b ? "above the 20-candle high" : "below the 20-candle low") + " on the " + tf + " chart with a strong candle - a fresh " + (b ? "breakout" : "breakdown") + " in the direction of the trend.";
   return t;
  }

string ProMemIdea(int sid, int d, int tfm)
  {
   bool b = (d > 0);
   string tf = (tfm == 15) ? "15-minute" : "1-hour";
   string t = "";
   switch(sid)
     {
      case 0: t = "Price moved " + (b ? "down" : "up") + " three candles in a row on the " + tf + " chart and the move looks exhausted - we expect a turn."; break;
      case 1: t = "Price broke the last swing " + (b ? "high" : "low") + " and came back to retest it - the level held."; break;
      case 2: t = "Price is stretched far " + (b ? "below" : "above") + " its average on the " + tf + " chart - a snap-back is likely."; break;
      case 3: t = "In a strong trend price touched the 20 EMA on the " + tf + " chart and " + (b ? "buyers" : "sellers") + " defended it."; break;
      case 4: t = "Price came back into a fresh fair value gap and " + (b ? "buyers" : "sellers") + " stepped in."; break;
      case 5: t = "A very large " + tf + " candle closed at its " + (b ? "low" : "high") + " - such extremes often reverse."; break;
      case 6: t = "Price is stretched outside its normal range (Keltner channel) - a move back toward the middle is expected."; break;
      case 7: t = "Price tested today's pivot " + (b ? "support" : "resistance") + " and was rejected."; break;
      case 8: t = "In the " + (b ? "uptrend" : "downtrend") + " a short " + (b ? "dip" : "rally") + " just ended - a good spot to join the trend."; break;
      case 9: t = "Price tested a round number and was rejected with a clear close."; break;
      case 10: t = "The stochastic turned from the " + (b ? "oversold" : "overbought") + " zone on the " + tf + " chart."; break;
      case 11: t = "Three strong " + (b ? "bullish" : "bearish") + " candles in a row on the " + tf + " chart show clear " + (b ? "buying" : "selling") + " pressure."; break;
      case 12: t = "Price is far " + (b ? "below" : "above") + " its 50 EMA on the " + tf + " chart - a move back toward it is likely."; break;
      case 13: t = "A " + (b ? "bullish" : "bearish") + " engulfing candle printed on the " + tf + " chart - " + (b ? "buyers" : "sellers") + " took control."; break;
      case 14: t = "Price swept the previous day's " + (b ? "low" : "high") + ", took the stops there and closed back " + (b ? "above" : "below") + " it."; break;
      case 15: t = "Price closed through a round number on the " + tf + " chart - the next round level is the magnet."; break;
      default: t = "A clear price-action signal printed on the " + tf + " chart."; break;
     }
   return t + " In our records this setup worked in this exact market condition across many years.";
  }

//--- pips / points of a price move in the usual unit of the market
string MoveText(double mv)
  {
   string sg = (mv >= 0) ? "+" : "-";
   double a = MathAbs(mv);
   if(IsCrypto()) return sg + "$" + DoubleToString(a, 0) + " move";
   double pip = (g_cls == SC_GOLD) ? 0.1 : ((Digits == 3 || Digits == 5) ? Point * 10 : Point);
   if(pip <= 0) pip = Point;
   string v = DoubleToString(a / pip, 0);
   return sg + v + ((v == "1") ? " pip" : " pips");
  }

string ProCloseReason(int cr)
  {
   if(cr == CR_SL) return "Stop loss hit - the market did not follow through this time.";
   if(cr == CR_TRAIL) return "Trailing stop hit - profit locked in.";
   if(cr == CR_NOLOSS) return "Price came back to the entry - closed without a loss.";
   if(cr == CR_TIME) return "The move took too long - closed on time.";
   if(cr == CR_TREND) return "The 4-hour trend is no longer supporting the trade.";
   if(cr == CR_WEEKEND) return "Closed before the weekend.";
   if(cr == CR_NEWS) return "Closed before high-impact news.";
   if(cr == CR_TP) return "Final target reached.";
   if(cr == CR_POINTS) return "Momentum turned against the trade - closed early to protect the account.";
   if(cr == CR_H4FLIP) return "The 4-hour trend turned against the trade - closed early.";
   return "Closed manually.";
  }

string ProResult(double r)
  {
   if(r > 0.05) return "<b>profit</b> - " + DoubleToString(r, 1) + " times the risk";
   if(r < -0.05) return "<b>loss</b> - " + DoubleToString(-r, 1) + " times the risk";
   return "<b>break-even</b>";
  }


//==================================================================
// CHART DRAWINGS (v10.3) - clean: labels never sit on top of each other (a label manager moves them to a free row),
// skipped signals are small grey dots (the reason is in the tooltip), only the patterns that matter NOW are drawn and they
// delete themselves after a few candles. Parts: EMA20 / EMA50, yesterday's high / low, H1 20-candle high / low, signals,
// position boxes, closed-trade boxes, candle patterns (engulfing, hammer / shooting star, inside bar, morning / evening star),
// auto trendlines (deleted when broken), H4 W / M with neckline. Every part has its own switch.
//==================================================================
int      g_sgN = 0;                 // signal marks drawn (the last 15 stay)
datetime g_skipT[2];                // last skipped-signal dot per side (one dot per side every 3 candles)
int      g_cbN = 0;                 // closed-trade boxes drawn (the last 6 stay)
datetime g_emaBar = 0;
datetime g_wmBar = 0;
datetime g_ptBar = 0;
datetime g_tlBar = 0;

bool DrawOK() { return !(IsTesting() && !IsVisualMode()); }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// basic objects
//------------------------------------------------------------------
void ObjSegment(string nm, datetime t1, double p1, datetime t2, double p2, color c, int width, int style)
  {
   if(ObjectFind(0, nm) < 0)
     {
      ObjectCreate(0, nm, OBJ_TREND, 0, t1, p1, t2, p2);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_RAY, false);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, 0);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_HIDDEN, 1);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_BACK, 1);
     }
   ObjectMove(0, nm, 0, t1, p1);
   ObjectMove(0, nm, 1, t2, p2);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_COLOR, c);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_WIDTH, width);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_STYLE, style);
  }

void ObjBox(string nm, datetime t1, double p1, datetime t2, double p2, color c)
  {
   if(ObjectFind(0, nm) < 0)
     {
      ObjectCreate(0, nm, OBJ_RECTANGLE, 0, t1, p1, t2, p2);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, 0);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_HIDDEN, 1);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_BACK, 1);
     }
   ObjectMove(0, nm, 0, t1, p1);
   ObjectMove(0, nm, 1, t2, p2);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_COLOR, c);
  }

void ObjText(string nm, datetime t, double p, string txt, color c, int size, int anchor)
  {
   if(ObjectFind(0, nm) < 0)
     {
      ObjectCreate(0, nm, OBJ_TEXT, 0, t, p);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, 0);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_HIDDEN, 1);
      ObjectSetString(0, nm, OBJPROP_FONT, "Segoe UI");
     }
   ObjectMove(0, nm, 0, t, p);
   ObjectSetString(0, nm, OBJPROP_TEXT, txt);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_COLOR, c);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, size);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, anchor);
  }

void ObjArrow(string nm, datetime t, double p, int code, color c, int width, string tip)
  {
   if(ObjectFind(0, nm) < 0)
     {
      ObjectCreate(0, nm, OBJ_ARROW, 0, t, p);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, 0);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_HIDDEN, 1);
     }
   ObjectMove(0, nm, 0, t, p);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, code);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_COLOR, c);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, nm, OBJPROP_WIDTH, width);
   if(tip != "") ObjectSetString(0, nm, OBJPROP_TOOLTIP, tip);
  }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// LABEL MANAGER: the right-hand labels (levels, entry / stop / target, EMA names) get rows at least one text height apart
//------------------------------------------------------------------
double g_lbP[];
int    g_lbN = 0;
double g_lbGap = 0;
double g_lbPpx = 0;                 // price per pixel of the chart photo while it is taken (0 = the window)

void LabelsReset()
  {
   g_lbN = 0;
   ArrayResize(g_lbP, 0);
   if(g_lbPpx > 0) { g_lbGap = 14.0 * g_lbPpx; return; }
   long hpx = ChartGetInteger(0, CHART_HEIGHT_IN_PIXELS, 0);
   double pmax = ChartGetDouble(0, CHART_PRICE_MAX, 0), pmin = ChartGetDouble(0, CHART_PRICE_MIN, 0);
   double ppx = 0;                                                      // price per pixel of the window
   if(hpx > 50 && pmax > pmin) ppx = (pmax - pmin) / (double)hpx;
   if(SendChartPhoto && ChartPhotoWidth > 50 && ChartPhotoHeight > 50)
     {
      //--- the chart photo has its own size (more or fewer candles, other height): take the larger price per pixel,
      //--- so the labels stay apart in the window AND in the photo
      int tf = Period();
      int sc = (int)ChartGetInteger(0, CHART_SCALE, 0);
      if(sc < 0) sc = 0;
      if(sc > 5) sc = 5;
      int nb = ChartPhotoWidth / (1 << sc);
      if(nb > iBars(_Symbol, tf) - 1) nb = iBars(_Symbol, tf) - 1;
      if(nb >= 10)
        {
         int kh = iHighest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_HIGH, nb, 0), kl = iLowest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_LOW, nb, 0);
         if(kh >= 0 && kl >= 0)
           {
            double r = iHigh(_Symbol, tf, kh) - iLow(_Symbol, tf, kl);
            if(r > 0) ppx = MathMax(ppx, 1.1 * r / (double)ChartPhotoHeight);
           }
        }
     }
   if(ppx > 0) g_lbGap = 14.0 * ppx;                                    // 14 pixels in price
   else g_lbGap = 0.35 * ATR(Period(), 1);
   if(g_lbGap <= 0) g_lbGap = 10 * Point;
  }

//--- the nearest free row to price p (tries above / below alternately)
double LabelRow(double p)
  {
   double best = p;
   for(int k = 0; k < 40; k++)
     {
      double cand = p + ((k % 2 == 0) ? 1 : -1) * ((k + 1) / 2) * g_lbGap;
      bool free = true;
      for(int i = 0; i < g_lbN; i++) if(MathAbs(g_lbP[i] - cand) < g_lbGap * 0.95) { free = false; break; }
      if(free) { best = cand; break; }
     }
   ArrayResize(g_lbP, g_lbN + 1);
   g_lbP[g_lbN] = best;
   g_lbN++;
   return best;
  }

datetime RightTime(int bars) { return iTime(_Symbol, Period(), 0) + Period() * 60 * bars; }

//--- SMALL TEXTS next to the candles (signal tags, pattern names, closed-trade results). Each one remembers its home price and
//--- the side it may move to; on every redraw (and for the chart photo, at the photo's own scale) they are spread out so no two
//--- are closer than one text height within 10 candles. They end at their candle (right anchor), so they never run into the
//--- label column on the right.
string   g_stN[30];
double   g_stH[30];
int      g_stA[30];
int      g_stC = 0;

void SmallTextAdd(string nm, double home, int away)
  {
   for(int i = 0; i < g_stC; i++) if(g_stN[i] == nm) { g_stH[i] = home; g_stA[i] = away; return; }
   //--- forget the texts that are gone from the chart (old marks are deleted: 15 signals, 6 results, 3 patterns stay)
   int w = 0;
   for(int r = 0; r < g_stC; r++)
     {
      if(ObjectFind(0, g_stN[r]) < 0) continue;
      g_stN[w] = g_stN[r];
      g_stH[w] = g_stH[r];
      g_stA[w] = g_stA[r];
      w++;
     }
   g_stC = w;
   if(g_stC >= 30)
     {
      for(int k = 1; k < g_stC; k++) { g_stN[k - 1] = g_stN[k]; g_stH[k - 1] = g_stH[k]; g_stA[k - 1] = g_stA[k]; }
      g_stC--;
     }
   g_stN[g_stC] = nm;
   g_stH[g_stC] = home;
   g_stA[g_stC] = away;
   g_stC++;
  }

void SmallTextsLayout()
  {
   int tf = Period();
   double gap = MathMax(g_lbGap, 0.3 * ATR(tf, 1));
   if(gap <= 0) return;
   int idx[30];
   datetime tm[30];
   int bs[30];
   double pp[30];
   ArrayInitialize(idx, 0);
   ArrayInitialize(tm, 0);
   ArrayInitialize(bs, 0);
   ArrayInitialize(pp, 0.0);
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < g_stC; i++)
     {
      if(ObjectFind(0, g_stN[i]) < 0) continue;                         // an old mark that was deleted
      idx[n] = i;
      tm[n] = (datetime)ObjectGetInteger(0, g_stN[i], OBJPROP_TIME, 0);
      n++;
     }
   for(int a = 1; a < n; a++)                                           // oldest first
     {
      int ki = idx[a];
      datetime kt = tm[a];
      int b = a - 1;
      while(b >= 0 && tm[b] > kt) { idx[b + 1] = idx[b]; tm[b + 1] = tm[b]; b--; }
      idx[b + 1] = ki;
      tm[b + 1] = kt;
     }
   for(int q = 0; q < n; q++)
     {
      int j = idx[q];
      bs[q] = iBarShift(_Symbol, tf, tm[q]);                            // distance in candles (weekend gaps!)
      double p = g_stH[j];
      for(int k = 0; k < 12; k++)
        {
         bool clash = false;
         for(int r = 0; r < q && !clash; r++) if(MathAbs(bs[r] - bs[q]) <= 10 && MathAbs(pp[r] - p) < gap) clash = true;
         if(!clash) break;
         p += g_stA[j] * gap;
        }
      pp[q] = p;
      ObjectMove(0, g_stN[j], 0, tm[q], p);
     }
  }

//--- plain word for a method on the chart (the photo goes to the channels: no robot words)
string ChartSetupWord(int m) { return (m == M_BO) ? "breakout" : ((m == M_TR) ? "trend" : ((m == M_PB) ? "pullback" : "setup")); }

//--- a right-hand label in a free row, with a thin leader line when it had to move away from its price
void RightLabel(string nm, double price, string txt, color c)
  {
   double row = LabelRow(price);
   ObjText(nm, RightTime(3), row, txt, c, 7, ANCHOR_LEFT);
   if(MathAbs(row - price) > g_lbGap * 0.3) ObjSegment(nm + "_l", RightTime(1), price, RightTime(3), row, c, 1, STYLE_DOT);
   else if(ObjectFind(0, nm + "_l") >= 0) ObjectDelete(0, nm + "_l");
  }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// EMA20 / EMA50 of the chart timeframe
//------------------------------------------------------------------
void DrawEmaLines()
  {
   if(!DrawEMAs) return;
   int tf = Period();
   datetime b = iTime(_Symbol, tf, 0);
   int n = (DrawEMABars < 20) ? 20 : DrawEMABars;
   if(n > iBars(_Symbol, tf) - 2) n = iBars(_Symbol, tf) - 2;
   for(int i = n; i >= 1; i--)
     {
      if(b == g_emaBar && i > 1) continue;
      ObjSegment(AK_OBJ + "EMA20_" + IntegerToString(i), iTime(_Symbol, tf, i), EMAv(tf, 20, i), iTime(_Symbol, tf, i - 1), EMAv(tf, 20, i - 1), C'40,140,255', 1, STYLE_SOLID);
      ObjSegment(AK_OBJ + "EMA50_" + IntegerToString(i), iTime(_Symbol, tf, i), EMAv(tf, 50, i), iTime(_Symbol, tf, i - 1), EMAv(tf, 50, i - 1), C'255,150,40', 1, STYLE_SOLID);
     }
   g_emaBar = b;
   RightLabel(AK_OBJ + "EMAL", EMAv(tf, 20, 0), "EMA20", C'40,140,255');
   RightLabel(AK_OBJ + "EMAL2", EMAv(tf, 50, 0), "EMA50", C'255,150,40');
  }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// LEVELS: yesterday's high / low (today only) and the H1 20-candle high / low (the breakout levels)
//------------------------------------------------------------------
void DrawLevels()
  {
   if(!DrawLevelsH1) return;
   datetime d0 = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_D1, 0);
   double ph = Hi(PERIOD_D1, 1), pl = Lo(PERIOD_D1, 1);
   if(ph > 0 && pl > 0)
     {
      ObjSegment(AK_OBJ + "LPDH", d0, ph, RightTime(2), ph, C'120,130,150', 1, STYLE_DOT);
      ObjSegment(AK_OBJ + "LPDL", d0, pl, RightTime(2), pl, C'120,130,150', 1, STYLE_DOT);
      RightLabel(AK_OBJ + "LPDHT", ph, "Yesterday high", C'150,160,180');
      RightLabel(AK_OBJ + "LPDLT", pl, "Yesterday low", C'150,160,180');
     }
   int kh = iHighest(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, MODE_HIGH, 20, 1), kl = iLowest(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, MODE_LOW, 20, 1);
   if(kh < 0 || kl < 0) return;
   datetime t1 = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, 20);
   double hi = Hi(PERIOD_H1, kh), lo = Lo(PERIOD_H1, kl);
   ObjSegment(AK_OBJ + "LVH", t1, hi, RightTime(2), hi, C'60,170,110', 1, STYLE_DASH);
   ObjSegment(AK_OBJ + "LVL", t1, lo, RightTime(2), lo, C'200,90,90', 1, STYLE_DASH);
   RightLabel(AK_OBJ + "LVHT", hi, "1H breakout " + Px(hi), C'60,170,110');
   RightLabel(AK_OBJ + "LVLT", lo, "1H breakdown " + Px(lo), C'200,90,90');
  }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// SIGNALS: taken = blue / red arrow with a short tag; skipped = small grey dot (reason in the tooltip). The last 15 stay.
//------------------------------------------------------------------
void MarkSignal(int m, int d, int tf, string setup, bool opened, string why)
  {
   if(!DrawSignals || !DrawOK()) return;
   int ctf = Period();                                                 // drawn on the chart candle where the signal came
   datetime t = iTime(_Symbol, ctf, 0);
   double a = ATR(ctf, 1);
   if(a <= 0) a = ATR(tf, 1);
   double ext = (d > 0) ? MathMin(Lo(ctf, 0), Lo(ctf, 1)) : MathMax(Hi(ctf, 0), Hi(ctf, 1));
   double p = ext - d * 0.6 * a;
   string id = AK_OBJ + "SG" + IntegerToString(g_sgN);
   if(opened)
     {
      ObjArrow(id + "A", t, p, (d > 0) ? 233 : 234, (d > 0) ? C'40,140,255' : C'240,90,90', 2, SideName(d) + " " + setup);
      double tp = p - d * 1.1 * a;                                       // text centre (all small texts are centred, so they compare fairly)
      ObjText(id + "T", t, tp, SideName(d) + " " + ChartSetupWord(m), (d > 0) ? C'40,140,255' : C'240,90,90', 7, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
      SmallTextAdd(id + "T", tp, -d);
      SmallTextsLayout();
     }
   else
     {
      //--- skipped: one small grey dot per side every 3 candles is enough (reason in the tooltip and on the panel)
      int sd = (d > 0) ? 0 : 1;
      if(t - g_skipT[sd] < 3 * ctf * 60) return;
      g_skipT[sd] = t;
      ObjArrow(id + "A", t, p, 159, C'110,115,130', 1, SideName(d) + " " + setup + " skipped: " + why);
     }
   int old = g_sgN - 15;
   if(old >= 0) { ObjectDelete(0, AK_OBJ + "SG" + IntegerToString(old) + "A"); ObjectDelete(0, AK_OBJ + "SG" + IntegerToString(old) + "T"); }
   g_sgN++;
  }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// POSITIONS: red box = risk, green box = target zone, labels on the right in free rows
//------------------------------------------------------------------
//--- the target zone of a trade box: first target (partial) / quick or memory target / full target / +2R lock
double BoxTarget(long key, int dir, double en, double R)
  {
   int exm = (int)PGet(key, "EXP", EX_TREND), xs = (int)PGet(key, "XS", 0);
   if(exm == EX_QUICK) return en + dir * QuickTargetR * R;
   if(exm == EX_MEM) return PGet(key, "TP", en + dir * 2.0 * R);
   if(xs == 2) return en + dir * PGet(key, "XT", 3.0) * R;
   if(xs == 1) return en + dir * 2.0 * R;
   return en + dir * PartialAtR * R;
  }

void DrawPositionBoxes()
  {
   DeleteObjects(AK_OBJ + "PX");
   if(!DrawPositions) return;
   datetime right = RightTime(10);
   //--- the newest trade gets the filled red / green boxes; older ones get dotted lines (overlapping filled boxes mix colours in MT4)
   datetime newest = 0;
   for(int n = OrdersTotal() - 1; n >= 0; n--)
     {
      if(!OrderSelect(n, SELECT_BY_POS, MODE_TRADES)) continue;
      if(MyOrder() && OrderOpenTime() > newest) newest = OrderOpenTime();
     }
   for(int i = OrdersTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
     {
      if(!OrderSelect(i, SELECT_BY_POS, MODE_TRADES)) continue;
      if(!MyOrder()) continue;
      long key = OrderKey();
      int dir = (OrderType() == OP_BUY) ? 1 : -1;
      datetime t0 = OrderOpenTime();
      double en = OrderOpenPrice(), R = PGet(key, "R", 0), sl0 = PGet(key, "SL0", OrderStopLoss()), sl = OrderStopLoss();
      if(R <= 0) continue;
      int exm = (int)PGet(key, "EXP", EX_TREND), xs = (int)PGet(key, "XS", 0);
      string id = AK_OBJ + "PX" + IntegerToString(key);
      double t1 = en + dir * PartialAtR * R;
      double tgt = BoxTarget(key, dir, en, R);
      double slz = (sl0 > 0) ? sl0 : en - dir * R;
      if(t0 == newest)
        {
         ObjBox(id + "R", t0, en, right, slz, C'80,28,34');
         ObjBox(id + "G", t0, en, right, tgt, C'22,70,44');
        }
      else
        {
         ObjSegment(id + "E", t0, en, right, en, C'150,155,170', 1, STYLE_DOT);
         ObjSegment(id + "Z", t0, slz, right, slz, C'200,80,80', 1, STYLE_DOT);
         ObjSegment(id + "T", t0, tgt, right, tgt, C'60,170,110', 1, STYLE_DOT);
        }
      if(sl > 0 && MathAbs(sl - sl0) > Point) ObjSegment(id + "S", t0, sl, right, sl, C'240,90,90', 1, STYLE_DOT);
      RightLabel(id + "L", en, SideName(dir) + " @ " + Px(en), clrWhite);
      RightLabel(id + "SLT", (sl > 0) ? sl : sl0, ((sl > 0 && dir * (sl - en) >= 0) ? "SL (risk-free) " : "SL ") + Px((sl > 0) ? sl : sl0), C'240,90,90');
      if(exm != EX_MEM && xs == 0) RightLabel(id + "FT", t1, "TP1 " + Px(t1) + ((PartialLots(PGet(key, "LOT0", OrderLots())) > 0) ? " (half)" : ""), C'60,200,120');
      else RightLabel(id + "FT", tgt, ((xs == 1) ? "+2R lock " : "TP ") + Px(tgt), C'60,200,120');
      if((int)PGet(key, "PRT", 0) == 1)
        {
         datetime tp = (datetime)PGet(key, "PRTT", 0);
         if(tp > 0) ObjArrow(id + "P", tp, PGet(key, "PRTPX", t1), 252, C'60,200,120', 2, "half booked");
        }
     }
  }

//--- a closed trade: thin box from entry to exit + the result (last 6 stay)
void DrawClosedBox(long key, int dir, double en, double ex, datetime ct, double resR)
  {
   if(!DrawPositions || !DrawOK()) return;
   string id = AK_OBJ + "CB" + IntegerToString(g_cbN);
   datetime t0 = KeyTime(key);
   if(ct <= t0) ct = t0 + 60;
   ObjBox(id + "R", t0, en, ct, ex, (resR > 0.05) ? C'45,150,90' : ((resR < -0.05) ? C'190,70,70' : C'110,115,130'));
   ObjectSetInteger(0, id + "R", OBJPROP_BACK, 0);                     // outline only (MT4 fills background rectangles)
   int away = (dir * (ex - en) >= 0) ? dir : -dir;                       // the result sits beyond the exit, away from the entry
   ObjText(id + "T", ct, ex, Rtx(resR) + " ", (resR >= 0) ? C'60,200,120' : C'240,90,90', 7, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
   SmallTextAdd(id + "T", ex, away);
   SmallTextsLayout();
   int old = g_cbN - 6;
   if(old >= 0) { ObjectDelete(0, AK_OBJ + "CB" + IntegerToString(old) + "R"); ObjectDelete(0, AK_OBJ + "CB" + IntegerToString(old) + "T"); }
   g_cbN++;
  }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// CANDLE PATTERNS (chart timeframe, last closed candle) - only at a meaningful place: a 10-candle extreme, the EMA20 or a level.
// Each label deletes itself after PatternLifeBars candles; at most 3 on the chart.
//------------------------------------------------------------------
string   g_ptName[3];
datetime g_ptExp[3];

void PatternAdd(string txt, int d, string tip)
  {
