   int tf = Period();
   int slot = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < 3; i++) if(g_ptName[i] == "") { slot = i; break; } else if(g_ptExp[i] < g_ptExp[slot]) slot = i;
   if(g_ptName[slot] != "") { ObjectDelete(0, g_ptName[slot]); ObjectDelete(0, g_ptName[slot] + "A"); }
   string nm = AK_OBJ + "PT" + IntegerToString((long)iTime(_Symbol, tf, 1)) + "_" + IntegerToString(slot);
   double a = ATR(tf, 1);
   double p = (d > 0) ? Lo(tf, 1) - 1.3 * a : Hi(tf, 1) + 1.3 * a;
   color c = (d > 0) ? C'60,200,120' : C'240,110,90';
   ObjText(nm, iTime(_Symbol, tf, 1), p, txt, c, 7, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
   SmallTextAdd(nm, p, -d);
   ObjectSetString(0, nm, OBJPROP_TOOLTIP, tip);
   ObjArrow(nm + "A", iTime(_Symbol, tf, 1), (d > 0) ? Lo(tf, 1) - 0.15 * a : Hi(tf, 1) + 0.15 * a, (d > 0) ? 241 : 242, c, 1, tip);
   g_ptName[slot] = nm;
   g_ptExp[slot] = iTime(_Symbol, tf, 0) + tf * 60 * PatternLifeBars;
  }

void PatternsExpire()
  {
   datetime now = iTime(_Symbol, Period(), 0);
   for(int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
     {
      if(g_ptName[i] == "" || g_ptExp[i] > now) continue;
      ObjectDelete(0, g_ptName[i]);
      ObjectDelete(0, g_ptName[i] + "A");
      g_ptName[i] = "";
     }
  }

void DrawCandlePatterns()
  {
   if(!DrawPatterns) return;
   int tf = Period();
   datetime b = iTime(_Symbol, tf, 0);
   PatternsExpire();
   if(b == g_ptBar || iBars(_Symbol, tf) < 60) return;
   g_ptBar = b;
   double o = Op(tf, 1), h = Hi(tf, 1), l = Lo(tf, 1), c = Cl(tf, 1), po = Op(tf, 2), pc = Cl(tf, 2), ph = Hi(tf, 2), pl = Lo(tf, 2);
   double a = ATR(tf, 1);
   if(a <= 0) return;
   double rng = h - l, body = MathAbs(c - o);
   if(rng <= 0) return;
   double e20 = EMAv(tf, 20, 1);
   int kl = iLowest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_LOW, 10, 1), kh = iHighest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_HIGH, 10, 1);
   bool atLow = (kl == 1 || kl == 2 || MathAbs(l - e20) <= 0.3 * a);
   bool atHigh = (kh == 1 || kh == 2 || MathAbs(h - e20) <= 0.3 * a);
   string where = " at a " + ((kl == 1 || kh == 1) ? "10-candle extreme" : "the EMA20");
   //--- engulfing
   if(c > o && pc < po && c >= po && o <= pc && body > 0.5 * a && atLow) { PatternAdd("Bullish engulfing", 1, "Bullish engulfing" + where); return; }
   if(c < o && pc > po && c <= po && o >= pc && body > 0.5 * a && atHigh) { PatternAdd("Bearish engulfing", -1, "Bearish engulfing" + where); return; }
   //--- hammer / shooting star (pin bars)
   double lw = MathMin(o, c) - l, uw = h - MathMax(o, c);
   if(lw >= 2 * body && lw >= 0.6 * rng && rng >= 0.8 * a && atLow) { PatternAdd("Hammer", 1, "Hammer (bullish pin bar)" + where); return; }
   if(uw >= 2 * body && uw >= 0.6 * rng && rng >= 0.8 * a && atHigh) { PatternAdd("Shooting star", -1, "Shooting star (bearish pin bar)" + where); return; }
   //--- morning / evening star (3 candles)
   double o3 = Op(tf, 3), c3 = Cl(tf, 3);
   if(c3 < o3 && MathAbs(c3 - o3) > 0.6 * a && MathAbs(pc - po) < 0.3 * a && c > o && c > (o3 + c3) / 2 && atLow) { PatternAdd("Morning star", 1, "Morning star - 3-candle bullish reversal" + where); return; }
   if(c3 > o3 && MathAbs(c3 - o3) > 0.6 * a && MathAbs(pc - po) < 0.3 * a && c < o && c < (o3 + c3) / 2 && atHigh) { PatternAdd("Evening star", -1, "Evening star - 3-candle bearish reversal" + where); return; }
   //--- inside bar in the trend direction (a pause before the next leg)
   int ts = TrendSide();
   if(ts != 0 && h < ph && l > pl && (ph - pl) >= 1.0 * a) PatternAdd("Inside bar", ts, "Inside bar - the market pauses; a break of the mother candle often starts the next move");
  }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// AUTO TRENDLINES: the rising line through the last two swing lows (support) and the falling line through the last two swing
// highs (resistance), chart timeframe, last 120 candles. Only lines that still hold are drawn; a break shows "trendline break"
// for a few candles and the line is removed.
//------------------------------------------------------------------
void DrawTrendlines()
  {
   if(!DrawTrendlinesOn) return;
   int tf = Period();
   datetime b = iTime(_Symbol, tf, 0);
   if(b == g_tlBar) return;
   g_tlBar = b;
   DeleteObjects(AK_OBJ + "TLN");
   int n = iBars(_Symbol, tf) - 5;
   if(n < 60) return;
   if(n > 120) n = 120;
   double a = ATR(tf, 1);
   if(a <= 0) return;
   //--- swing points (5-candle fractals, confirmed 2 candles later), newest first
   int lo1 = -1, lo2 = -1, hi1 = -1, hi2 = -1;
   for(int s = 3; s < n; s++)
     {
      double ls = Lo(tf, s), hs = Hi(tf, s);
      bool isL = (ls < Lo(tf, s - 1) && ls < Lo(tf, s - 2) && ls <= Lo(tf, s + 1) && ls <= Lo(tf, s + 2));
      bool isH = (hs > Hi(tf, s - 1) && hs > Hi(tf, s - 2) && hs >= Hi(tf, s + 1) && hs >= Hi(tf, s + 2));
      if(isL) { if(lo1 < 0) lo1 = s; else if(lo2 < 0 && s - lo1 >= 5 && ls < Lo(tf, lo1)) lo2 = s; }
      if(isH) { if(hi1 < 0) hi1 = s; else if(hi2 < 0 && s - hi1 >= 5 && hs > Hi(tf, hi1)) hi2 = s; }
     }
   //--- support (rising): line from lo2 to lo1, extended; still valid if no close below it since lo1 (by 0.3 ATR)
   if(lo1 > 0 && lo2 > 0)
     {
      double slope = (Lo(tf, lo1) - Lo(tf, lo2)) / (double)(lo2 - lo1);
      bool ok = true;
      for(int s2 = lo1 - 1; s2 >= 1; s2--) if(Cl(tf, s2) < Lo(tf, lo1) + slope * (lo1 - s2) - 0.3 * a) { ok = false; break; }
      double now = Lo(tf, lo1) + slope * lo1;
      if(ok && MathAbs(Cl(tf, 1) - now) <= 4 * a)
        {
         ObjSegment(AK_OBJ + "TLNS", iTime(_Symbol, tf, lo2), Lo(tf, lo2), RightTime(5), now + slope * 5, C'60,170,110', 1, STYLE_SOLID);
         ObjectSetString(0, AK_OBJ + "TLNS", OBJPROP_TOOLTIP, "Rising trendline (support)");
        }
      else if(!ok && Cl(tf, 1) < Lo(tf, lo1) + slope * (lo1 - 1) - 0.3 * a && Cl(tf, 2) >= Lo(tf, lo1) + slope * (lo1 - 2) - 0.3 * a)
         PatternAdd("Trendline break", -1, "Price closed below the rising trendline");
     }
   //--- resistance (falling)
   if(hi1 > 0 && hi2 > 0)
     {
      double slope2 = (Hi(tf, hi1) - Hi(tf, hi2)) / (double)(hi2 - hi1);
      bool ok2 = true;
      for(int s3 = hi1 - 1; s3 >= 1; s3--) if(Cl(tf, s3) > Hi(tf, hi1) + slope2 * (hi1 - s3) + 0.3 * a) { ok2 = false; break; }
      double now2 = Hi(tf, hi1) + slope2 * hi1;
      if(ok2 && MathAbs(Cl(tf, 1) - now2) <= 4 * a)
        {
         ObjSegment(AK_OBJ + "TLNR", iTime(_Symbol, tf, hi2), Hi(tf, hi2), RightTime(5), now2 + slope2 * 5, C'210,90,90', 1, STYLE_SOLID);
         ObjectSetString(0, AK_OBJ + "TLNR", OBJPROP_TOOLTIP, "Falling trendline (resistance)");
        }
      else if(!ok2 && Cl(tf, 1) > Hi(tf, hi1) + slope2 * (hi1 - 1) + 0.3 * a && Cl(tf, 2) <= Hi(tf, hi1) + slope2 * (hi1 - 2) + 0.3 * a)
         PatternAdd("Trendline break", 1, "Price closed above the falling trendline");
     }
  }

//------------------------------------------------------------------
// H4 W (double bottom) / M (double top) with its neckline - only while it is recent (same rules as the memory analysis)
//------------------------------------------------------------------
void DrawWMPattern()
  {
   if(!DrawPatterns) return;
   datetime b = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, 0);
   if(b == g_wmBar) return;
   g_wmBar = b;
   DeleteObjects(AK_OBJ + "WM");
   int tf = PERIOD_H4;
   int bars = iBars(_Symbol, tf) - 2;
   if(bars < 80) return;
   int n = (bars > MPB) ? MPB : bars;
   double h[], l[], c[], atr[], tr[];
   ArrayResize(h, n);
   ArrayResize(l, n);
   ArrayResize(c, n);
   ArrayResize(atr, n);
   ArrayResize(tr, n);
   for(int i = 0; i < n; i++)
     {
      int sh = n - i;
      h[i] = iHigh(_Symbol, tf, sh);
      l[i] = iLow(_Symbol, tf, sh);
      c[i] = iClose(_Symbol, tf, sh);
     }
   double s = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < n; i++)
     {
      tr[i] = (i == 0) ? h[0] - l[0] : MathMax(h[i], c[i - 1]) - MathMin(l[i], c[i - 1]);
      s += tr[i];
      if(i >= 14) s -= tr[i - 14];
      atr[i] = (i >= 13) ? s / 14.0 : 0.0;
     }
   int lo1 = -1, lo2 = -1, hi1 = -1, hi2 = -1, lastE = -1, lastS = 0, pA = -1, pB = -1;
   double lastNeck = 0;
   for(int j = 4; j < n; j++)
     {
      int k = j - 2;
      if(l[k] < l[k - 1] && l[k] < l[k - 2] && l[k] <= l[k + 1] && l[k] <= l[k + 2]) { lo1 = lo2; lo2 = k; }
      if(h[k] > h[k - 1] && h[k] > h[k - 2] && h[k] >= h[k + 1] && h[k] >= h[k + 2]) { hi1 = hi2; hi2 = k; }
      double a = atr[j];
      if(!(a > 0)) continue;
      if(lo1 >= 0 && lo2 - lo1 >= 5 && lo2 - lo1 <= 60 && MathAbs(l[lo2] - l[lo1]) <= 0.3 * a)
        {
         double neck = h[lo1];
         for(int q = lo1; q <= lo2; q++) if(h[q] > neck) neck = h[q];
         if(neck - MathMax(l[lo1], l[lo2]) >= a && c[j] > neck && c[j - 1] <= neck && j - lo2 <= 30) { lastE = j; lastS = 1; pA = lo1; pB = lo2; lastNeck = neck; }
        }
      if(hi1 >= 0 && hi2 - hi1 >= 5 && hi2 - hi1 <= 60 && MathAbs(h[hi2] - h[hi1]) <= 0.3 * a)
        {
         double neck2 = l[hi1];
         for(int q = hi1; q <= hi2; q++) if(l[q] < neck2) neck2 = l[q];
         if(MathMin(h[hi1], h[hi2]) - neck2 >= a && c[j] < neck2 && c[j - 1] >= neck2 && j - hi2 <= 30) { lastE = j; lastS = -1; pA = hi1; pB = hi2; lastNeck = neck2; }
        }
     }
   if(lastE < 0 || (n - 1) - lastE > 30) return;                         // only a recent one
   datetime ta = iTime(_Symbol, tf, n - pA), tb = iTime(_Symbol, tf, n - pB), te = iTime(_Symbol, tf, n - lastE);
   double va = (lastS > 0) ? l[pA] : h[pA], vb = (lastS > 0) ? l[pB] : h[pB];
   color cc = (lastS > 0) ? C'60,170,110' : C'210,90,90';
   ObjSegment(AK_OBJ + "WM1", ta, va, tb, vb, cc, 2, STYLE_SOLID);
   ObjSegment(AK_OBJ + "WM2", ta, lastNeck, te + 4 * 3600, lastNeck, cc, 1, STYLE_DASH);
   ObjText(AK_OBJ + "WM3", te, lastNeck, (lastS > 0) ? " 4H double bottom" : " 4H double top", cc, 7, (lastS > 0) ? ANCHOR_LEFT_LOWER : ANCHOR_LEFT_UPPER);
  }

//--- Bollinger bands (20, 2) and Supertrend (14, x3) of the chart timeframe (both OFF by default - cleaner chart)
datetime g_bbBar = 0;
void DrawBandsST()
  {
   if(!DrawBands && !DrawSupertrend) return;
   int tf = Period();
   datetime b = iTime(_Symbol, tf, 0);
   if(b == g_bbBar) return;
   g_bbBar = b;
   int n = (DrawEMABars < 20) ? 20 : DrawEMABars;
   if(n > iBars(_Symbol, tf) - 30) n = iBars(_Symbol, tf) - 30;
   if(DrawBands)
     {
      for(int i = n; i >= 2; i--)
        {
         double m1 = 0, m2 = 0;
         double s1 = TxSd(tf, i, m1), s2 = TxSd(tf, i - 1, m2);
         datetime t1 = iTime(_Symbol, tf, i), t2 = iTime(_Symbol, tf, i - 1);
         ObjSegment(AK_OBJ + "BBU" + IntegerToString(i), t1, m1 + 2 * s1, t2, m2 + 2 * s2, C'95,95,130', 1, STYLE_DOT);
         ObjSegment(AK_OBJ + "BBL" + IntegerToString(i), t1, m1 - 2 * s1, t2, m2 - 2 * s2, C'95,95,130', 1, STYLE_DOT);
        }
     }
   if(DrawSupertrend)
     {
      int nb = iBars(_Symbol, tf) - 2;
      if(nb > 400 + n) nb = 400 + n;
      if(nb < 80) return;
      double up = 0, dn = 0, prevLine = 0;
      int dir = 1;
      datetime prevT = 0;
      for(int s = nb; s >= 1; s--)
        {
         double a = ATR(tf, s);
         double m = (Hi(tf, s) + Lo(tf, s)) / 2.0, u = m - 3.0 * a, dd = m + 3.0 * a;
         if(s == nb) { up = u; dn = dd; continue; }
         double cp = Cl(tf, s + 1), c = Cl(tf, s);
         double upPrev = up, dnPrev = dn;
         up = (cp > upPrev) ? MathMax(u, upPrev) : u;
         dn = (cp < dnPrev) ? MathMin(dd, dnPrev) : dd;
         if(dir < 0 && c > dnPrev) dir = 1;
         else if(dir > 0 && c < upPrev) dir = -1;
         double line = (dir > 0) ? up : dn;
         datetime t = iTime(_Symbol, tf, s);
         if(s <= n && prevT > 0) ObjSegment(AK_OBJ + "STL" + IntegerToString(s), prevT, prevLine, t, line, (dir > 0) ? C'60,170,110' : C'210,90,90', 1, STYLE_SOLID);
         prevLine = line;
         prevT = t;
        }
     }
  }

//--- everything above (called with the panel, once per second); the right-hand labels are placed fresh every time
void DrawChart()
  {
   if(!DrawOK()) return;
   LabelsReset();
   DrawPositionBoxes();                  // trade labels get the best rows first
   DrawLevels();
   DrawEmaLines();
   DrawBandsST();
   DrawCandlePatterns();
   DrawTrendlines();
   DrawWMPattern();
   SmallTextsLayout();                   // signal tags / patterns / results spread out at the current scale
  }


//==================================================================
// METHOD 4 - TREND H4 / H1: the entries that stayed positive in BOTH halves of the 5-year study (2021-24 and 2024-26).
// Each symbol uses the entries that worked on it (TrendTestedSets = true); each entry also has its own ON / OFF switch.
// Own magic (MagicBase + 3000), own limit (MaxOpenTrend), own risk budget (TrendRiskPct). Exit as in the study: half at 1R
// (when the lot can be split), stop to entry, rest trails 2R behind the best price from 3R, closed after 96 candles at the latest.
//==================================================================


string TxName(int code)
  {
   switch(code)
     {
      case 1: return "Heikin-Ashi colour change H4";
      case 2: return "EMA50 deep pullback H4";
      case 3: return "EMA20 pullback H4";
      case 4: return "MACD signal cross H4";
      case 5: return "Momentum candle H4";
      case 6: return "Supertrend flip H1";
      case 7: return "Bollinger squeeze breakout H1";
      case 8: return "Supertrend flip H4";
      case 9: return "EMA20/50 cross H4";
      case 10: return "MACD signal cross H1";
      case 11: return "55-candle breakout H1";
      case 12: return "MACD zero cross H4";
      case 13: return "RSI cross 50 H4";
      case 14: return "Pin bar in trend H4";
      case 15: return "EMA20 pullback + ADX H4";
      case 16: return "Ichimoku TK cross H4";
      case 17: return "Inside-day breakout H4";
      case 18: return "RANGE failed-breakout fade H4";
      case 19: return "RANGE Bollinger re-entry H4";
      case 20: return "RANGE Bollinger re-entry H1";
     }
   return "trend entry";
  }

int TxTf(int code) { return (code == 6 || code == 7 || code == 10 || code == 11 || code == 20) ? PERIOD_H1 : PERIOD_H4; }

bool TxSwitch(int code)
  {
   switch(code)
     {
      case 1: return TX_HeikinAshiH4;
      case 2: return TX_EMA50PullbackH4;
      case 3: return TX_EMA20PullbackH4;
      case 4: return TX_MACDCrossH4;
      case 5: return TX_MomentumH4;
      case 6: return TX_SupertrendH1;
      case 7: return TX_BollingerSqueezeH1;
      case 8: return TX_SupertrendH4;
      case 9: return TX_EMACrossH4;
      case 10: return TX_MACDCrossH1;
      case 11: return TX_Breakout55H1;
      case 12: return TX_MACDZeroH4;
      case 13: return TX_RSI50H4;
      case 14: return TX_PinBarH4;
      case 15: return TX_EMA20ADXH4;
      case 16: return TX_IchimokuH4;
      case 17: return TX_InsideDayH4;
      case 18: return UseRangeEntries && TX_RangeFadeH4;
      case 19: return UseRangeEntries && TX_RangeBandH4;
      case 20: return UseRangeEntries && TX_RangeBandH1;
     }
   return false;
  }

//--- is the entry active on this symbol, and does it need the D1 + H4 trend to agree? (as tested)
bool CodeInList(int code, string lst)
  {
   if(lst == "") return false;
   string parts[];
   int n = StringSplit(lst, ',', parts);
   for(int i = 0; i < n; i++) if(StringToInteger(TrimStr(parts[i])) == code) return true;
   return false;
  }

string TxAddList() { return (g_cls == SC_GOLD) ? TrendAddGold : ((g_cls == SC_BTC) ? TrendAddBTC : ((g_cls == SC_ETH) ? TrendAddETH : ((g_cls == SC_OIL) ? TrendAddOil : ""))); }
string TxDropList() { return (g_cls == SC_GOLD) ? TrendDropGold : ((g_cls == SC_BTC) ? TrendDropBTC : ((g_cls == SC_ETH) ? TrendDropETH : ((g_cls == SC_OIL) ? TrendDropOil : ""))); }

bool TxActive(int code, bool &needTrend)
  {
   needTrend = true;
   if(!TxSwitch(code) || !TrendSymbolOn()) return false;
   if(CodeInList(code, TxDropList())) return false;
   if(CodeInList(code, TxAddList())) return true;
   if(!TrendTestedSets) return true;
   if(g_cls == SC_GOLD)
     {
      if(code == 20) { needTrend = false; return true; }
      return (code >= 1 && code <= 7);
     }
   if(g_cls == SC_BTC)
     {
      if(code == 2 || code == 9 || code == 5) { needTrend = false; return true; }
      return (code == 8 || code == 10 || code == 11);
     }
   if(g_cls == SC_ETH)
     {
      if(code == 18 || code == 19) { needTrend = false; return true; }
      return (code == 12 || code == 9 || code == 13);
     }
   if(g_cls == SC_OIL)
     {
      if(code == 3) { needTrend = false; return true; }
      return (code == 14 || code == 15);
     }
   if(g_cls == SC_FX && IsEURUSD()) return (code == 16 || code == 17);
   return false;
  }

string TxActiveList()
  {
   string s = "";
   bool nt = true;
   for(int c = 1; c <= TX_N; c++) if(TxActive(c, nt)) s += ((s == "") ? "" : ", ") + TxName(c) + (nt ? "" : " (no trend filter)");
   if(!TrendSymbolOn()) return "OFF for this symbol (TrendOn" + ClassName(g_cls) + " = false)";
   return (s == "") ? "none for this symbol (TrendTestedSets = false uses all 13)" : s;
  }

bool TrendSymbolOn()
  {
   if(g_cls == SC_GOLD) return TrendOnGold;
   if(g_cls == SC_BTC) return TrendOnBTC;
   if(g_cls == SC_ETH) return TrendOnETH;
   if(g_cls == SC_OIL) return TrendOnOil;
   return TrendOnEUR;
  }

bool UpTr(int tf, int sh) { return EMAv(tf, 20, sh) > EMAv(tf, 50, sh); }
bool DnTr(int tf, int sh) { return EMAv(tf, 20, sh) < EMAv(tf, 50, sh); }

//--- Heikin-Ashi colour change on the closed candle, in the EMA20 / EMA50 direction
int TxHA(int tf)
  {
   int n = iBars(_Symbol, tf) - 2;
   if(n < 60) return 0;
   if(n > 300) n = 300;
   double hao = (Op(tf, n) + Cl(tf, n)) / 2.0, hacPrev = (Op(tf, n) + Hi(tf, n) + Lo(tf, n) + Cl(tf, n)) / 4.0;
   double ho2 = 0, hc2 = 0, ho1 = 0, hc1 = 0;
   for(int s = n - 1; s >= 1; s--)
     {
      hao = (hao + hacPrev) / 2.0;
      double hac = (Op(tf, s) + Hi(tf, s) + Lo(tf, s) + Cl(tf, s)) / 4.0;
      if(s == 2) { ho2 = hao; hc2 = hac; }
      if(s == 1) { ho1 = hao; hc1 = hac; }
      hacPrev = hac;
     }
   if(UpTr(tf, 1) && hc1 > ho1 && hc2 <= ho2) return 1;
   if(DnTr(tf, 1) && hc1 < ho1 && hc2 >= ho2) return -1;
   return 0;
  }

int TxMacdSig(int tf)
  {
   double m1 = iMACD(_Symbol, tf, 12, 26, 9, PRICE_CLOSE, MODE_MAIN, 1), s1 = iMACD(_Symbol, tf, 12, 26, 9, PRICE_CLOSE, MODE_SIGNAL, 1);
   double m2 = iMACD(_Symbol, tf, 12, 26, 9, PRICE_CLOSE, MODE_MAIN, 2), s2 = iMACD(_Symbol, tf, 12, 26, 9, PRICE_CLOSE, MODE_SIGNAL, 2);
   if(UpTr(tf, 1) && m1 > s1 && m2 <= s2) return 1;
   if(DnTr(tf, 1) && m1 < s1 && m2 >= s2) return -1;
   return 0;
  }

int TxMacdZero(int tf)
  {
   double m1 = iMACD(_Symbol, tf, 12, 26, 9, PRICE_CLOSE, MODE_MAIN, 1), m2 = iMACD(_Symbol, tf, 12, 26, 9, PRICE_CLOSE, MODE_MAIN, 2);
   if(m1 > 0 && m2 <= 0) return 1;
   if(m1 < 0 && m2 >= 0) return -1;
   return 0;
  }

//--- Supertrend (ATR 14, x3): +1 / -1 direction at shift 1 and 2
void TxSTDir(int tf, int &d1, int &d2)
  {
   d1 = 0;
   d2 = 0;
   int n = iBars(_Symbol, tf) - 2;
   if(n < 80) return;
   if(n > 400) n = 400;
   double up = 0, dn = 0;
   int dir = 1;
   for(int s = n; s >= 1; s--)
     {
      double a = ATR(tf, s);
      double m = (Hi(tf, s) + Lo(tf, s)) / 2.0, u = m - 3.0 * a, d = m + 3.0 * a;
      if(s == n) { up = u; dn = d; continue; }
      double cp = Cl(tf, s + 1), c = Cl(tf, s);
      double upPrev = up, dnPrev = dn;
      up = (cp > upPrev) ? MathMax(u, upPrev) : u;
      dn = (cp < dnPrev) ? MathMin(d, dnPrev) : d;
      if(dir < 0 && c > dnPrev) dir = 1;
      else if(dir > 0 && c < upPrev) dir = -1;
      if(s == 2) d2 = dir;
      if(s == 1) d1 = dir;
     }
  }

int TxST(int tf)
  {
   int a = 0, b = 0;
   TxSTDir(tf, a, b);
   if(a > 0 && b < 0) return 1;
   if(a < 0 && b > 0) return -1;
   return 0;
  }

double TxSd(int tf, int sh, double &ma)
  {
   double s = 0, s2 = 0;
   for(int i = sh; i < sh + 20; i++) { double c = Cl(tf, i); s += c; s2 += c * c; }
   ma = s / 20.0;
   double v = (s2 - 20.0 * ma * ma) / 19.0;
   return (v > 0) ? MathSqrt(v) : 0;
  }

//--- Bollinger squeeze breakout: band width at the candle before is in the lowest 20% of the last 120, close outside the band
int TxBB(int tf)
  {
   if(iBars(_Symbol, tf) < 200) return 0;
   double w[];
   ArrayResize(w, 120);
   for(int k = 0; k < 120; k++)
     {
      double mk = 0;
      double sk = TxSd(tf, 2 + k, mk);
      w[k] = (mk > 0) ? 4.0 * sk / mk : 0;
     }
   double cur = w[0];
   ArraySort(w);
   double pos = 0.2 * 119.0;
   int lo = (int)MathFloor(pos);
   double q = w[lo] + (pos - lo) * (w[lo + 1] - w[lo]);
   if(cur > q) return 0;
   double ma = 0;
   double sd = TxSd(tf, 1, ma);
   double c = Cl(tf, 1);
   if(c > ma + 2 * sd) return 1;
   if(c < ma - 2 * sd) return -1;
   return 0;
  }

int TxCross(int tf)
  {
   if(UpTr(tf, 1) && !UpTr(tf, 2)) return 1;
   if(DnTr(tf, 1) && !DnTr(tf, 2)) return -1;
   return 0;
  }

int TxRSI50(int tf)
  {
   double r1 = iRSI(_Symbol, tf, 14, PRICE_CLOSE, 1), r2 = iRSI(_Symbol, tf, 14, PRICE_CLOSE, 2);
   if(r1 > 50 && r2 <= 50) return 1;
   if(r1 < 50 && r2 >= 50) return -1;
   return 0;
  }

int TxT55(int tf)
  {
   if(iBars(_Symbol, tf) < 120) return 0;
   int kh = iHighest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_HIGH, 55, 2), kl = iLowest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_LOW, 55, 2);
   int kh2 = iHighest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_HIGH, 55, 3), kl2 = iLowest(_Symbol, tf, MODE_LOW, 55, 3);
   if(kh < 0 || kl < 0 || kh2 < 0 || kl2 < 0) return 0;
   double c1 = Cl(tf, 1), c2 = Cl(tf, 2);
   if(c1 > Hi(tf, kh) && c2 <= Hi(tf, kh2)) return 1;
   if(c1 < Lo(tf, kl) && c2 >= Lo(tf, kl2)) return -1;
   return 0;
  }

int TxSignal(int code)
  {
   int tf = TxTf(code);
   if(iBars(_Symbol, tf) < 120) return 0;
   switch(code)
     {
      case 1: return TxHA(tf);
      case 2: return SigPullback(tf, 50);
      case 3: return SigPullback(tf, 20);
      case 4: return TxMacdSig(tf);
      case 5: return SigMomentum(tf);
      case 6: return TxST(tf);
      case 7: return TxBB(tf);
      case 8: return TxST(tf);
      case 9: return TxCross(tf);
      case 10: return TxMacdSig(tf);
      case 11: return TxT55(tf);
      case 12: return TxMacdZero(tf);
      case 13: return TxRSI50(tf);
      case 14: return TxPinBar(tf);
      case 15: return (iADX(_Symbol, tf, 14, PRICE_CLOSE, MODE_MAIN, 1) > 25) ? SigPullback(tf, 20) : 0;
      case 16: return TxIchimoku(tf);
      case 17: return TxInsideDay(tf);
      case 18: return RangeMarket() ? TxFailFade(tf) : 0;
      case 19: case 20: return RangeMarket() ? TxBandBack(tf) : 0;
     }
   return 0;
  }

//--- RANGE MARKET: the H4 ADX (Wilder, 14) of the last closed H4 candle is below the symbol limit
double TxAdxW(int tf, int sh)
  {
   int n = iBars(_Symbol, tf) - 2;
   int st = MathMin(n, sh + 300);
   if(st < sh + 40) return 100;
